📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 63,420 名患者中有 97,279 名 ARI 入院。年龄≥ 50 岁和≥ 75 岁的成年人中,RSV 相关 ARI 入院率估计范围分别为 0.70/1,000 人年 (PY) 至 1.92/1,000 PY。
  • 结果表明,RBA和TMC通过氢键和π-π堆积相互作用形成RTSN。RTSN 表现出 抗病毒药物 功效,选择性指数为 43.8,有效浓度 (EC50) 为 10.9 μM。 [10.1007/s12247-026-11114-8]。
  • 通过核磁共振 (NMR) 光谱和液相色谱-质谱 (LC-MS) 进行的分析研究揭示了两类化合物的筛选样品和重新合成的化合物之间的差异。 [10.1002/cmdc.70501]。
  • 系统发育分析表明,WGS定义的谱系命名法定义的主要谱系可以使用HVR2-G区域进行分类,自举率超过70%;无法区分嵌套的谱系。HRSVA 和 B 的 19 个和 37 个先前 HVR2-G 基因型分别减少为 4 个(A.1、A.2、A.3 和 A.D)和 5 个(B.1、B.2、B.3、B.4 和 B.D)谱系,P 距离阈值分别大于 0.102 和 0.0591。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • There were 97,279 ARI admissions in 63,420 patients. Estimated RSV-associated ARI hospital admission rates ranged from 0.70/1,000 person-years (PY) to 1.92/1,000 PY in adults aged ≥ 50 and ≥ 75, respectively.
  • The results showed that RBA and TMC formed RTSN through hydrogen bonding and π-π stacking interactions. RTSN exhibited antiviral efficacy with a selectivity index of 43.8 and an effective concentration (EC50) of 10.9 μM.
  • Analytical investigations by nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectroscopy and liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS) revealed discrepancies between the screening samples and the resynthesized compounds for two compound classes.
  • Phylogenetic analyses revealed that the primary lineages defined by WGS-defined lineage nomenclature could be classified using the HVR2-G region with a bootstrap over 70%; the nested lineages could not be distinguished.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Public health research & practice  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.1071/pu25136  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 4, PU25136

2019-2023 年悉尼儿童医院网络收治的幼儿的增强型 呼吸道合胞病毒 监测。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.
作者： Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在全面描述被送往悉尼儿童医院网络的呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)疾病患儿的临床特征、管理及预后,确定严重疾病的总体负担,并识别特定人群进入重症监护病房(ICU)的风险因素。
设计与对象
该研究是一项多中心、回顾性 队列研究 研究,在澳大利亚新南威尔士州大都会的两家三级儿科医院进行了 5 年多的时间,涉及 4414 名因实验室确诊 RSV 住院的 2 岁以下儿童,其中 11.3% 入住 ICU。
核心方法
这项研究是在澳大利亚新南威尔士州大都市的两家三级儿科医院进行的,当时该医院正在实施政府资助的儿童 RSV 免疫计划。主要结果指标是 2 岁以下儿童 RSV 住院和 ICU 入院的特征和 发病率 率。使用多元逻辑回归确定了与 ICU 入院几率较高相关的因素。
主要结果
5 年期间,4414 名儿童因实验室确诊的 RSV 住院,其中 11.3% (499/4414) 入住 ICU。与非 ICU 样本 (n = 1000;3.3 个月 vs 7.1 个月,P < 0.0001) 相比,ICU 队列研究 中的中位年龄 (n = 499) 更年轻。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,RSV 免疫计划预计将大大减轻澳大利亚儿童第一年的住院负担。他们还发现,土著儿童、早产儿童或患有心脏病的儿童患严重疾病的风险最大。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为土著儿童、早产儿和患有心脏病的儿童制定有针对性的预防计划提供信息,并指导澳大利亚 RSV 免疫计划的实施。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在政府资助的免疫计划实施之前进行的,这可能会限制其普遍性。证据有力,基于全面的数据收集和统计分析进行了高置信度评估。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.

Authors: Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: In this study, we aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network (SCHN), determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission. STUDY TYPE: Multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years. METHODS: The study was conducted at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, before implementation of government-funded immunisation programs against RSV in children. Participants were children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV from 1 January 2019 to 31 December 2023, comprising all ICU admitted, and a random sample of non-ICU admitted patients. The main outcome measures were characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions among children aged <2 years. RESULTS: Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001). Factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission in multivariate logistic regression were decreasing month of age (OR 1.10, 95% CI 1.06-1.11), prematurity (OR 2.17, 95% CI 1.61-2.91), Indigenous identity (OR 4.10, 95% CI 2.30-7.56) and cardiac disease (OR 3.39, 95% CI 2.06-5.70). The average hospitalisation incidence rate was 1615 per 100,000 (95% CI 1565-1666), with higher rates among children aged <1 year, 2141 per 100,000 (95% CI 2061-2224) compared with those aged 1-2 years, 1088 per 100,000 (95% CI 1030-1148). CONCLUSIONS: RSV immunisation programs are expected to substantially reduce the burden of hospitalisation among Australian children in their first year of life. Children who are Indigenous, were born prematurely or have heart disease are at greatest risk of severe disease and may benefit from extended preventative programs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission.
Design and population
The study was a multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, involving 4414 children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% were admitted to ICU.
Core methods
The study was conducted at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, before implementation of government-funded immunisation programs against RSV in children. The main outcome measures were characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions among children aged <2 years. Factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission were identified using multivariate logistic regression.
Main results
Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that RSV immunisation programs are expected to substantially reduce the burden of hospitalisation among Australian children in their first year of life. They also identified that Indigenous children, those born prematurely or with cardiac disease are at greatest risk of severe disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted preventative programs for Indigenous children, those born prematurely and children with cardiac disease, as well as guide the implementation of RSV immunisation programs in Australia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted before the implementation of government-funded immunisation programs, which may limit its generalisability. The evidence is strong, with a high confidence assessment based on the comprehensive data collection and statistical analysis.

急诊室中除 呼吸道合胞病毒 之外的下呼吸道感染的增加:RISE 研究季节(2024/25)的结果。

Rise of Lower Respiratory Tract Infections Other than Respiratory Syncytial Virus in the Emergency Room: Results from the Season (2024/25) of the RISE Study.
作者： Maria Adams, Vivien Greese, Anissa Paschereit, Tamina Schenck, Julia Kristin Singer, Johannes Paul Paulick, Rafael Paolo Pricoco, Hermann Joseph Girschick, Eva Jenz, Martina Rabe
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 是儿科急诊 (ED) 就诊和住院的主要原因。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性 监测 研究量化了 ARI 相关的急诊就诊次数,描述了病原体特异性 流行病学,并评估了 2024 年 11 月 1 日至 2025 年 5 月 31 日期间柏林两个儿科急诊中 60 个月以下儿童的就诊率和住院率以及下呼吸道感染与上呼吸道感染 (LRTI/URTI) 的分布。
核心方法
使用通过多重实时 RT-PCR (QIAstat-Dx® Respiratory Panel Plus) 测试的鼻咽拭子样本来鉴定呼吸道病原体。
主要结果
Hospitalization due to LRTI predominated among children with RSV (24/26, 92.3%) and RV infections (20/28, 71.4%), whereas FLU infections were less frequently observed (7/13, 53.9%).
解释与创新
此外,年龄较小、肺炎病史、毛细支气管炎病史和缺乏母乳喂养与住院有关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会影响 监测 策略、临床管理和未来的公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
与上一季节相比,RSV 与最高的出勤率和住院率相关,我们观察到 2024/2025 季节的 RV 感染负担很高。

Rise of Lower Respiratory Tract Infections Other than Respiratory Syncytial Virus in the Emergency Room: Results from the Season (2024/25) of the RISE Study.

Authors: Maria Adams, Vivien Greese, Anissa Paschereit, Tamina Schenck, Julia Kristin Singer, Johannes Paul Paulick, Rafael Paolo Pricoco, Hermann Joseph Girschick, Eva Jenz, Martina Rabe
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Acute respiratory infections (ARI) are a major cause of pediatric emergency department (ED) visits and hospitalizations. Although respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza, and rhinovirus (RV) are common etiological agents, pathogen-specific data from pediatric emergency settings remain limited. METHODS: This prospective surveillance study quantified ARI-related ED visits, described pathogen-specific epidemiology, and assessed attendance and hospitalization rates as well as the distribution of lower versus upper respiratory tract infections (LRTI/URTI) in children younger than 60 months in two pediatric EDs in Berlin between November 1, 2024 and May 31, 2025. Respiratory pathogens were identified using nasopharyngeal swab samples tested by multiplex real-time RT-PCR (QIAstat-Dx® Respiratory Panel Plus). RESULTS: Within the study period 4042 ARI-related ED visits occurred. A total of 310 children were enrolled in our study (median age 18 months); 115 (37.1%) had LRTI and 195 (62.9%) URTI. At least one respiratory pathogen was detected in 271 children (87.4%). The attendance rate for RSV was 24.4% (66 cases), for FLU 25.8% (70 cases), and for RV 29.5% (80 cases). Overall, 93 children (30.0%) were hospitalized, including 65 with LRTI and 28 with URTI. Hospitalization rates were highest for RSV infections (26/65, 40.0%) and RV infections (28/65, 43.1%), and lower for FLU infections (13/69, 18.8%). Hospitalization due to LRTI predominated among children with RSV (24/26, 92.3%) and RV infections (20/28, 71.4%), whereas FLU infections were less frequently observed (7/13, 53.9%). Furthermore, younger age, a history of pneumonia, a history of bronchiolitis, and the absence of breastfeeding were associated with hospitalization. CONCLUSIONS: In contrast to the previous season, in which RSV was associated with the highest attendance and hospitalization rate, we observed a high burden of RV infections in the 2024/2025 season. These findings may impact surveillance strategies, clinical management, and future public health strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute respiratory infections (ARI) are a major cause of pediatric emergency department (ED) visits and hospitalizations.
Design and population
This prospective surveillance study quantified ARI-related ED visits, described pathogen-specific epidemiology, and assessed attendance and hospitalization rates as well as the distribution of lower versus upper respiratory tract infections (LRTI/URTI) in children younger than 60 months in two pediatric EDs in Berlin between November 1, 2024 and May 31, 2025.
Core methods
Respiratory pathogens were identified using nasopharyngeal swab samples tested by multiplex real-time RT-PCR (QIAstat-Dx® Respiratory Panel Plus).
Main results
Hospitalization due to LRTI predominated among children with RSV (24/26, 92.3%) and RV infections (20/28, 71.4%), whereas FLU infections were less frequently observed (7/13, 53.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
Furthermore, younger age, a history of pneumonia, a history of bronchiolitis, and the absence of breastfeeding were associated with hospitalization.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may impact surveillance strategies, clinical management, and future public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
In contrast to the previous season, in which RSV was associated with the highest attendance and hospitalization rate, we observed a high burden of RV infections in the 2024/2025 season.

荷兰 50 岁以上成年人的 呼吸道合胞病毒:数据库和时间序列建模分析(2013-2020 年)。

Respiratory syncytial virus in adults aged ≥ 50 years in the Netherlands: a database and time-series modelling analysis (2013-2020).
作者： Rachel Melanie Reeves, Sonya Parmar, Saskia P. Hagenaars, Brenda N. Baak, Louise Lemmens, Jordy Gaspersz, Karin J. Velthove, Paul Loubet, Richard Osei-Yeboah, Emanuele Amodio
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是老年人急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 的一个重要但历史上未被充分研究的因素。本研究旨在利用时间序列模型量化荷兰二级护理中与 ARI 相关的住院情况并估计与 RSV 相关的疾病负担。方法 这项回顾性 队列研究 研究(2013 年 7 月 1 日至 2020 年 2 月 29 日)利用了从 PHARMO 数据网络常规收集的初级和二级护理数据。通过国际疾病分类 (ICD)-10 代码确定的年龄 ≥ 50 岁患者与 ARI 相关的入院情况,根据患者和临床特征以及医疗资源利用情况进行描述。使用准泊松回归模型估算了按年龄和风险组划分的 RSV 和流感相关 ARI 和下呼吸道疾病 (LRTD) 住院人数和年发生率。结果 63,420 名患者中有 97,279 名 ARI 入院。年龄≥ 50 岁和≥ 75 岁的成年人中,RSV 相关 ARI 入院率估计范围分别为 0.70/1,000 人年 (PY) 至 1.92/1,000 PY。这些组中与 RSV 相关的 LRTD 入院率从 0.63/1,000 PY 到 1.73/1,000 PY 不等。高危人群的住院率较高。估计的 RSV 入院人数比观察到的高出六倍。在高峰月份,高达 30% 的 ARI(LRTD 的 20%)入院病例是由 RSV 引起的。RSV 编码入院的平均住院时间为 7-8 天,30% 的患者在 90 天内因各种原因再次入院。结论 RSV 造成的巨大住院负担要求优先考虑 RSV 预防策略,包括 疫苗接种,以及流感和 ARI 和 LRTD 的其他原因。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是老年人急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 的一个重要但历史上未被充分研究的因素。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究(2013 年 7 月 1 日至 2020 年 2 月 29 日)利用从 PHARMO 数据网络常规收集的初级和二级护理数据来检查年龄 ≥ 50 岁患者的 ARI 相关住院情况。
核心方法
使用准泊松回归模型估算了按年龄和风险组划分的 RSV 和流感相关 ARI 和下呼吸道疾病 (LRTD) 住院人数和年发生率。
主要结果
63,420 名患者中有 97,279 名 ARI 入院。年龄≥ 50 岁和≥ 75 岁的成年人中,RSV 相关 ARI 入院率估计范围分别为 0.70/1,000 人年 (PY) 至 1.92/1,000 PY。
解释与创新
该研究对 50 岁以上成年人与 RSV 相关的入院情况进行了新的估计,强调了该人群中 RSV 造成的巨大负担。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的 RSV 预防策略,特别是对于高危人群,以减少与 RSV 相关的住院负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于常规收集的数据,并未考虑潜在的漏报情况,这可能会影响估计 RSV 入院人数的准确性。

Respiratory syncytial virus in adults aged ≥ 50 years in the Netherlands: a database and time-series modelling analysis (2013-2020).

Authors: Rachel Melanie Reeves, Sonya Parmar, Saskia P. Hagenaars, Brenda N. Baak, Louise Lemmens, Jordy Gaspersz, Karin J. Velthove, Paul Loubet, Richard Osei-Yeboah, Emanuele Amodio
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a significant yet historically understudied contributor to acute respiratory infection (ARI) in older adults. This study aimed to quantify ARI-associated hospital admissions and estimate RSV-related burden of disease in secondary care in the Netherlands using time-series modelling. Methods This retrospective cohort study (1 July 2013 to 29 February 2020) utilised routinely collected primary and secondary care data from the PHARMO Data Network. ARI-associated hospital admissions in patients aged ≥ 50 years, identified via International Classification of Diseases (ICD)-10 codes, were described in terms of patient and clinical characteristics and healthcare resource utilisation. The number and annual rate of RSV- and influenza-associated ARI and lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD) hospital admissions by age and risk group were estimated using a Quasi-Poisson regression model. Results There were 97,279 ARI admissions in 63,420 patients. Estimated RSV-associated ARI hospital admission rates ranged from 0.70/1,000 person-years (PY) to 1.92/1,000 PY in adults aged ≥ 50 and ≥ 75, respectively. RSV-associated LRTD admissions varied from 0.63/1,000 PY to 1.73/1,000 PY in these groups. Hospitalisation rates were higher in high-risk individuals. Estimated RSV admissions were up to six times higher than observed. During peak months, up to 30% of ARI (20% of LRTD) admissions were attributable to RSV. RSV-coded admissions had an average length of stay of 7–8 days, and 30% of patients experienced all-cause readmission within 90 days. Conclusion The substantial hospitalisation burden of RSV calls for prioritisation of RSV prevention strategies, including vaccination, alongside influenza and other causes of ARI and LRTD.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a significant yet historically understudied contributor to acute respiratory infection (ARI) in older adults.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study (1 July 2013 to 29 February 2020) utilised routinely collected primary and secondary care data from the PHARMO Data Network to examine ARI-associated hospital admissions in patients aged ≥ 50 years.
Core methods
The number and annual rate of RSV- and influenza-associated ARI and lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD) hospital admissions by age and risk group were estimated using a Quasi-Poisson regression model.
Main results
There were 97,279 ARI admissions in 63,420 patients. Estimated RSV-associated ARI hospital admission rates ranged from 0.70/1,000 person-years (PY) to 1.92/1,000 PY in adults aged ≥ 50 and ≥ 75, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides novel estimates of RSV-associated hospital admissions in adults aged ≥ 50 years, highlighting the substantial burden of RSV in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for targeted RSV prevention strategies, particularly in high-risk individuals, to reduce the hospitalisation burden associated with RSV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on routinely collected data and does not account for potential underreporting, which may affect the accuracy of the estimated RSV admissions.

压力之下:从澳大利亚悉尼快速实施 呼吸道合胞病毒 孕产妇 疫苗 和婴儿 尼塞韦单抗 中吸取的经验教训。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.
作者： Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
摘要中文翻译目标:分享悉尼地方卫生区 (SLHD) 推出 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。服务类型:推出 RSV 母体 疫苗 和用于新生儿的可注射长效单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗,以保护他们免受 RSV 感染和住院治疗。方法:以计数和比例的形式对两家 SLHD 妇产医院的 RSV 免疫状况电子病历中的去识别化汇总数据进行描述性分析。实施研究综合框架被用作反思推出过程的镜头。结果:两家 SLHD 医院分娩妇女的 RSV 疫苗接种 覆盖率从推出第一个月(2025 年 2 月)的 4% 提高到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月的 70-74%。随着产前覆盖率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36%的家庭拒绝了尼塞韦单抗,但没有记录拒绝的原因。随着推广的进展,RSV 免疫状态的记录得到改善。经验教训:多学科领导者和临床医生的参与对于协调应对和计划成功推出至关重要。利用现有的教育结构、员工会议和使用在线预录音可以优化临床医生教育。新计划需要时间才能融入常规临床实践,而涉及为不同群体提供多种免疫产品的计划需要明确的信息传递和系统保障措施以防止错误。将免疫接种作为标准做法纳入医疗记录工作流程可以支持计划的采用和采用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在分享快速实施 RSV 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。这项研究需要解决将新的 RSV 预防策略纳入常规临床实践的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在悉尼地方卫生区的两家妇产医院推出 RSV 母体 疫苗 和可注射单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗。该研究时间跨度为 2025 年 2 月至 9 月,重点关注母亲和符合条件的新生儿的免疫接种情况。
核心方法
来自电子病历的去识别化摘要数据被描述性地分析为计数和比例。实施研究综合框架用于评估推广过程并确定影响免疫接种的因素。
主要结果
RSV 疫苗接种 在两家医院分娩的妇女的覆盖率从推出第一个月的 4% 增加到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月的 70-74%。随着产前覆盖率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36%的家庭拒绝了尼塞韦单抗,拒绝的原因没有记录。随着推广的进展,RSV 免疫状态的记录得到改善。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明多学科领导者和临床医生的协调参与对于项目的成功推出至关重要。该研究强调了在针对不同群体实施多种免疫产品时,明确的信息传递和系统保障措施的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过证明临床医生教育和将免疫接种纳入医疗记录工作流程等策略的有效性,为未来 RSV 预防计划的发展提供信息。他们还提供了有关提高 RSV 疫苗 和单克隆抗体的吸收率和降低拒绝率的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它依赖于未识别身份的汇总数据,并且没有记录拒绝的原因。由于分析的描述性以及缺乏详细的统计或因果解释,证据的强度中等。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.

Authors: Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To share lessons learnt from the Sydney Local Health District (SLHD) rollout of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prevention products, and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. TYPE OF SERVICE: Rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and the injectable, long-acting monoclonal antibody nirsevimab for newborn infants to protect them from RSV infection and hospitalisation. METHODS: Deidentified summary data from the electronic medical record on RSV immunisation status in two SLHD maternity hospitals were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used as a lens to reflect on the rollout process. RESULTS: RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in two SLHD hospitals improved from 4% in the first month of rollout (February 2025) to a range of 70-74% from June to September 2025. As antenatal coverage improved, the number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reason for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved as the rollout progressed. LESSONS LEARNT: Engaging multidisciplinary leaders and clinicians is critical for a coordinated response and program rollout success. Leveraging existing education structures, staff meetings and using online prerecording can optimise clinician education. New programs take time to embed into routine clinical practice, and programs that involve delivering multiple immunisation products for distinct groups require clear messaging and systemic safeguards to prevent errors. Integrating immunisation into medical record workflows as standard practice can support program adoption and uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to share lessons from the rapid implementation of RSV prevention products and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. The research was needed to address the challenge of integrating new RSV prevention strategies into routine clinical practice.
Design and population
The study involved the rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and the injectable monoclonal antibody nirsevimab in two maternity hospitals within the Sydney Local Health District. The study period spanned from February to September 2025, focusing on immunisation uptake among mothers and eligible newborns.
Core methods
Deidentified summary data from electronic medical records were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used to evaluate the rollout process and identify factors influencing immunisation uptake.
Main results
RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in the two hospitals increased from 4% in the first month of rollout to 70-74% from June to September 2025. As antenatal coverage improved, the number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reasons for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved as the rollout progressed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that coordinated engagement with multidisciplinary leaders and clinicians is critical for successful program rollout. The study highlights the importance of clear messaging and systemic safeguards in implementing multiple immunisation products for distinct groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of future RSV prevention programs by demonstrating the effectiveness of strategies such as clinician education and integration of immunisation into medical record workflows. They also provide insights into improving uptake and reducing refusal rates for RSV vaccines and monoclonal antibodies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on deidentified summary data and the absence of recorded reasons for refusal. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the descriptive nature of the analysis and the lack of detailed statistical or causal interpretations.

Fulminant necrotizing enterocolitis associated with severe RSV infection in a preterm infant following Nirsevimab prophylaxis: a case report.
作者： Céline Weber, Thomas Menter, Raphael N. Vuille-dit-Bille, Philippe Meyer, Stefan Gerhard Holland-Cunz, Maya Caroline Andre, Maya C. André
摘要中文翻译使用单克隆抗体 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防在预防婴儿严重下呼吸道感染方面显示出高效。然而,导致突破性感染的免疫病理学现象的序列以及 RSV 在引起肺外疾病中的作用仍然知之甚少。我们报告了一名早产儿,在使用 尼塞韦单抗 被动免疫 22 天后,在严重 RSV-毛细支气管炎 的情况下,出现了异常严重的坏死性小肠结肠炎 (NEC),需要多次紧急剖腹手术和长期重症监护 治疗。使用经过验证的 RSV PCR 检测在急性疾病期获得的切除肠道组织中回顾性检测到 RSV RNA,但在随后的手术重建期间收集的组织中未检测到 RSV RNA。我们的病例强调临床医生需要对严重 RSV 感染婴儿的肠道并发症保持高度怀疑。尽管这些发现提出了肠道 RSV 受累的可能性,但从这一单一病例中无法确定 尼塞韦单抗 预防、抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 和 NEC 之间的因果关系。需要更多临床和转化研究的证实。需要进一步的临床和转化研究来阐明 RSV 在肠道疾病中的潜在作用以及严重突破性感染的机制。

Fulminant necrotizing enterocolitis associated with severe RSV infection in a preterm infant following Nirsevimab prophylaxis: a case report.

Authors: Céline Weber, Thomas Menter, Raphael N. Vuille-dit-Bille, Philippe Meyer, Stefan Gerhard Holland-Cunz, Maya Caroline Andre, Maya C. André
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prophylaxis with the monoclonal antibody Nirsevimab has shown high efficacy in preventing severe lower respiratory tract infections in infants. However, the sequences of immunopathological phenomena leading to breakthrough infections and the role of RSV in causing extrapulmonary disease remain poorly understood. We report on a former preterm infant who developed an unusually severe form of necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC) requiring multiple emergency laparotomies and prolonged intensive care treatment in the context of severe RSV-bronchiolitis 22 days after passive immunization with Nirsevimab. RSV RNA was retrospectively detected using a validated RSV PCR assay in resected intestinal tissue obtained during the acute disease phase but was not detected in tissue collected during later surgical reconstruction. Our case highlights the need for clinicians to maintain a high index of suspicion for intestinal complications in infants with severe RSV infection. Although these findings raise the possibility of intestinal RSV involvement, a causal relationship between Nirsevimab prophylaxis, antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), and NEC cannot be established from this single case. Confirmation in additional clinical and translational studies is required. Further clinical and translational studies are required to clarify the potential role of RSV in intestinal disease and the mechanisms underlying severe breakthrough infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
However, the sequences of immunopathological phenomena leading to breakthrough infections and the role of RSV in causing extrapulmonary disease remain poorly understood.
Design and population
Our case highlights the need for clinicians to maintain a high index of suspicion for intestinal complications in infants with severe RSV infection.
Core methods
RSV RNA was retrospectively detected using a validated RSV PCR assay in resected intestinal tissue obtained during the acute disease phase but was not detected in tissue collected during later surgical reconstruction.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
Confirmation in additional clinical and translational studies is required.
Scientific and public-health significance
We report on a former preterm infant who developed an unusually severe form of necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC) requiring multiple emergency laparotomies and prolonged intensive care treatment in the context of severe RSV-bronchiolitis 22 days after passive immunization with Nirsevimab.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further clinical and translational studies are required to clarify the potential role of RSV in intestinal disease and the mechanisms underlying severe breakthrough infections.

呼吸道合胞病毒 感染情况下同种异体造血干细胞移植后继发性肺泡蛋白沉积症:A 病例报告。

Secondary Pulmonary Alveolar Proteinosis after Allogeneic Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation in the Setting of an Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection: A Case Report.
作者： Yuki Mori, Koichi Nakase, Risa Hashida, Yuichiro Nawa, Norihiko Nakanishi
摘要中文翻译继发性肺泡蛋白沉积症 (SPAP) 很少见,并且常常导致不良结果;因此,正确诊断和治疗至关重要。一名 23 岁男性在就诊前 18 个月接受了同种异体造血干细胞移植 (allo-HSCT),在 RSV 感染的情况下在异体 HSCT 后被诊断为 SPAP。患者因RSV感染治疗后呼吸道症状改善,随访期间SPAP逐渐改善。该病例表明,对于 RSV 感染且出现持续呼吸道症状或放射学异常的 HSCT 后患者,应考虑 SPAP。

问题与背景
继发性肺泡蛋白沉积症 (SPAP) 很少见,并且常常导致不良结果;因此,正确诊断和治疗至关重要。

解释与创新
该病例强调了认识到 SPAP 作为接受异基因 HSCT 并感染 RSV 的患者的潜在并发症的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,临床医生应意识到 HSCT 后 RSV 感染患者发生 SPAP 的可能性,并在鉴别 诊断 时予以考虑。

Secondary Pulmonary Alveolar Proteinosis after Allogeneic Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation in the Setting of an Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection: A Case Report.

Authors: Yuki Mori, Koichi Nakase, Risa Hashida, Yuichiro Nawa, Norihiko Nakanishi
Original abstract (English)
Secondary pulmonary alveolar proteinosis (SPAP) is rare and often results in poor outcomes; therefore, it is crucial to diagnose and treat it appropriately. A 23-year-old man who received allogeneic hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (allo-HSCT) 18 months before presentation was diagnosed with SPAP after allo-HSCT in the setting of an RSV infection. The patient's respiratory symptoms improved after treatment for RSV infection, and SPAP gradually improved during the follow-up period. This case suggests that SPAP should be considered in post-HSCT patients with persistent respiratory symptoms or radiological abnormalities in the setting of an RSV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Secondary pulmonary alveolar proteinosis (SPAP) is rare and often results in poor outcomes; therefore, it is crucial to diagnose and treat it appropriately.
Design and population
A 23-year-old man who received allogeneic hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (allo-HSCT) 18 months before presentation was diagnosed with SPAP after allo-HSCT in the setting of an RSV infection.
Core methods
The patient's respiratory symptoms improved after treatment for RSV infection, and SPAP gradually improved during the follow-up period.
Main results
This case suggests that SPAP should be considered in post-HSCT patients with persistent respiratory symptoms or radiological abnormalities in the setting of an RSV infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The case highlights the importance of recognizing SPAP as a potential complication in patients who have undergone allo-HSCT and are infected with RSV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that clinicians should be aware of the possibility of SPAP in post-HSCT patients with RSV infection and consider it in the differential diagnosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a case report, which limits its generalizability and provides moderate evidence for the association between RSV infection and SPAP in post-HSCT patients.

针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 的小分子进入抑制剂的验证。

Validation of Small-Molecule Entry Inhibitors Targeting the Respiratory Syncytial Virus.
作者： Christina Karhan, Svenja M Sake, Melanie Urbanek-Quaing, Diletta Bergamo, Jessica Rückert, Jiabin Huang, Christina Grethe, Antonia P Gunesch, Anne Kühnel, Birthe Reinecke
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是全世界严重呼吸道感染的主要原因,尤其是婴儿、幼儿和老年人。尽管有 疫苗 和单克隆抗体等预防选择,但针对急性感染的有效 抗病毒药物 疗法仍然有限。因此,我们研究了在高通量试验中发现的小分子进入抑制剂,并通过进行再合成并确定其 抗病毒药物 活性来详细评估最有前途的候选物。不幸的是,核磁共振(NMR)光谱和液相色谱-质谱(LC-MS)的分析研究揭示了两类化合物的筛选样品和重新合成的化合物之间的差异,突出了命中验证的复杂性和杂质的潜在影响。通过发布这些结果,我们的目的不仅是强调筛选活动中严格的分析和生物学验证的重要性,而且还支持开发改进的策略来识别可靠的 RSV 抑制剂。我们还认为,发布假阳性结果非常重要,以避免在将此类化合物假设为真实结果的项目上花费时间。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是全世界严重呼吸道感染的主要原因,尤其是婴儿、幼儿和老年人。尽管有 疫苗 和单克隆抗体等预防选择,但针对急性感染的有效 抗病毒药物 疗法仍然有限。
设计与对象
我们研究了在高通量活动中发现的小分子进入抑制剂,并通过进行再合成和确定其 抗病毒药物 活性来详细评估最有前途的候选物。
核心方法
通过核磁共振(NMR)光谱和液相色谱-质谱(LC-MS)分析研究来评估这些化合物。
主要结果
通过核磁共振 (NMR) 光谱和液相色谱-质谱 (LC-MS) 进行的分析研究揭示了两类化合物的筛选样品和重新合成的化合物之间的差异。
解释与创新
通过发布这些结果,我们的目的不仅是强调筛选活动中严格的分析和生物学验证的重要性,而且还支持开发改进的策略来识别可靠的 RSV 抑制剂。
科研与公卫意义
我们还认为,发布假阳性结果非常重要,以避免在将此类化合物假设为真实结果的项目上花费时间。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性或证据强度。

Validation of Small-Molecule Entry Inhibitors Targeting the Respiratory Syncytial Virus.

Authors: Christina Karhan, Svenja M Sake, Melanie Urbanek-Quaing, Diletta Bergamo, Jessica Rückert, Jiabin Huang, Christina Grethe, Antonia P Gunesch, Anne Kühnel, Birthe Reinecke
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe respiratory infections worldwide, particularly in infants, young children, and elderly individuals. Despite the availability of preventive options such as vaccines and monoclonal antibodies, effective antiviral therapies for acute infections remain limited. Therefore, we investigated small-molecule entry inhibitors identified in a high-throughput campaing and evaluated the most promising candidates in detail by performing resynthesis and determining their antiviral activity. Unfortunately, analytical investigations by nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectroscopy and liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS) revealed discrepancies between the screening samples and the resynthesized compounds for two compound classes, highlighting the complexity of hit validation and the potential influence of impurities. By publishing these results, we aim not only to underscore the importance of rigorous analytical and biological validation in screening campaigns but also to support the development of improved strategies for identifying reliable RSV inhibitors. We also believe it is important to publish false-positive hits to avoid time efforts on projects that assume such compounds as genuine hits.
View research seven-element analysis

By publishing these results, we aim not only to underscore the importance of rigorous analytical and biological validation in screening campaigns but also to support the development of improved strategies for identifying reliable RSV inhibitors.
Scientific and public-health significance
We also believe it is important to publish false-positive hits to avoid time efforts on projects that assume such compounds as genuine hits.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength for the study.

呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)——呼吸系统疾病预防的当务之急

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)–An Urgent Priority in Respiratory Disease Prevention
作者： Poonam Talwan, Darsh Gautam
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿、老年人以及患有心肺合并症或免疫功能低下的个体下呼吸道疾病的主要原因。最近的进展使得分层预防成为可能,通过用于婴儿保护的母体 疫苗接种、用于新生儿和特定幼儿的长效单克隆抗体以及用于老年人的 疫苗。然而,监测 中的不平等、季节性驱动的时间安排以及负担能力和交付方面的挑战(特别是在低收入和中等收入国家)阻碍了公平的采用。这篇社论强调了重点人群,综合了当前的证据和政策方向,并概述了融资、采购和综合母婴保健服务方面的可行杠杆,以加速获得服务,使 RSV 预防成为紧迫且可实现的全球卫生优先事项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿、老年人以及患有心肺合并症或免疫功能低下的个体下呼吸道疾病的主要原因。
设计与对象
这篇社论综合了当前的证据和政策方向,强调优先人群,并概述了融资、采购和综合母婴健康服务方面的可行杠杆,以加速 RSV 预防。
核心方法
最近的进展使得分层预防成为可能,通过用于婴儿保护的母体 疫苗接种、用于新生儿和特定幼儿的长效单克隆抗体以及用于老年人的 疫苗。
主要结果
该社论强调了通过各种策略预防 RSV 的潜力,包括母体 疫苗接种、单克隆抗体和针对特定人群的 疫苗。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为强调了将 RSV 问题作为全球卫生优先事项的紧迫性,并概述了改善获取和实施的可行步骤。

研究结果表明需要对高危人群采取有针对性的干预措施,并强调改善 RSV 预防的 监测、可负担性和输送系统的重要性。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)–An Urgent Priority in Respiratory Disease Prevention

Authors: Poonam Talwan, Darsh Gautam
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract disease in infants, young children, older adults, and individuals with cardiopulmonary comorbidities or immunocompromised states. Recent advances enable layered prevention through maternal vaccination for infant protection, long-acting monoclonal antibodies for newborns and select toddlers, and vaccines for older adults. However, inequities in surveillance, seasonality-driven timing, and challenges in affordability and delivery—particularly in low- and middle-income countries— impede equitable uptake. This editorial underscores priority populations, synthesizes current evidence and policy directions, and outlines actionable levers in financing, procurement, and integrated maternal–child health delivery to accelerate access, making RSV prevention an urgent and achievable global health priority.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract disease in infants, young children, older adults, and individuals with cardiopulmonary comorbidities or immunocompromised states.
Design and population
This editorial synthesizes current evidence and policy directions to underscore priority populations and outline actionable levers in financing, procurement, and integrated maternal–child health delivery to accelerate access to RSV prevention.
Core methods
Recent advances enable layered prevention through maternal vaccination for infant protection, long-acting monoclonal antibodies for newborns and select toddlers, and vaccines for older adults.
Main results
The editorial highlights the potential for RSV prevention through various strategies, including maternal vaccination, monoclonal antibodies, and vaccines for specific populations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as emphasizing the urgency of addressing RSV as a global health priority and outline actionable steps to improve access and implementation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted interventions in high-risk populations and highlight the importance of improving surveillance, affordability, and delivery systems for RSV prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessments.

母亲 疫苗接种 与 尼塞韦单抗 的使用以及足月婴儿中 呼吸道合胞病毒 的感染

Maternal Vaccination vs Nirsevimab Use and Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection Among Full-Term Infants
作者： Karen Blake Jacobson, Andrew J. Watson, Lauren D. Liao, Sally C. Stephens, Maqdooda A. Merchant, Ousseny Zerbo, Nicola P. Klein
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估使用 RSVpreF 的母亲 疫苗接种 与婴儿 尼塞韦单抗 在预防 2023 年至 2025 年 RSV 季节出生的足月婴儿 RSV 感染和住院方面的相对有效性。自2023年起,婴儿可以通过母体疫苗接种或婴儿单克隆抗体治疗来保护婴儿免受RSV感染。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2023 年 12 月 3 日至 2024 年 3 月 30 日或 2024 年 9 月 29 日至 2025 年 3 月 15 日在北加州 Kaiser Permanente 出生的足月婴儿。婴儿根据出生周、性别和母亲因素进行匹配,RSVpreF 与 尼塞韦单抗 的最佳完全匹配(高达 1:5)对于未受保护的婴儿,最多为 1:10。
核心方法
该研究使用 PCR 确认的 RSV 感染和住院作为主要结局。暴露是妊娠 32 至 36 周时的母亲 RSVpreF 或婴儿 尼塞韦单抗 至 7 天的暴露。主要结果指标是限制 8 至 180 天婴儿或 RSV 季节结束(两个季节均为 4 月 30 日)期间因感染和住院而损失的平均时间。
主要结果
在15,124例匹配的婴儿中,有146例(0.97%)出现呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)感染(其中RSVpreF组106例,尼塞韦单抗组40例),有12例(0.08%)住院治疗(其中RSVpreF组7例,尼塞韦单抗组5例)。RSVpreF与尼塞韦单抗相比,对感染的相对预防效果(rPE)为-5.3%(95%置信区间:-44.8%至23.4%),对住院的相对预防效果为61.5%(95%置信区间:-10.8%至86.7%)。与无保护相比,RSVpreF将感染率降低了69.3%(95%置信区间:59.9%至76.6%),将住院率降低了86.4%(95%置信区间:66.4%至94.5%);尼塞韦单抗分别将感染率降低了65.2%(95%置信区间:51.0%至75.4%)和住院率降低了73.8%(95%置信区间:33.4%至89.7%)。
解释与创新
研究发现,母亲RSVpreF和婴儿尼塞韦单抗在RSV感染或住院方面没有统计学上的显着差异。与没有保护的情况相比,这两种产品都可以减少感染和住院治疗。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究支持美国现行的母亲 RSVpreF 或婴儿 尼塞韦单抗 指南,以保护所有婴儿免受 RSV 感染,表明这两种方法都能有效降低 RSV 感染率和住院率。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的未测量混杂因素而受到限制。样本量足以进行初步分析,但 rPE 估计值的置信区间很宽,表明结果存在一定的不确定性。

Maternal Vaccination vs Nirsevimab Use and Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection Among Full-Term Infants

Authors: Karen Blake Jacobson, Andrew J. Watson, Lauren D. Liao, Sally C. Stephens, Maqdooda A. Merchant, Ousseny Zerbo, Nicola P. Klein
Original abstract (English)
Importance: Since 2023, infants can be protected from respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) through a vaccine (RSVpreF) for pregnant persons or a monoclonal antibody (nirsevimab) for infants. Objective: To assess the relative product effectiveness (rPE) of maternal RSVpreF vs infant nirsevimab against RSV infection and hospitalization among infants born in the 2023 to 2025 RSV seasons. Design, Setting, and Participants: This retrospective cohort study included full-term infants born at Kaiser Permanente Northern California (KPNC), an integrated health care system with approximately 40 000 births each year, from December 3, 2023, to March 30, 2024, or September 29, 2024, to March 15, 2025. Infants protected by RSVpreF were matched to infants protected with nirsevimab with optimal full matching (up to 1:5), adjusting for birth week, sex, and maternal factors. Each group was also compared with infants with no protection, matching unprotected infants to both RSVpreF and nirsevimab infants (up to 1:10). Exposures: Maternal RSVpreF at 32 to 36 weeks' gestation or infant nirsevimab through age 7 days. Main Outcomes and Measures: Polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-confirmed RSV and RSV hospitalization (PCR-confirmed RSV 10 days before to 7 days after hospitalization) in infants aged 8 to 180 days or through RSV season end (April 30 in both seasons). rPE was estimated from age 8 to 180 days using restricted mean time lost. Results: Of 15 124 matched infants (7729 [51.1%] male) in the primary analysis, 146 (0.97%) had RSV infection (106 of 11 293 [0.94%] in RSVpreF group and 40 of 3831 [1.04%] in nirsevimab group) and 12 (0.08%) were hospitalized (7 [0.06%] in RSVpreF group and 5 [0.13%] in nirsevimab group). The rPE of RSVpreF vs nirsevimab was -5.3% (95% CI, -44.8% to 23.4%) against infection and 61.5% (95% CI, -10.8% to 86.7%) against hospitalization. Compared with no protection, RSVpreF rPE was 69.3% (95% CI, 59.9% to 76.6%) against infection and 86.4% (95% CI, 66.4% to 94.5%) against hospitalization; nirsevimab rPE was 65.2% (95% CI, 51.0%-75.4%) and 73.8% (95% CI, 33.4%-89.7%), respectively. Conclusion: In this cohort study of infants born in the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 RSV seasons, no statistically significant difference between maternal RSVpreF and infant nirsevimab in RSV infection or hospitalization was detected. Compared with no protection, both products were associated with fewer infections and hospitalizations. This study supports current US guidelines for either maternal RSVpreF or infant nirsevimab to protect all infants from RSV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the relative effectiveness of maternal vaccination with RSVpreF versus infant nirsevimab in preventing RSV infection and hospitalization among full-term infants born in the 2023 to 2025 RSV seasons. Since 2023, infants can be protected from RSV through maternal vaccination or infant monoclonal antibody treatment.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study included full-term infants born at Kaiser Permanente Northern California from December 3, 2023, to March 30, 2024, or September 29, 2024, to March 15, 2025. Infants were matched based on birth week, sex, and maternal factors, with optimal full matching (up to 1:5) for RSVpreF vs nirsevimab and up to 1:10 for unprotected infants.
Core methods
The study used PCR-confirmed RSV infection and hospitalization as primary outcomes. Exposures were maternal RSVpreF at 32 to 36 weeks' gestation or infant nirsevimab through age 7 days. The main outcome measures were restricted mean time lost for infection and hospitalization in infants aged 8 to 180 days or through RSV season end (April 30 in both seasons).
Main results
Of 15,124 matched infants, 146 (0.97%) had RSV infection (106 in the RSVpreF group and 40 in the nirsevimab group) and 12 (0.08%) were hospitalized (7 in the RSVpreF group and 5 in the nirsevimab group). The rPE of RSVpreF vs nirsevimab was -5.3% (95% CI, -44.8% to 23.4%) against infection and 61.5% (95% CI, -10.8% to 86.7%) against hospitalization. Compared with no protection, RSVpreF had a 69.3% (95% CI, 59.9% to 76.6%) reduction in infections and 86.4% (95% CI, 66.4% to 94.5%) in hospitalizations; nirsevimab had a 65.2% (95% CI, 51.0% to 75.4%) and 73.8% (95% CI, 33.4% to 89.7%) reduction, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found no statistically significant difference between maternal RSVpreF and infant nirsevimab in RSV infection or hospitalization. Both products were associated with fewer infections and hospitalizations compared to no protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study supports current US guidelines for either maternal RSVpreF or infant nirsevimab to protect all infants from RSV, indicating that both methods are effective in reducing RSV infection and hospitalization rates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the potential for unmeasured confounders. The sample size was adequate for the primary analysis, but the confidence intervals for the rPE estimates are wide, indicating some uncertainty in the results.

马来西亚森美兰州住院下呼吸道感染儿科患者的 流行病学 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 的季节性(2017-2024 年)

Epidemiology and seasonality of respiratory syncytial virus among hospitalized pediatric patients with lower respiratory tract infections in Negeri Sembilan, Malaysia (2017–2024)
作者： Nuur Hafizah Md Iderus, Sarbhan Singh, David Chun-Ern Ng, Sumarni Mohd Ghazali, Nur’ain Mohd Ghazali, Mei Cheng Lim, Nur Ar Rabiah Binti Ahmad, Qistina Ruslan, Chuin-Hen Liew, Marlindawati Mohd Ali
摘要中文翻译背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是全球重大的公共卫生问题,温带地区具有明显的季节性模式,而热带地区全年都会经历 RSV 并伴有季节性波动。本研究调查了影响马来西亚森美兰州 12 岁以下住院儿童 RSV 病例的季节性和外源性因素。方法 使用分解和自相关函数对 417 个连续 WHO 流行病学周(2017 年第 1 周到 2024 年第 52 周)住院儿科患者中出现急性下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的每周实验室确诊的 RSV 阳性病例进行分析。通过互相关和外生变量自回归综合移动平均 (ARIMAX) 模型评估 RSV 与共循环病毒、气象变量和假期之间的关系。结果 在总共 45,817 份检测中,共有 6,545 份 (14.29%) 样本呈 RSV 阳性。历史峰值(7 月至 9 月)在 2020 年至 2023 年期间被破坏,但在 2024 年重新建立。ARIMAX (2,0,4) (1,0,0) 52 模型显示,RSV 动态主要由内部自回归模式驱动,而不是气象因素。主要公众假期表现出显着的双重效应,报告病例数立即下降 (p = 0.012),随后显着激增 (p = 0.014)。流感活动是一个滞后一周的重要预测因素 (p = 0.012)。结论 大流行暂时改变了 RSV 的季节性,此后已恢复正常。与假期和流感活动的关联表明,行为因素和病毒干扰可能会影响 RSV 循环。这些发现强调需要动态 监测 来优化马来西亚未来 RSV 免疫接种的时间。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查影响马来西亚森美兰州 12 岁以下住院儿童 RSV 病例的季节性和外源性因素,解决了解热带地区 RSV 独特季节性模式的需要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2017 年至 2024 年连续 417 个世卫组织流行病学周内住院的急性下呼吸道感染儿科患者中每周实验室确诊的 RSV 阳性病例,重点关注马来西亚森美兰州。
核心方法
使用分解和自相关函数分析 RSV 动态,并通过互相关和 ARIMAX 建模评估与共循环病毒、气象变量和假期的关系。
主要结果
在 45,817 份检测中,共有 6,545 份 (14.29%) 样本呈 RSV 阳性。历史峰值在 2020-2023 年期间被破坏,但在 2024 年重新建立。ARIMAX (2,0,4) (1,0,0) 52 模型表明 RSV 动态主要由内部自回归模式驱动。重大公众假期显示出显着的双重影响,报告病例数立即减少,随后激增。流感活动是一个滞后一周的重要预测因素。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,流感大流行暂时改变了 RSV 的季节性,此后该季节已恢复正常。与假期和流感活动的关联表明,行为因素和病毒干扰可能会影响 RSV 循环。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要动态 监测 来优化马来西亚未来 RSV 免疫接种的时间,强调了解 RSV 在热带环境中行为的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果受到对历史数据的依赖和潜在的混杂因素的限制。由于该研究的观察性质且缺乏随机对照试验,证据强度中等。

Epidemiology and seasonality of respiratory syncytial virus among hospitalized pediatric patients with lower respiratory tract infections in Negeri Sembilan, Malaysia (2017–2024)

Authors: Nuur Hafizah Md Iderus, Sarbhan Singh, David Chun-Ern Ng, Sumarni Mohd Ghazali, Nur’ain Mohd Ghazali, Mei Cheng Lim, Nur Ar Rabiah Binti Ahmad, Qistina Ruslan, Chuin-Hen Liew, Marlindawati Mohd Ali
Original abstract (English)
Background Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) remains a significant public health concern globally, with distinct seasonal patterns in temperate regions, while tropical settings experience RSV year-round with seasonal fluctuations. This study investigated seasonality and exogenous factors influencing RSV cases among hospitalized children under 12 years in Negeri Sembilan, Malaysia. Methods Weekly laboratory-confirmed RSV-positive cases among hospitalized pediatric patients presenting with acute lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) across 417 continuous WHO epidemiological weeks (EpiWeek 1, 2017 to EpiWeek 52, 2024) were analyzed using decomposition and autocorrelation functions. Relationships between RSV and co-circulating viruses, meteorological variables, and holidays were assessed via cross-correlation and Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average with Exogenous variables (ARIMAX) modeling. Results A total of 6,545 (14.29%) samples were RSV-positive from a total of 45,817 tested. Historical peaks (July–September) were disrupted during 2020–2023 but re-established in 2024. The ARIMAX (2,0,4) (1,0,0) 52 model revealed that RSV dynamics were driven primarily by internal autoregressive patterns rather than meteorological factors. Major public holidays exhibited a significant dual effect, with an immediate decrease in reported cases ( p = 0.012) followed by a significant surge ( p = 0.014). Influenza activity was a significant predictor with a one-week lag ( p = 0.012). Conclusion The pandemic temporarily shifted RSV seasonality, which has since normalized. The associations with holiday periods and influenza activity suggests that behavioral factors and viral interference may influence RSV circulation. These findings emphasize the need for dynamic surveillance to optimize the timing of future RSV immunizations in Malaysia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the seasonality and exogenous factors influencing RSV cases among hospitalized children under 12 years in Negeri Sembilan, Malaysia, addressing the need to understand RSV's distinct seasonal patterns in tropical settings.
Design and population
The study analyzed weekly laboratory-confirmed RSV-positive cases among hospitalized pediatric patients with acute lower respiratory tract infections across 417 continuous WHO epidemiological weeks from 2017 to 2024, focusing on Negeri Sembilan, Malaysia.
Core methods
RSV dynamics were analyzed using decomposition and autocorrelation functions, and relationships with co-circulating viruses, meteorological variables, and holidays were assessed via cross-correlation and ARIMAX modeling.
Main results
A total of 6,545 (14.29%) samples were RSV-positive from 45,817 tested. Historical peaks were disrupted during 2020–2023 but re-established in 2024. The ARIMAX (2,0,4) (1,0,0) 52 model indicated that RSV dynamics were primarily driven by internal autoregressive patterns. Major public holidays showed a significant dual effect, with an immediate decrease followed by a surge in reported cases. Influenza activity was a significant predictor with a one-week lag.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the pandemic temporarily shifted RSV seasonality, which has since normalized. The associations with holiday periods and influenza activity indicate that behavioral factors and viral interference may influence RSV circulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings emphasize the need for dynamic surveillance to optimize the timing of future RSV immunizations in Malaysia, highlighting the importance of understanding RSV's behavior in tropical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are limited by the reliance on historical data and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of randomized controlled trials.

住院儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染临床表现与年龄相关的差异:一项三年回顾性研究

Age-Related Differences in the Clinical Presentation of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in Hospitalized Children: A Three-Year Retrospective Study
作者： Andreea Văduva, Alexandru Dinulescu, Anca Cristina Drăgănescu, Doina Anca Pleșca
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估因RSV感染而住院的儿童在临床表现和呼吸道严重程度方面与年龄相关的差异,考虑到婴儿早期患严重疾病的风险最高,因此有必要了解年龄如何影响婴儿和幼儿的疾病严重程度和临床表型。
设计与对象
一项回顾性观察性 队列研究 研究对 2022 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间因实验室确诊 RSV 感染而住院的 241 名 0-24 个月儿童进行,涵盖四个年龄组(0-3、4-6、7-12 和 13-24 个月)。
核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归比较不同年龄组的临床表现,调整性别、早产、病毒合并感染和喂养类型。应用霍尔姆程序来解释多重比较。
主要结果
Fever increased significantly with age, from 48.8% among children aged 0–3 months to 81.7% among those aged 13–24 months (p < 0.001). Each additional month of age was associated with higher odds of fever (aOR 1.090, 95% CI 1.045–1.137; Holm-adjusted p < 0.001) and lower odds of thoracoabdominal asynchrony (aOR 0.951, 95% CI 0.917–0.986; Holm-adjusted p = 0.044). Infants aged 0–3 months required oxygen more frequently than older children (46.5% vs. 25.3%) and had significantly higher adjusted odds of oxygen requirement (aOR 2.54, 95% CI 1.26–5.0; p = 0.009).
解释与创新
该研究强调了特定年龄的临床评估和婴儿在生命最初几个月的脆弱性,尽管发烧频率较低,但年龄较小的婴儿表现出更明显的呼吸窘迫症状,并且需要补充氧气的可能性特别高。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 针对 RSV 感染的策略提供信息,强调特定年龄临床评估的重要性,并加强婴儿在生命最初几个月的脆弱性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、潜在的选择偏倚以及需要更大的样本量来证实研究结果而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Age-Related Differences in the Clinical Presentation of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in Hospitalized Children: A Three-Year Retrospective Study

Authors: Andreea Văduva, Alexandru Dinulescu, Anca Cristina Drăgănescu, Doina Anca Pleșca
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of hospitalization in infants and young children, with the highest risk of severe disease occurring during early infancy. Age may influence not only disease severity but also the clinical phenotype of RSV infection. This study aimed to evaluate age-related differences in clinical presentation and respiratory severity among children hospitalized with RSV infection. Methods: We conducted a retrospective observational cohort study including 241 children aged 0–24 months hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed RSV infection between January 2022 and December 2024. Clinical manifestations were compared across four age groups (0–3, 4–6, 7–12, and 13–24 months). Multivariable logistic regression was used to assess associations between exact age in months and individual clinical manifestations, adjusting for sex, prematurity, documented viral co-infection and feeding type. The Holm procedure was applied to account for multiple comparisons in the exact-age analyses. Supplemental oxygen requirement was evaluated as a marker of respiratory severity. Results: Fever increased significantly with age, from 48.8% among children aged 0–3 months to 81.7% among those aged 13–24 months (p < 0.001). In adjusted analyses, each additional month of age was associated with higher odds of fever (aOR 1.090, 95% CI 1.045–1.137; Holm-adjusted p < 0.001) and lower odds of thoracoabdominal asynchrony (aOR 0.951, 95% CI 0.917–0.986; Holm-adjusted p = 0.044). Associations with tachypnea/polypnea and intercostal/subcostal retractions did not remain significant after correction for multiple comparisons. Supplemental oxygen was required in 29.0% of patients. Infants aged 0–3 months required oxygen more frequently than older children (46.5% vs. 25.3%) and had significantly higher adjusted odds of oxygen requirement (aOR 2.54, 95% CI 1.26–5.0; p = 0.009). Conclusions: The clinical phenotype and respiratory severity of RSV infection may vary s with age during the first two years of life. Younger infants exhibit more prominent signs of respiratory distress despite a lower frequency of fever, and infants aged 0–3 months have a particularly high likelihood of requiring supplemental oxygen. These findings highlight the importance of age-specific clinical assessment and reinforce the vulnerability of infants during the earliest months of life.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate age-related differences in clinical presentation and respiratory severity among children hospitalized with RSV infection, addressing the need to understand how age influences disease severity and clinical phenotype in infants and young children, given the highest risk of severe disease during early infancy.
Design and population
A retrospective observational cohort study was conducted on 241 children aged 0–24 months hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed RSV infection between January 2022 and December 2024, across four age groups (0–3, 4–6, 7–12, and 13–24 months).
Core methods
Clinical manifestations were compared across age groups using multivariable logistic regression, adjusting for sex, prematurity, viral co-infection, and feeding type. The Holm procedure was applied to account for multiple comparisons.
Main results
Fever increased significantly with age, from 48.8% among children aged 0–3 months to 81.7% among those aged 13–24 months (p < 0.001). Each additional month of age was associated with higher odds of fever (aOR 1.090, 95% CI 1.045–1.137; Holm-adjusted p < 0.001) and lower odds of thoracoabdominal asynchrony (aOR 0.951, 95% CI 0.917–0.986; Holm-adjusted p = 0.044). Infants aged 0–3 months required oxygen more frequently than older children (46.5% vs. 25.3%) and had significantly higher adjusted odds of oxygen requirement (aOR 2.54, 95% CI 1.26–5.0; p = 0.009).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights age-specific clinical assessment and the vulnerability of infants during the earliest months of life, with younger infants exhibiting more prominent signs of respiratory distress despite a lower frequency of fever, and a particularly high likelihood of requiring supplemental oxygen.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for RSV infection, emphasizing the importance of age-specific clinical assessment and reinforcing the vulnerability of infants during the earliest months of life.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, potential for selection bias, and the need for larger sample sizes to confirm findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

MUC5B 表达与 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染和肺部炎症减少相关

MUC5B Expression Is Associated with Reduced Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection and Pulmonary Inflammation
作者： Yufang Sun, Ying Xie, Lian Liu, Huijie Yang, Jiaojiao Nie, Wenwen Wang, Su Zhang, Yaru Quan, Changgui Li
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是严重下呼吸道疾病的主要原因,特别是在婴儿和老年人中。RSV 感染期间,凝胶形成粘蛋白的过度分泌和积聚会阻塞小气道并导致肺部病变。然而,气道粘蛋白也形成一线屏障,捕获吸入的病原体并限制其进入气道上皮。MUC5B(下呼吸道的一种主要分泌粘蛋白)是否影响 RSV 感染和相关的肺部反应仍知之甚少。在这里,我们使用稳定过表达 MUC5B 的人 A549 上皮细胞和雌性 Muc5b 缺陷小鼠研究了 MUC5B 在 RSV 感染过程中的作用。A2、Long 和 B1 毒株感染后,MUC5B 过表达显着降低了细胞内 RSV RNA 水平。在体内,Muc5b−/− 小鼠在感染后 5 天表现出明显更高的肺部 RSV RNA 负荷,而在鼻甲中没有检测到显着的基因型依赖性差异。Muc5b 缺乏还与 RSV 感染后更严重的呼吸道病理和更高比例的肺部中性粒细胞有关。野生型和 Muc5b−/− 小鼠感染后嗜酸性粒细胞比例均增加,而两种基因型的肺泡巨噬细胞比例均减少,间质巨噬细胞比例均增加。总的来说,这些发现表明,尽管粘液过多积聚已确定会导致气道阻塞,但 MUC5B 在 RSV 感染期间也具有保护作用。MUC5B 可能通过促进气道屏障功能、减少肺部病毒负荷、限制组织损伤和减轻中性粒细胞炎症来增强肺部对 RSV 的抵抗力。这些发现将 MUC5B 确定为粘膜 抗病毒药物 防御的重要组成部分,并拓宽了我们对气道粘蛋白如何影响 RSV 感染易感性的理解。

MUC5B Expression Is Associated with Reduced Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection and Pulmonary Inflammation

Authors: Yufang Sun, Ying Xie, Lian Liu, Huijie Yang, Jiaojiao Nie, Wenwen Wang, Su Zhang, Yaru Quan, Changgui Li
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract disease, particularly in infants and older adults. During RSV infection, excessive secretion and accumulation of gel-forming mucins can obstruct the small airways and contribute to pulmonary pathology. However, airway mucins also form a first-line barrier that traps inhaled pathogens and limits their access to the airway epithelium. Whether MUC5B, a major secreted mucin in the lower respiratory tract, influences RSV infection and associated pulmonary responses remains poorly understood. Here, we investigated the role of MUC5B during RSV infection using human A549 epithelial cells stably overexpressing MUC5B and female Muc5b-deficient mice. MUC5B overexpression significantly reduced intracellular RSV RNA levels following infection with the A2, Long, and B1 strains. In vivo, Muc5b−/− mice exhibited substantially higher pulmonary RSV RNA burdens at 5 days post-infection, whereas no significant genotype-dependent differences were detected in the nasal turbinates. Muc5b deficiency was also associated with more severe respiratory tract pathology and a higher proportion of pulmonary neutrophils after RSV infection. Eosinophil proportions increased after infection in both wild-type and Muc5b−/− mice, whereas the proportions of alveolar macrophages decreased and those of interstitial macrophages increased in both genotypes. Collectively, these findings suggest that, despite the established contribution of excessive mucus accumulation to airway obstruction, MUC5B also has a protective role during RSV infection. MUC5B may enhance pulmonary resistance to RSV by contributing to airway barrier function, reducing pulmonary viral burden, limiting tissue injury, and attenuating neutrophilic inflammation. These findings identify MUC5B as an important component of mucosal antiviral defense and broaden our understanding of how airway mucins influence susceptibility to RSV infection.

Limitations and evidence strength

Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of acute respiratory infection requiring hospitalization in children worldwide. Although no combination therapies exist for RSV, ribavirin (RBA) and TMC353121 (TMC) can efficiently combat RSV through different mechanisms. Question and background
The study aimed to explore an alternative therapy for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection by investigating the feasibility of formulating ribavirin and TMC353121 into an RBA-TMC self-assembled nanoparticle (RTSN) and assessing its antiviral activity.
Design and population
The study involved the formulation of RBA and TMC into RTSN and assessed its antiviral activity through in vitro and in vivo experiments, including a mouse model of RSV-induced pneumonia.
Core methods
The methods included the formulation of ribavirin and TMC353121 into self-assembled nanoparticles, testing their antiviral activity through in vitro assays, and evaluating their efficacy in a mouse model of RSV-induced pneumonia.
Main results
The results showed that RBA and TMC formed RTSN through hydrogen bonding and π-π stacking interactions. RTSN exhibited antiviral efficacy with a selectivity index of 43.8 and an effective concentration (EC50) of 10.9 μM.

Limitations and evidence strength

COVID-19 相关时期儿科 呼吸道合胞病毒 和腺病毒阳性的时间变化:一项为期六年的回顾性 队列研究 研究

Temporal changes in pediatric respiratory syncytial virus and adenovirus positivity across COVID-19-related periods: A six-year retrospective cohort study
作者： Yunus Nas
摘要中文翻译目的:描述在三个与COVID-19相关的时期中,对接受检测的儿童中呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)和腺病毒阳性率的时间变化。方法:本单中心回顾性队列研究研究纳入了8,253名0–18岁儿童,这些儿童在2018年1月至2023年6月期间因疑似急性呼吸道感染接受了RSV和腺病毒抗原的同步检测。时期划分为疫情前(2018年1月–2020年2月)、限制性疫情期间(2020年3月–2021年5月)和限制后(2021年6月–2023年6月)。比较各时期的阳性比例。使用疫情前时期作为参考,依据各时期计数计算未调整的比值比(OR)。结果:检测到RSV的儿童为266例(3.2%),腺病毒为612例(7.4%)。RSV阳性率在疫情前为1.5%(55/3,785),限制期间为1.2%(13/1,055),限制后为5.8%(198/3,413)。相对于疫情前时期,限制期间RSV阳性率的OR为0.85(95% CI 0.46–1.55),无显著差异;限制后OR为4.18(95% CI 3.09–5.65),显著升高。腺病毒阳性率分别为8.8%、10.2%和5.0%;对应的OR分别为1.18(95% CI 0.94–1.48)和0.54(95% CI 0.45–0.66)。结论:在临床选择的检测儿童中,RSV和腺病毒阳性率呈现不同的时间模式。这些未调整的关联不应解释为特定公共卫生措施的因果效应,因为季节性、检测实践和就医行为可能在各时期存在差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查与 COVID-19 大流行相关的三个不同时期儿童中 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和腺病毒的阳性率如何随时间变化,从而满足了解公共卫生措施对这些病毒感染的影响的需要。
设计与对象
单中心回顾性队列研究研究分三个时期进行:大流行前(2018年1月至2020年2月)、限制性大流行(2020年3月至2021年5月)和限制后(2021年6月至2023年6月)。其中包括 8,253 名 0-18 岁儿童,他们因疑似急性呼吸道感染而同时接受了 RSV 和腺病毒抗原检测。
核心方法
未调整的比值比 (OR) 是根据特定时期的计数计算的,以大流行前时期为参考。按时期比较阳性比例,以大流行前时期作为分析基线。
主要结果
检测到RSV的儿童有266名(3.2%),腺病毒612名(7.4%)。疫情前时期RSV阳性率为1.5%(55/3,785),疫情限制期为1.2%(13/1,055),疫情后解除限制期为5.8%(198/3,413)。腺病毒阳性率分别为8.8%、10.2%和5.0%,相应的OR分别为1.18(95% CI 0.94-1.48)和0.54(95% CI 0.45-0.66)相对于疫情前时期。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RSV 和腺病毒阳性率遵循不同的时间模式,与大流行前相比,RSV 阳性率在限制后显着增加,而腺病毒阳性率则有所下降。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,公共卫生措施和就医行为的变化可能影响了 RSV 阳性率,这可以为未来的 监测 和儿科呼吸道感染干预策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于未经调整的比值比和潜在的混杂因素,例如季节性和测试实践。证据是中等的,因为它基于单中心回顾性分析,没有控制这些变量。

Temporal changes in pediatric respiratory syncytial virus and adenovirus positivity across COVID-19-related periods: A six-year retrospective cohort study

Authors: Yunus Nas
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To describe temporal changes in respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and adenovirus positivity among children tested across three COVID-19-related periods.Methods: This single-center retrospective cohort study included 8,253 children aged 0–18 years who underwent concurrent RSV and adenovirus antigen testing for suspected acute respiratory infection from January 2018 through June 2023. Periods were defined as pre-pandemic (January 2018–February 2020), restrictive pandemic (March 2020–May 2021), and post-restriction (June 2021–June 2023). Positivity proportions were compared by period. Unadjusted odds ratios (ORs) were calculated from period-specific counts using the pre-pandemic period as reference.Results: RSV was detected in 266 children (3.2%) and adenovirus in 612 (7.4%). RSV positivity was 1.5% (55/3,785) pre-pandemic, 1.2% (13/1,055) during restrictions, and 5.8% (198/3,413) post-restriction. Relative to the pre-pandemic period, the odds of RSV positivity did not differ during restrictions (OR 0.85, 95% CI 0.46–1.55) but were higher post-restriction (OR 4.18, 95% CI 3.09–5.65). Adenovirus positivity was 8.8%, 10.2%, and 5.0%, respectively; corresponding ORs were 1.18 (95% CI 0.94–1.48) and 0.54 (95% CI 0.45–0.66).Conclusion: Among clinically selected children who were tested, RSV and adenovirus positivity followed different temporal patterns. These unadjusted associations should not be interpreted as causal effects of specific public-health measures because seasonality, testing practices, and healthcare-seeking behavior may have differed across periods.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how the positivity rates of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and adenovirus changed over time among children during three distinct periods related to the COVID-19 pandemic, addressing the need to understand the impact of public health measures on these viral infections.
Design and population
A single-center retrospective cohort study was conducted across three periods: pre-pandemic (January 2018–February 2020), restrictive pandemic (March 2020–May 2021), and post-restriction (June 2021–June 2023). It included 8,253 children aged 0–18 years who underwent concurrent RSV and adenovirus antigen testing for suspected acute respiratory infection.
Core methods
Unadjusted odds ratios (ORs) were calculated from period-specific counts using the pre-pandemic period as reference. Positivity proportions were compared by period, with the pre-pandemic period serving as the baseline for analysis.
Main results
RSV was detected in 266 children (3.2%) and adenovirus in 612 (7.4%). RSV positivity was 1.5% (55/3,785) pre-pandemic, 1.2% (13/1,055) during restrictions, and 5.8% (198/3,413) post-restriction. Adenovirus positivity was 8.8%, 10.2%, and 5.0%, respectively, with corresponding ORs of 1.18 (95% CI 0.94–1.48) and 0.54 (95% CI 0.45–0.66) relative to the pre-pandemic period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that RSV and adenovirus positivity followed different temporal patterns, with RSV positivity increasing significantly post-restriction compared to the pre-pandemic period, while adenovirus positivity showed a decrease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that changes in public health measures and healthcare-seeking behavior may have influenced RSV positivity rates, which could inform future surveillance and intervention strategies for pediatric respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on unadjusted odds ratios and potential confounding factors such as seasonality and testing practices. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single-center retrospective analysis without controlling for these variables.

预防婴儿和儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 疾病的建议:技术报告

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Technical Report
作者： James D Campbell, Pia S Pannaraj, Monica I Ardura, Kristina A Bryant, Eric J Chow, Roberta L DeBiasi, Claudia Espinosa, Robert W Frenck, C. Mary Healy, Betsy C Herold
摘要中文翻译本技术报告附有美国儿科学会 (AAP) 政策声明 (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079047) 以及 2026-2027 年 RSV 季节期间 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫的建议。提出了建议对在第一个 RSV 季节期间或进入其第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的所有 8 个月以下婴儿进行免疫接种的理由,除非婴儿有记录表明怀孕父母对 疫苗接种 具有保护作用;8 至 19 个月大的婴儿和儿童,他们是严重 RSV 疾病的高危人群,并已进入第二个 RSV 季节。该报告综合了儿科人群中 RSV 流行病学、疾病负担、免疫有效性、安全性和成本效益的当前证据;包括支持在第二季管理中扩大高风险群体的证据。此外,该报告还概述了可用的 RSV 免疫产品、配方以及与其他免疫的联合给药。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本报告提出了关于预防婴儿和儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疾病的更新建议的需求,重点关注 2026-2027 年 RSV 季节期间的免疫策略。这些建议的基本原理是基于当前对 RSV 流行病学 和疾病负担的理解。
设计与对象
该研究是一份技术报告,综合了儿科人群 RSV 免疫有效性、安全性和成本效益的现有证据。其中包括针对 8 个月以下婴儿以及 8 至 19 个月高危婴儿和儿童的建议。
核心方法
该报告汇编并评估了有关 RSV 流行病学、疾病负担、免疫效果、安全性和成本效益的当前证据。它还概述了可用的 RSV 免疫产品及其配方。
主要结果
该报告概述了建议对在第一个 RSV 季节期间或进入其第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的所有 8 个月以下婴儿进行免疫接种的理由,除非婴儿有记录表明怀孕父母对 疫苗接种 具有保护作用;8 至 19 个月大的婴儿和儿童,他们是严重 RSV 疾病的高危人群,并已进入第二个 RSV 季节。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持扩大第二季 RSV 免疫接种的高危人群。新颖之处在于根据最新证据更新的免疫接种建议。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 RSV 免疫的公共卫生策略提供信息,特别是在识别高危人群和优化婴儿和儿童的 疫苗 给药方案方面。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于对当前文献的综合,报告不包含新数据。证据的强度中等,因为它依赖于现有研究而不是原始研究。

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Technical Report

Authors: James D Campbell, Pia S Pannaraj, Monica I Ardura, Kristina A Bryant, Eric J Chow, Roberta L DeBiasi, Claudia Espinosa, Robert W Frenck, C. Mary Healy, Betsy C Herold
Original abstract (English)
This technical report accompanies the American Academy of Pediatrics (AAP) policy statement (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079047) and recommendations for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization during the 2026–2027 RSV season. The rationale is presented for recommending immunization of all infants younger than 8 months born during or entering their first RSV season, unless the infant has documented protection from vaccination of the pregnant parent; and infants and children 8 through 19 months of age who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season. The report synthesizes current evidence on RSV epidemiology, disease burden, immunization effectiveness, safety, and cost-effectiveness in pediatric populations; including evidence supporting expansion of high-risk groups for second season administration. Additionally, the report provides an overview of available RSV immunization products, formulations, and coadministration with other immunizations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This report addresses the need for updated recommendations on preventing respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease in infants and children, focusing on immunization strategies during the 2026–2027 RSV season. The rationale for these recommendations is grounded in the current understanding of RSV epidemiology and disease burden.
Design and population
The study is a technical report that synthesizes existing evidence on RSV immunization effectiveness, safety, and cost-effectiveness in pediatric populations. It includes recommendations for infants younger than 8 months and high-risk infants and children aged 8 through 19 months.
Core methods
The report compiles and evaluates current evidence on RSV epidemiology, disease burden, immunization effectiveness, safety, and cost-effectiveness. It also provides an overview of available RSV immunization products and their formulations.
Main results
The report outlines the rationale for recommending immunization of all infants younger than 8 months born during or entering their first RSV season, unless the infant has documented protection from vaccination of the pregnant parent; and infants and children 8 through 19 months of age who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as supporting the expansion of high-risk groups for second season RSV immunization. The novelty lies in the updated recommendations for immunization based on the latest evidence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for RSV immunization, particularly in identifying high-risk groups and optimizing vaccine administration schedules for infants and children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a synthesis of current literature, and the report does not include new data. The strength of the evidence is moderate, as it relies on existing studies rather than original research.

HRSV分子流行病学 监测中HVR2-G的应用:WGS应用的补充

Using HVR2-G in molecular epidemiology surveillance of HRSV: a supplement for the application of WGS
作者： Jinhua Song, Zhen Zhu, Huiling Wang, Hai Li, Baicheng Xia, Junrui Chen, Liwei Sun, Yonghao Guo, Shu Liang, Xiaoling Tian

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 HVR2-G 测序窗口对于谱系分类的适用性,并将以前的 HVR2-G 基因型与新的 WGS 定义的谱系命名法相整合。人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (HRSV) 是引起婴儿、幼儿和老年人急性下呼吸道感染的主要病原体。
设计与对象
该研究是使用 GenBank 的全 基因组 序列 (WGS) 数据和 Nextstrain 的谱系命名数据进行的。从这些序列中提取 HVR2-G 区域以评估其在谱系分类中的效用。
核心方法
HVR2-G 区域从 GenBank 的 WGS 序列中提取,谱系命名数据从 Nextstrain 下载。分别使用 IQ-TREE 2.3.6 和 MEGA11.0 进行 HVR2-G 序列的系统发育和核苷酸成对距离(p 距离)分析。
主要结果
系统发育分析表明,WGS定义的谱系命名法定义的主要谱系可以使用HVR2-G区域进行分类,自举率超过70%;无法区分嵌套的谱系。HRSVA 和 B 的 19 个和 37 个先前 HVR2-G 基因型分别减少为 4 个(A.1、A.2、A.3 和 A.D)和 5 个(B.1、B.2、B.3、B.4 和 B.D)谱系,P 距离阈值分别大于 0.102 和 0.0591。
解释与创新
该研究证实,HVR2-G 测序窗口提供了主要 HRSV 谱系的可靠分配,并能够与历史 监测 数据集集成。该方法有可能成为中国和全球HRSV分子病毒学监测全基因组测序(WGS)应用的补充,特别是在资源有限的地区。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HVR2-G 测序可用于增强 HRSV 的分子 流行病学 监测,特别是在 WGS 可能不可行的领域。这种集成允许将历史数据与现代测序技术结合使用。
局限与证据强度
该研究并未解决 HVR2-G 测序窗口对其他区域或人群的推广的潜在限制。证据是温和的,因为它是基于对现有序列数据的分析而不是新的实验结果。

Using HVR2-G in molecular epidemiology surveillance of HRSV: a supplement for the application of WGS

Authors: Jinhua Song, Zhen Zhu, Huiling Wang, Hai Li, Baicheng Xia, Junrui Chen, Liwei Sun, Yonghao Guo, Shu Liang, Xiaoling Tian
Original abstract (English)
Methods The HVR2-G region was extracted from WGS sequences from GenBank and the lineage nomenclature data were downloaded from Nextstrain. Results Phylogenetic analyses revealed that the primary lineages defined by WGS-defined lineage nomenclature could be classified using the HVR2-G region with a bootstrap over 70%; the nested lineages could not be distinguished. Conclusion This study verified that the HVR2-G sequencing window provides reliable assignment of major HRSV lineages and enables integration with historical surveillance datasets. 12879_2026_14356_MOESM1_ESM.xlsx (download XLSX ) Supplementary Material 1: Supplementary Table 1: Sequences and information used in this study. Supplementary Table 4: Sequences from China downloaded from the GenBank and used in this study. Abstract Background Human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) is among the major pathogens causing acute lower respiratory tract infections in infants, young children and older adults. The second hypervariable region near the C-terminus of the G gene (HVR2-G) is the target for genotyping that has been used in molecular epidemiology surveillance for nearly 28 years, and a large amount of virological data has been accumulated. Whole-genome sequence (WGS) has been suggested for use in classifying lineages because it has provided the highest resolution since 2020. As such, evaluating the applicability of the HVR2-G sequencing window for lineage classification and integrating previous HVR2-G genotypes with the new WGS-defined lineage nomenclature are needed. Phylogenetic and nucleotide pairwise distance (p-distance) analyses of HVR2-G sequences were conducted using IQ-TREE 2.3.6 and MEGA11.0, respectively. Nineteen and 37 previous HVR2-G genotypes of HRSVA and B were reduced to 4 (A.1, A.2, A.3 and A.D...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the applicability of the HVR2-G sequencing window for lineage classification and integrate previous HVR2-G genotypes with the new WGS-defined lineage nomenclature. Human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) is a major pathogen causing acute lower respiratory tract infections in infants, young children and older adults.
Design and population
The study was conducted using whole-genome sequence (WGS) data from GenBank and lineage nomenclature data from Nextstrain. The HVR2-G region was extracted from these sequences to assess its utility in lineage classification.
Core methods
The HVR2-G region was extracted from WGS sequences from GenBank and the lineage nomenclature data were downloaded from Nextstrain. Phylogenetic and nucleotide pairwise distance (p-distance) analyses of HVR2-G sequences were conducted using IQ-TREE 2.3.6 and MEGA11.0, respectively.
Main results
Phylogenetic analyses revealed that the primary lineages defined by WGS-defined lineage nomenclature could be classified using the HVR2-G region with a bootstrap over 70%; the nested lineages could not be distinguished. Nineteen and 37 previous HVR2-G genotypes of HRSVA and B were reduced to 4 (A.1, A.2, A.3 and A.D) and 5 (B.1, B.2, B.3, B.4 and B.D) lineages with P-distance thresholds greater than 0.102 and 0.0591, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study confirmed that the HVR2-G sequencing window provides reliable assignment of major HRSV lineages and enables integration with historical surveillance datasets. This approach has the potential to serve as a complement to the application of WGS for molecular virological surveillance of HRSV in China and globally, especially in resource-limited areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HVR2-G sequencing can be used to enhance molecular epidemiology surveillance of HRSV, particularly in areas where WGS may not be feasible. This integration allows for the use of historical data in conjunction with modern sequencing techniques.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not address potential limitations in the generalizability of the HVR2-G sequencing window to other regions or populations. The evidence is moderate as it is based on analysis of existing sequence data rather than new experimental findings.
