通用 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防对新生儿 毛细支气管炎 住院治疗和疾病严重程度的影响:一项真实世界单中心研究

Impact of Universal Nirsevimab Immunoprophylaxis on Bronchiolitis Hospitalizations and Disease Severity in Neonates: A Real-World Single-Centre Study
作者： Domenico Umberto De Rose, Livia Antilici, Alessandra Salvatori, Anna Chiara Vittucci, Velia Chiara Di Maio, Anna Maria Musolino, Venere Cortazzo, Simona Lozzi, Maria Paola Ronchetti, Annabella Braguglia
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估通用 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防对新生儿 毛细支气管炎 住院治疗和疾病严重程度的影响,解决评估这种预防方法在减少 RSV 相关住院治疗和严重程度方面的有效性的需要。
设计与对象
在意大利罗马的一家三级儿科中心进行了一项回顾性观察性研究,比较了连续四个 RSV 季节:普遍预防(2022-2024 年)与普遍预防(2024-2026 年)。
核心方法
该研究包括因急性 毛细支气管炎 住院的足月儿和早产儿 ≤ 30 天以及足月校正年龄以下,入院 72 小时内通过病毒检测证实 RSV 状态。
主要结果
Total bronchiolitis admissions dropped by 52% (256 to 122), with RSV-positive cases decreasing from 66.8% to 43.4% (RR 0.65, 95% CI 0.52–0.81; p < 0.001). Monthly peak RSV admissions fell progressively, and ICU rates dropped from 18.4% to 4.1% (RR 0.22, 95% CI 0.09–0.55; p < 0.001). Among RSV-positive neonates, respiratory support requirements and ICU admissions also decreased significantly.
解释与创新
普遍的 尼塞韦单抗 预防与新生儿 毛细支气管炎 住院率、RSV 阳性率、呼吸支持需求和 ICU 入院率的显着减少相关,凸显了在季节性 RSV 高峰之前早期、高覆盖率实施的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,普遍的 尼塞韦单抗 预防可以显着减轻新生儿 RSV 相关 毛细支气管炎 的负担,可能改善临床结果并减少住院治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单中心设计的限制,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,两个季节的高覆盖率和一致的结果支持了研究结果的稳健性。

Impact of Universal Nirsevimab Immunoprophylaxis on Bronchiolitis Hospitalizations and Disease Severity in Neonates: A Real-World Single-Centre Study

Authors: Domenico Umberto De Rose, Livia Antilici, Alessandra Salvatori, Anna Chiara Vittucci, Velia Chiara Di Maio, Anna Maria Musolino, Venere Cortazzo, Simona Lozzi, Maria Paola Ronchetti, Annabella Braguglia
Original abstract (English)
(1) Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of neonatal lower respiratory tract infection. We described changes in neonatal bronchiolitis hospitalizations, RSV detection and disease severity across the transition from two seasons without systematic RSV prophylaxis to two seasons following the introduction of universal nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis (Lazio region, December 2024) in a tertiary pediatric center. (2) Methods: A retrospective, observational study was conducted at “Bambino Gesù” Children’s Hospital IRCCS (Rome, Italy) comparing four consecutive RSV seasons: pre-universal prophylaxis (2022–2024, n = 256) versus post-universal prophylaxis (2024–2026, n = 122). Term infants ≤30 days and preterm infants under term-corrected age hospitalized for acute bronchiolitis were included. RSV status was confirmed by viral testing within 72 h of admission. (3) Results: Total bronchiolitis admissions dropped by 52% (256 to 122), with RSV-positive cases decreasing from 66.8% to 43.4% (RR 0.65, 95% CI 0.52–0.81; p < 0.001). Monthly peak RSV admissions fell progressively from 56 (December 2022) to 6 (January 2026). Among all admissions, ICU rates dropped from 18.4% to 4.1% (RR 0.22, 95% CI 0.09–0.55; p < 0.001). Within RSV-positive neonates, respiratory support requirements decreased from 93.0% to 71.7% (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.65–0.92; p < 0.001), and ICU admissions declined from 24.0% to 5.7% (RR 0.24, 95% CI 0.08–0.73; p = 0.003). Non-RSV pathogens dominated the post-campaign viral profile, with the relative prevalence of human metapneumovirus (hMPV) increasing from 3.9% to 11.5% (RR 2.94, 95% CI 1.34–6.42; p = 0.006) without increasing overall severity. In the post-campaign period, 22 of 53 RSV-positive admissions occurred in nirsevimab-immunized neonates (breakthrough infections): 7/35 in 2024–25 and 15/18 in 2025–26 (p < 0.001), reflecting higher population coverage. (4) Conclusions: Universal nirsevimab prophylaxis was associated with marked reductions in neonatal bronchiolitis hospitalizations, RSV positivity, respiratory support needs, and ICU admissions. Early, high-coverage implementation prior to seasonal RSV peaks maximizes population-level protection in highly vulnerable newborns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of universal nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis on bronchiolitis hospitalizations and disease severity in neonates, addressing the need to assess the effectiveness of this prophylaxis in reducing RSV-related hospitalizations and severity.
Design and population
A retrospective, observational study was conducted at a tertiary pediatric center in Rome, Italy, comparing four consecutive RSV seasons: pre-universal prophylaxis (2022–2024) with post-universal prophylaxis (2024–2026).
Core methods
The study included term and preterm infants ≤30 days and under term-corrected age hospitalized for acute bronchiolitis, with RSV status confirmed by viral testing within 72 hours of admission.
Main results
Total bronchiolitis admissions dropped by 52% (256 to 122), with RSV-positive cases decreasing from 66.8% to 43.4% (RR 0.65, 95% CI 0.52–0.81; p < 0.001). Monthly peak RSV admissions fell progressively, and ICU rates dropped from 18.4% to 4.1% (RR 0.22, 95% CI 0.09–0.55; p < 0.001). Among RSV-positive neonates, respiratory support requirements and ICU admissions also decreased significantly.
Interpretation and novelty
Universal nirsevimab prophylaxis was associated with marked reductions in neonatal bronchiolitis hospitalizations, RSV positivity, respiratory support needs, and ICU admissions, highlighting the effectiveness of early, high-coverage implementation prior to seasonal RSV peaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that universal nirsevimab prophylaxis can significantly reduce the burden of RSV-related bronchiolitis in neonates, potentially leading to improved clinical outcomes and reduced hospitalizations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design, which may affect generalizability. However, the high coverage and consistent results across two seasons support the robustness of the findings.

基于医院的 疫苗 儿童可预防疾病的 监测:荷兰三级医院的回顾性研究(2024-2025 年)。

Hospital-based surveillance of vaccine-preventable diseases in children: a retrospective study in a Dutch tertiary hospital (2024-2025).
作者： Susanne Heemskerk, Veerle Roels, Jojanneke van Summeren, Louis J. Bont, Joanne G Wildenbeest
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管制定了免疫计划,但 疫苗 可预防疾病 (VPD) 仍然是儿童发病的主要原因。VPD 的 监测 对于公共卫生战略和加强国家免疫规划至关重要。这项试点研究评估了使用常规收集的医院数据进行儿科 VPD 住院 监测 的可行性。方法:我们于2024年7月至2025年6月全国实施RSV免疫之前在荷兰一家三级医院进行了回顾性队列研究研究。无论是否纳入国家免疫计划,均包括因具有可用许可的 疫苗 病原体引起的实验室确诊感染的 18 岁以下儿童。根据案例确定、关键变量的完整性和 疫苗接种 信息的可用性来评估可行性。结果:我们纳入了 99 名儿童(103 次 VPD 发作;中位年龄 0.6 岁),其中 60% (n = 59) 需要儿科重症监护。大多数住院治疗是由呼吸道感染引起的,特别是呼吸道合胞病毒 (n = 52) 和流感 (n = 27)。病例确定需要整合多个数据源,因为 45% 的儿童在研究医院外接受了检测。临床变量基本完整,并且可以确定 84% 的发作的 疫苗接种 状态。总体而言,9% (n = 9) 的 VPD 发作发生在完全接种疫苗的儿童中。大多数 VPD 发作(76%;n = 78)发生在未接种特定病原体疫苗的儿童中,主要是因为他们太小,或者因为国家免疫计划或现行指南未涵盖病原体或血清型。结论:基于医院的儿童 VPD 回顾性 监测 是可行的,并为指导优化国家免疫规划工作提供了关键信息。通过捕获疾病严重程度和 疫苗 可预防性,它补充了荷兰现有的 监测 数据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估使用常规收集的医院数据进行儿科 疫苗 可预防疾病 (VPD) 住院 疫苗 的可行性,满足为公共卫生战略提供信息和加强国家免疫规划的需要。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究于 2024 年 7 月至 2025 年 6 月在荷兰一家三级医院进行,重点关注由可获得许可的 疫苗 病原体引起的实验室确诊感染的 18 岁以下儿童,无论其是否纳入国家免疫计划。
核心方法
根据案例确定、关键变量的完整性以及 疫苗接种 信息的可用性来评估可行性,需要集成多个数据源。
主要结果
该研究包括 99 名儿童(103 次 VPD 发作;中位年龄 0.6 岁),其中 60% (n = 59) 需要儿科重症监护。大多数住院治疗是由呼吸道感染引起的,特别是呼吸道合胞病毒 (n = 52) 和流感 (n = 27)。
解释与创新
基于医院的儿科 VPD 回顾性 监测 是可行的,并提供了指导优化国家免疫规划工作的关键信息,补充了荷兰现有的 监测 数据。
科研与公卫意义
通过捕获疾病的严重程度和 疫苗 的可预防性,该研究强调了整合 疫苗 医院数据的重要性,这可以为公共卫生策略提供信息并改善免疫计划。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括需要整合多个来源的数据,以及 9% 的 VPD 发作发生在完全接种疫苗的儿童中,这表明 疫苗 覆盖范围或有效性存在潜在差距。

Hospital-based surveillance of vaccine-preventable diseases in children: a retrospective study in a Dutch tertiary hospital (2024-2025).

Authors: Susanne Heemskerk, Veerle Roels, Jojanneke van Summeren, Louis J. Bont, Joanne G Wildenbeest
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Vaccine-preventable diseases (VPDs) are a major cause of morbidity in children, despite established immunization programs. Surveillance of VPDs is essential to inform public health strategies and strengthen national immunization programs. This pilot study evaluated the feasibility of using routinely collected hospital data for in-hospital surveillance of pediatric VPDs. METHODS: We performed a retrospective cohort study at a tertiary hospital in the Netherlands from July 2024 to June 2025, before national implementation of RSV immunization. Children <18 years admitted with laboratory-confirmed infections caused by pathogens with available licensed vaccines were included, regardless of inclusion in the national immunization program. Feasibility was assessed based on case ascertainment, completeness of key variables and availability of vaccination information. RESULTS: We included 99 children (103 VPD episodes; median age 0.6 years), of whom 60% (n = 59) required pediatric intensive care. Most hospitalizations were caused by respiratory infections, particularly RSV (n = 52) and influenza (n = 27). Case ascertainment required integration of multiple data sources, as 45% of children were tested outside the study hospital. Clinical variables were largely complete and vaccination status could be determined for 84% of episodes. Overall, 9% (n = 9) of VPD episodes occurred in fully vaccinated children. Most VPD episodes (76%; n = 78) occurred in children not vaccinated against the specific pathogen, mainly because they were too young or because the pathogen or serotype was not covered by the national immunization program or current guidelines. CONCLUSIONS: Retrospective hospital-based surveillance of pediatric VPDs is feasible and provides key information to guide efforts to optimize the national immunization program. By capturing disease severity and vaccine preventability, it complements existing surveillance data in the Netherlands.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the feasibility of using routinely collected hospital data for in-hospital surveillance of pediatric vaccine-preventable diseases (VPDs), addressing the need to inform public health strategies and strengthen national immunization programs.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted at a tertiary hospital in the Netherlands from July 2024 to June 2025, focusing on children <18 years old with laboratory-confirmed infections caused by pathogens with available licensed vaccines, regardless of their inclusion in the national immunization program.
Core methods
Feasibility was assessed based on case ascertainment, completeness of key variables, and availability of vaccination information, requiring integration of multiple data sources.
Main results
The study included 99 children (103 VPD episodes; median age 0.6 years), with 60% (n = 59) requiring pediatric intensive care. Most hospitalizations were caused by respiratory infections, particularly RSV (n = 52) and influenza (n = 27).
Interpretation and novelty
Retrospective hospital-based surveillance of pediatric VPDs is feasible and provides key information to guide efforts to optimize the national immunization program, complementing existing surveillance data in the Netherlands.
Scientific and public-health significance
By capturing disease severity and vaccine preventability, the study highlights the importance of integrating hospital data for surveillance, which can inform public health strategies and improve immunization programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the need for data integration from multiple sources and the fact that 9% of VPD episodes occurred in fully vaccinated children, suggesting potential gaps in vaccine coverage or effectiveness.

迈瑞MN2880系统检测呼吸道病毒核酸的综合性能验证。

Comprehensive Performance Verification of the Mindray MN2880 System in Detecting Respiratory Virus Nucleic Acids.
作者： Jin-Liang Chen, Lian-Ying Zeng, Hao Huang, Pei-Song Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的验证迈瑞 MN2880 系统同时检测 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感病毒(IAV)、乙型流感病毒(IBV)和 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)核酸的临床性能。方法 根据 CNAS-GL039:2019 和 CNAS-GL037:2019 分子诊断验证要求,我们对迈瑞 MN2880 多重呼吸道病毒检测的七个关键性能参数进行了全面评估:临床一致性、检测限 (LoD)、重复性、精密度、交叉反应/抗干扰性、日常质量控制和污染控制。结果迈瑞 MN2880 系统与参考方法表现出高度的诊断一致性,SARS-CoV-2 的总一致性百分比为 99.51%(95% CI:99.00%–99.76%),IAV 的总一致性百分比为 99.93%(95% CI:99.60%–99.99%),IBV 和 RSV 的总一致性百分比均为 100%,并达到了临床验证的极限检测范围(LoD:SARS-CoV-2 为 100 拷贝/mL,IAV/IBV/RSV 为 200 拷贝/mL)。通过阴性/低/中/高浓度组的 100% 检测来确认分析精度,重复性(Ct 值)的 CV ≤ 3%,批间精度的 CV ≤ 5%。该检测对 6 种临床相关病原体表现出 100% 特异性,包括人鼻病毒、副流感病毒、腺病毒、肺炎支原体、流感嗜血杆菌和金黄色葡萄球菌,未观察到交叉反应。此外,MN2880 系统表现出强大的抗干扰性(100% 检测出掺有 7 种干扰物的弱阳性样品)、稳定的日常 QC 性能(115 次连续运行中 100% 有效对照)以及 100% 污染预防。结论 MN2880 系统满足呼吸道病毒多重检测诊断用途所需的关键分析基准,其性能已根据 CNAS-GL039:2019 和 CNAS-GL037:2019 标准进行了验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
验证迈瑞 MN2880 系统同时检测 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感病毒(IAV)、乙型流感病毒(IBV)和 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)核酸的临床性能。
设计与对象
对迈瑞 MN2880 多重呼吸道病毒检测的七个关键性能参数进行综合评估:临床一致性、检测限 (LoD)、重复性、精密度、交叉反应性/抗干扰性、日常质量控制和污染控制。
核心方法
根据CNAS-GL039:2019和CNAS-GL037:2019要求进行评估,包括检测SARS-CoV-2、IAV、IBV和RSV核酸,具有较高的诊断一致性、经过临床验证的检测限和强大的抗干扰能力。
主要结果
与参考方法诊断高度一致,经临床验证的检测限(LoD:SARS-CoV-2 为 100 拷贝/mL,IAV/IBV/RSV 为 200 拷贝/mL)、100% 精确度,且未观察到交叉反应。
解释与创新
MN2880 系统满足呼吸道病毒多重检测中诊断用途所需的关键分析基准,其性能已根据 CNAS-GL039:2019 和 CNAS-GL037:2019 标准进行了验证。
科研与公卫意义
该系统可以提高呼吸道病毒检测的准确性和可靠性,及时辅助诊断和治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告任何重大局限性,而且证据有力。

Comprehensive Performance Verification of the Mindray MN2880 System in Detecting Respiratory Virus Nucleic Acids.

Authors: Jin-Liang Chen, Lian-Ying Zeng, Hao Huang, Pei-Song Chen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Objective To verify the clinical performance of the Mindray MN2880 system for simultaneous detection of SARS‐CoV‐2, influenza A virus (IAV), influenza B virus (IBV), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) nucleic acids. Methods According to the CNAS‐GL039:2019 and CNAS‐GL037:2019 requirements for molecular diagnostic verification, we comprehensively evaluated the Mindray MN2880 multiplex respiratory virus assay across seven critical performance parameters: clinical concordance, limit of detection (LoD), repeatability, precision, cross‐reactivity/interference resistance, daily QC, and contamination control. Results The Mindray MN2880 system demonstrated high diagnostic concordance with the reference method, with total percent agreements of 99.51% (95% CI: 99.00%–99.76%) for SARS‐CoV‐2, 99.93% (95% CI: 99.60%–99.99%) for IAV, and 100% for both IBV and RSV, and achieved clinically verified limits of detection (LoD: SARS‐CoV‐2 at 100 copies/mL, IAV/IBV/RSV at 200 copies/mL). Analytical precision was confirmed through 100% detection across negative/low/medium/high concentration panels with ≤ 3% CV for repeatability (Ct values) and ≤ 5% CV for inter‐run precision. The assay exhibited 100% specificity against 6 clinically relevant pathogens, including Human Rhinovirus, parainfluenza virus, adenovirus, Mycoplasma pneumoniae , Haemophilus influenzae , and Staphylococcus aureus , with no observed cross‐reactivity. Moreover, the MN2880 system demonstrated robust interference resistance (100% detection of weakly‐positive samples spiked with seven interferents), stable daily QC performance (100% valid controls across 115 consecutive runs), and 100% contamination prevention. Conclusion The MN2880 system satisfies key analytical benchmarks required for diagnostic use in respiratory virus multiplex testing, with performance verified against CNAS‐GL039:2019 and CNAS‐GL037:2019 standards.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To verify the clinical performance of the Mindray MN2880 system for simultaneous detection of SARS‐CoV‐2, influenza A virus (IAV), influenza B virus (IBV), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) nucleic acids.
Design and population
Comprehensive evaluation of the Mindray MN2880 multiplex respiratory virus assay across seven critical performance parameters: clinical concordance, limit of detection (LoD), repeatability, precision, cross‐reactivity/interference resistance, daily QC, and contamination control.
Core methods
Evaluation according to CNAS‐GL039:2019 and CNAS‐GL037:2019 requirements, including detection of SARS‐CoV‐2, IAV, IBV, and RSV nucleic acids with high diagnostic concordance, clinically verified limits of detection, and robust interference resistance.
Main results
High diagnostic concordance with reference method, clinically verified limits of detection (LoD: SARS‐CoV‐2 at 100 copies/mL, IAV/IBV/RSV at 200 copies/mL), 100% precision, and no observed cross‐reactivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The MN2880 system satisfies key analytical benchmarks required for diagnostic use in respiratory virus multiplex testing, with performance verified against CNAS‐GL039:2019 and CNAS‐GL037:2019 standards.
Scientific and public-health significance
The system can improve the accuracy and reliability of respiratory virus detection, aiding in timely diagnosis and treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report any significant limitations, and the evidence is strong.

中国慢性阻塞性肺疾病诊断与治疗指南(2026年修订)

[Chinese guidelines for the diagnosis and management of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (2026 revision)].
作者： Chinese Thoracic Society, Chinese Thoracic Society, Chinese Medical Association, Chinese Association of Chest Physicians.
摘要中文翻译中国慢性阻塞性肺疾病诊断及治疗指南(2026年修订)由中华医学会胸科学会慢性阻塞性肺病学组和中华医学会胸科医师分会慢性阻塞性肺病学组制定。此次修订在2021年版的基础上,结合了过去五年发表的证据和中国慢阻肺预防和管理的经验,对疾病定义、病因和发病机制、病例发现、诊断和综合评估、慢阻肺稳定管理、急性加重的诊断和诊断、合并症管理以及未来的研究方向进行了全面更新。该指南主要采用建议分级评估、制定和评估(GRADE)方法,并辅以牛津循证医学中心的证据水平,评估证据的确定性并制定建议。它为 诊断 和 COPD 的 22 个临床问题的管理提供了实用建议。旨在促进COPD的早期识别、标准化诊断和诊断以及综合管理,从而提高我国COPD的规范化护理水平,减轻COPD的疾病负担。22个关键临床问题和相应建议总结如下。问题1:鉴于目前COPD诊断不足的情况,哪些策略可以改善早期诊断?建议1:我们建议积极和机会性病例发现,以识别高危人群COPD 并促进早期 诊断。(1B/强推荐)问题2:慢阻肺患者选择初始药物治疗时应评估哪些参数?建议2:慢阻肺患者应根据症状负担和加重风险进行个体化评估,分为A、B、E组。其他评估还应包括肺功能检测中气流阻塞的严重程度、合并症以及外周血嗜酸性粒细胞计数等生物标志物。(2B/强推荐)问题3:ABE分类中应如何使用mMRC和CAT评分进行症状评估?建议3:应使用改良医学研究委员会(mMRC)呼吸困难量表和/或COPD评估测试(CAT)来评估COPD症状。两种仪器的结果可能不一致:mMRC 量表侧重于呼吸困难,而 CAT 更适合对多种症状进行综合评估。(2B/强推荐)问题4:慢性阻塞性肺疾病(COPD)的初步评估应包括胸部CT吗?建议4:慢性阻塞性肺病(COPD)的初步评估应包括胸部影像学,首选胸部计算机断层扫描(CT)。(2B/有条件推荐)问题5:针对危险因素暴露的关键预防措施有哪些?建议5:应积极向所有COPD患者提供戒烟干预措施。建议使用清洁燃料、有效通风、无污染炉灶和类似措施,以减少有害环境暴露;还应加强职业防护,避免持续接触潜在刺激物。(1A/强推荐)问题6:COPD患者应该如何推荐疫苗接种?建议6:推荐每年流感疫苗接种。还推荐常规肺炎球菌 疫苗接种 与 PPSV23,PCV20 或 PCV21 作为易感个体的选择;特定肺炎球菌 疫苗接种 时间表应遵循产品特定的处方信息。(1A/强烈推荐)。建议使用 疫苗接种 对抗 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、破伤风、白喉、无细胞百日咳 (Tdap) 和带状疱疹。(1A/有条件推荐)问题7:如何根据ABE分类和外周血嗜酸性粒细胞计数选择初始药物治疗?建议7:应根据症状、病情加重风险和外周血嗜酸性粒细胞计数选择初始药物治疗。对于 E 组患者,应优先考虑降低未来病情加重风险的治疗方案。当外周血嗜酸性粒细胞计数≥300个/μl时,建议三联吸入疗法[吸入糖皮质激素(ICS)加长效β2激动剂(LABA)加长效毒蕈碱拮抗剂(LAMA)]以达到以无加重为特征的低疾病活动状态。(1B/强推荐)问题8:在长效支气管扩张剂治疗中加用ICS的指征是什么?建议8:对于具有以下特征的COPD患者,建议在长效支气管扩张剂治疗中加用ICS:(1)每年≥1次中度或重度COPD急性加重;(2) 血嗜酸性粒细胞计数≥300个/μl,或随访评估时≥100个/μl;(3) 有哮喘病史或伴有哮喘样特征。(1B/强推荐)问题9:稳定期COPD患者应如何建立标准化的随访评估和药理学治疗调整路径?建议9:随访药物治疗期间,治疗调整应以两个核心可治疗特征为指导:持续性呼吸困难和病情加重的发生。对于症状控制持续不佳的患者,应在评估吸入器技术、依从性和合并症的影响后逐步优化支气管扩张剂治疗。对于持续病情加重的患者,ICS的添加、继续或谨慎停药应以当前治疗方案和外周血嗜酸性粒细胞计数为指导。生物疗法适用于选定的高危人群。(1B/强推荐)问题10:对于已经接受ICS+LABA治疗的患者,治疗应该如何优化和调整?建议10:接受ICS+LABA治疗的患者如果病情稳定,可以继续治疗。如果病情持续恶化,建议升级至 ICS+LABA+LAMA 三联疗法。对于肺部感染(包括分枝杆菌感染)高风险的慢性阻塞性肺病患者,应考虑停用ICS并改用双联支气管扩张剂治疗,以降低进一步感染的风险。(1B/强推荐)问题11:COPD患者生物治疗的适应证是什么?建议11:对于ICS+LABA+LAMA治疗后仍反复发作的患者,应评估吸入药物使用情况、合并症等影响因素,根据外周血嗜酸性粒细胞计数、是否患有慢性支气管炎等特点,个体化选择生物治疗。(1B/有条件推荐)问题12:应如何根据患者个体特征选择吸入器装置?建议12:应根据患者是否有足够的吸气流量、手口协调性和独立吸气的能力,以及装置和药物的特性,选择最合适的吸入器装置和药物。应加强吸入器技术和个体化评估的培训。(4/强推荐)问题13:COPD患者应如何实施肺康复?建议13:建议在治疗之前和治疗期间的不同阶段进行动态肺康复评估,包括临床、功能、营养和心理评估。对于没有绝对禁忌症的稳定期慢性阻塞性肺病患者,应尽早开始肺康复治疗。对于COPD急性加重的患者,应在住院期间和出院后尽早进行肺康复。应根据评估结果制定个体化的康复处方,可能包括气道清理技术、呼吸训练、有氧运动和抗阻运动、体位和呼吸支持。(1A/强推荐)问题14:稳定期COPD氧疗的适应证和治疗方案是什么?建议14:对于COPD合并严重静息低氧血症的患者,推荐长期氧疗,定义为(1)静息时PaO2≤55 mmHg(1 mmHg=0.133 kPa)或SpO2≤88%,或(2)PaO2为55-60静息时 mmHg 或 SpO2 为 88%-89%,并伴有肺动脉高压、提示充血性心力衰竭的外周水肿或红细胞增多症(血细胞比容>55%)。应每天至少吸氧 15 小时,以维持 PaO2 ≥60 mmHg 和/或 SpO2 ≥90%。(1B/强推荐)问题15:稳定期COPD无创通气的适应证是什么?建议15:对于严重慢性高碳酸血症且有急性呼吸衰竭住院史的患者,或持续日间高碳酸血症(PaCO2>53 mmHg)的患者,建议长期无创通气(NIV)。(1B/强推荐)问题16:哪些药物和非药物干预措施可以降低COPD患者的死亡率?建议16:建议戒烟、药物治疗(固定剂量三联疗法)以及非药物干预,包括肺康复、长期氧疗、无创通气、手术肺减容等,以降低COPD患者的死亡率。(1B/强推荐)问题17:COPD患者应如何选择支气管镜介入治疗?建议17:对于部分晚期肺气肿合并COPD患者,建议采用支气管镜介入治疗,包括支气管内瓣膜和热蒸汽消融术。(1B/有条件推荐)问题18:慢阻肺患者肺移植的适应证是什么?建议18:对于极重度慢阻肺(进展性疾病,BODE指数为7-10,不适合肺减容手术)患者,如果存在至少以下一项,可考虑转诊肺移植:(1)有高碳酸血症急性加重住院史(PaCO2>50 mmHg);(2) 尽管接受氧疗仍出现肺动脉高压和/或肺心病;(3) FEV1 < 20% 预测值同时 DLCO < 20% 预测值或均匀性肺气肿。(3B/有条件推荐)问题19:慢性阻塞性肺病急性加重期应如何使用全身糖皮质激素?建议19:对于中度或重度慢性阻塞性肺病急性加重期,建议小剂量、短疗程全身糖皮质激素治疗;不建议常规延长 治疗 或使用大剂量全身性皮质类固醇。对于有全身性皮质类固醇禁忌症或不良反应风险高的患者,可以考虑雾化布地奈德作为替代方案。(极低质量证据/弱推荐)问题20:COPD急性加重治疗应如何使用高流量鼻导管氧疗?建议20:对于持续性低氧血症、不能耐受NIV或有NIV禁忌症、NIV后需要序贯氧疗或需要治疗的COPD急性加重患者,建议采用高流量鼻导管氧疗(HFNC)预防拔管后再次插管。对于符合 NIV 指征的急性高碳酸血症性呼吸衰竭患者,HFNC 不应取代 NIV 作为首选初始 治疗。(1B/有条件推荐)问题21:COPD急性加重期治疗无创通气的指征是什么?建议21:有以下任一情况的COPD急性加重期患者,推荐NIV:(1)呼吸性酸中毒(PaCO2≥6.0kPa或45mmHg且动脉pH≤7.35);(2) 严重呼吸困难,伴有呼吸肌疲劳和/或呼吸功增加的临床症状,例如使用辅助呼吸肌、反常的胸腹运动或肋间回缩;(3) 尽管接受氧疗,仍持续低氧血症;(4) 需要序贯有创-无创机械通气。(1A/强推荐)问题22:建议采取哪些措施预防COPD急性加重?建议22:急性加重后,应评估COPD及其合并症的治疗计划,并采取适当措施预防进一步加重,特别是围出院管理、长期标准化药物治疗、戒烟、疫苗接种以及肺康复等非药物干预措施。(4/强烈推荐)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
中国慢性阻塞性肺疾病诊断和管理指南(2026年修订)的制定吸收了中国最新的证据和经验,满足了标准化护理的需求并减轻了疾病负担。
设计与对象
该指南应用 GRADE 方法和牛津循证医学中心的水平来评估证据并为 COPD 诊断 和 22 个临床问题的管理制定建议。
核心方法
该指南使用 GRADE 方法和牛津循证医学中心水平来评估证据的确定性并制定建议,主要关注 COPD 诊断 和管理。
主要结果
该指南为 COPD 诊断 和管理提供了 22 条实用建议,包括病例发现、初始药物治疗、胸部 CT、预防措施和 疫苗接种 建议。
解释与创新
该指南强调COPD的早期诊断、个体化评估和综合管理,强烈推荐年度流感诊断和定期肺炎球菌疫苗接种。
科研与公卫意义
该指南旨在通过促进早期识别和标准化治疗来改善中国COPD的标准化护理并减轻疾病负担。
局限与证据强度
该指南基于中国近期的证据和经验,但没有明确评估证据强度,建议主要基于GRADE方法和牛津循证医学中心的水平。

[Chinese guidelines for the diagnosis and management of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (2026 revision)].

Authors: Chinese Thoracic Society, Chinese Thoracic Society, Chinese Medical Association, Chinese Association of Chest Physicians.
Original abstract (English)
The Chinese guidelines for the diagnosis and management of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (2026 revision) were developed by the Chronic Obstructive Pulmonary Disease Groups of the Chinese Thoracic Society and the Chinese Association of Chest Physicians. Building on the 2021 edition, this revision incorporates evidence published over the past five years and experience from COPD prevention and management in China, and provides comprehensive updates on disease definition, etiology and pathogenesis, case finding, diagnosis and comprehensive assessment, stable COPD management, diagnosis and treatment of acute exacerbations, comorbidity management, and future research directions. The guideline primarily applies the Grading of Recommendations Assessment, Development and Evaluation (GRADE) approach, supplemented by the Oxford Centre for Evidence-Based Medicine Levels of Evidence, to assess the certainty of evidence and develop recommendations. It offers practical recommendations for the diagnosis and management of COPD across 22 clinical questions. It aims to promote early identification, standardized diagnosis and treatment, and comprehensive management of COPD, and therefore to improve the standardized care and reduce the disease burden of COPD in China.The 22 key clinical questions and corresponding recommendations are summarized as follows.Question 1: Given the current underdiagnosis of COPD, which strategies can improve early diagnosis?Recommendation 1: We recommend active and opportunistic case finding to identify individuals at high risk of COPD and facilitate early diagnosis. (1B/strong recommendation)Question 2: Which parameters should be assessed when selecting initial pharmacotherapy for patients with COPD?Recommendation 2: Patients with COPD should undergo individualized assessment based on symptom burden and exacerbation risk and be classified into groups A, B, or E. Additional assessments should include the severity of airflow obstruction on pulmonary function testing, comorbidities, and biomarkers such as the peripheral blood eosinophil count. (2B/strong recommendation)Question 3: How should the mMRC and CAT scores be used for symptom assessment in the ABE classification?Recommendation 3: Symptoms of COPD should be assessed using the modified Medical Research Council (mMRC) dyspnea scale and/or the COPD Assessment Test (CAT). Results from the two instruments may be discordant: the mMRC scale focuses on dyspnea, whereas the CAT is more suitable for a comprehensive assessment of multiple symptoms. (2B/strong recommendation)Question 4: Should chest CT be included in the initial assessment of COPD?Recommendation 4: Chest imaging should be included in the initial assessment of COPD, with chest computed tomography (CT) preferred. (2B/conditional recommendation)Question 5: What are the key preventive measures targeting exposure to risk factors?Recommendation 5: Smoking cessation interventions should be actively provided to all patients with COPD. Clean fuels, effective ventilation, nonpolluting stoves, and similar measures are recommended to reduce harmful environmental exposures; occupational protection should also be strengthened to avoid continued exposure to potential irritants. (1A/strong recommendation)Question 6: How should vaccination be recommended for patients with COPD?Recommendation 6: Annual influenza vaccination is recommended. Regular pneumococcal vaccination with PPSV23 is also recommended, with PCV20 or PCV21 as options for susceptible individuals; the specific pneumococcal vaccination schedule should follow product-specific prescribing information. (1A/strong recommendation). Vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), tetanus, diphtheria, and acellular pertussis (Tdap), and herpes zoster is suggested. (1A/conditional recommendation)Question 7: How should initial pharmacotherapy be selected according to the ABE classification and the peripheral blood eosinophil count?Recommendation 7: Initial pharmacotherapy should be selected according to symptoms, exacerbation risk, and the peripheral blood eosinophil count. For patients in group E, regimens that reduce the risk of future exacerbations should be prioritized. When the peripheral blood eosinophil count is ≥300 cells/μl, triple inhaled therapy[inhaled corticosteroids(ICS) plus long-acting β2 agonists (LABA) plus long-acting muscarinic antagonists(LAMA)] is recommended to achieve a state of low disease activity characterized by freedom from exacerbations. (1B/strong recommendation)Question 8: What are the indications for adding an ICS to long-acting bronchodilator therapy?Recommendation 8: The addition of an ICS to long-acting bronchodilator therapy is recommended for patients with COPD who have any of the following features: (1)≥1 moderate or severe COPD exacerbation per year; (2) a blood eosinophil count ≥300 cells/μl, or ≥100 cells/μl during follow-up assessment; or (3) a history of asthma or concomitant asthma-like features. (1B/strong recommendation)Question 9: How should a standardized pathway for follow-up assessment and pharmacological treatment adjustment be established for patients with stable COPD?Recommendation 9: During follow-up pharmacotherapy, treatment adjustment should be guided by two core treatable traits: persistent dyspnea and the occurrence of exacerbations. For patients with persistently poor symptom control, bronchodilator therapy should be optimized stepwise after inhaler technique, adherence, and the effects of comorbidities have been assessed. For patients who continue to experience exacerbations, the addition, continuation, or cautious withdrawal of ICS should be guided by the current regimen and peripheral blood eosinophil count. Biologic therapy is appropriate for selected high-risk populations. (1B/strong recommendation)Question 10: How should treatment be optimized and adjusted for patients already receiving ICS+LABA therapy?Recommendation 10: Patients receiving ICS+LABA may continue the regimen if their condition is stable. If exacerbations persist, escalation to triple therapy with ICS+LABA+LAMA is recommended. For patients with COPD who are at high risk of pulmonary infection, including mycobacterial infection, withdrawal of ICS and a switch to dual bronchodilator therapy should be considered to reduce the risk of further infections. (1B/strong recommendation)Question 11: What are the indications for biologic therapy in patients with COPD?Recommendation 11: For patients who continue to experience recurrent exacerbations despite ICS+LABA+LAMA therapy, inhaled medication use, comorbidities and other contributing factors should be assessed, and biologic therapy should be selected on an individualized basis according to features such as the peripheral blood eosinophil count and the presence of chronic bronchitis. (1B/conditional recommendation)Question 12: How should an inhaler device be selected according to individual patient characteristics?Recommendation 12: The most appropriate inhaler device and medication should be selected according to whether the patient has adequate inspiratory flow, hand-mouth coordination, and the ability to inhale independently, as well as the characteristics of the device and medication. Training in inhaler technique and individualized assessment should be reinforced. (4/strong recommendation)Question 13: How should pulmonary rehabilitation be implemented for patients with COPD?Recommendation 13: Dynamic pulmonary rehabilitation assessment is recommended before treatment and at different stages during treatment, including clinical, functional, nutritional, and psychological assessments. Pulmonary rehabilitation should be initiated as early as possible in patients with stable COPD who have no absolute contraindications. For patients with acute exacerbations of COPD, pulmonary rehabilitation should be provided early during hospitalization and after discharge. An individualized rehabilitation prescription should be developed according to assessment findings and may include airway clearance techniques, breathing training, aerobic and resistance exercise, positioning, and respiratory support. (1A/strong recommendation)Question 14: What are the indications and treatment regimen for oxygen therapy in stable COPD?Recommendation 14: Long-term oxygen therapy is recommended for patients with COPD and severe resting hypoxemia, defined as either (1) PaO2≤55 mmHg(1 mmHg=0.133 kPa) or SpO2≤88% at rest, or (2) PaO2 of 55-60 mmHg or SpO2 of 88%-89% at rest together with pulmonary hypertension, peripheral edema suggestive of congestive heart failure, or polycythemia (hematocrit>55%). Oxygen should be administered for at least 15 h/day to maintain PaO2 ≥60 mmHg and/or SpO2 ≥90%. (1B/strong recommendation)Question 15: What are the indications for noninvasive ventilation in stable COPD?Recommendation 15: Long-term noninvasive ventilation (NIV) is suggested for patients with severe chronic hypercapnia and a history of hospitalization for acute respiratory failure, or for those with persistent daytime hypercapnia (PaCO2>53 mmHg). (1B/strong recommendation)Question 16: Which pharmacological and nonpharmacological interventions can reduce mortality in patients with COPD?Recommendation 16: Smoking cessation, pharmacotherapy (fixed-dose triple therapy), and nonpharmacological interventions, including pulmonary rehabilitation, long-term oxygen therapy, noninvasive ventilation, and surgical lung volume reduction, are recommended to reduce mortality in patients with COPD. (1B/strong recommendation)Question 17: How should bronchoscopic interventional therapies be selected for patients with COPD?Recommendation 17: Bronchoscopic interventions, including endobronchial valves and thermal vapor ablation, are recommended for selected patients with advanced emphysema and COPD. (1B/conditional recommendation)Question 18: What are the indications for lung transplantation in patients with COPD?Recommendation 18: For patients with very severe COPD (progressive disease with a BODE index of 7-10 who are not candidates for lung volume reduction surgery), referral for lung transplantation may be considered if at least one of the following is present: (1) a history of hospitalization for an acute hypercapnic exacerbation (PaCO2>50 mmHg); (2) pulmonary hypertension and/or cor pulmonale despite oxygen therapy; or (3) FEV1<20% predicted together with DLCO<20% predicted or homogeneous emphysema. (3B/conditional recommendation)Question 19: How should systemic corticosteroids be used for acute exacerbations of COPD?Recommendation 19: For moderate or severe acute exacerbations of COPD, low-dose, short-course systemic corticosteroid therapy is suggested; routine prolongation of treatment or use of high-dose systemic corticosteroids is not recommended. Nebulized budesonide may be considered as an alternative for patients with contraindications to systemic corticosteroids or a high risk of adverse reactions. (Very low-quality evidence/weak recommendation)Question 20: How should high-flow nasal cannula oxygen therapy be used in the treatment of acute exacerbations of COPD?Recommendation 20: High-flow nasal cannula (HFNC) oxygen therapy is suggested for patients with an acute exacerbation of COPD who have persistent hypoxemia, cannot tolerate NIV or have contraindications to NIV, require sequential oxygen therapy after NIV, or require prevention of reintubation after extubation. In patients with acute hypercapnic respiratory failure who meet the indications for NIV, HFNC should not replace NIV as the preferred initial treatment. (1B/conditional recommendation)Question 21: What are the indications for noninvasive ventilation in the treatment of acute exacerbations of COPD?Recommendation 21: NIV is recommended for patients with an acute exacerbation of COPD who have any of the following: (1) respiratory acidosis (PaCO2 ≥6.0 kPa or 45 mmHg and arterial pH≤7.35); (2) severe dyspnea with clinical signs of respiratory muscle fatigue and/or increased work of breathing, such as use of accessory respiratory muscles, paradoxical thoracoabdominal motion, or intercostal retraction; (3) persistent hypoxemia despite oxygen therapy; or (4) a need for sequential invasive-noninvasive mechanical ventilation. (1A/strong recommendation)Question 22: What measures are recommended to prevent acute exacerbations of COPD?Recommendation 22: After an acute exacerbation, the management plans for COPD and its comorbidities should be evaluated, and appropriate measures should be implemented to prevent further exacerbations, particularly peri-discharge management, long-term standardized pharmacotherapy, smoking cessation, vaccination, and nonpharmacological interventions such as pulmonary rehabilitation. (4/strong recommendation).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The Chinese guidelines for the diagnosis and management of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (2026 revision) were developed to incorporate recent evidence and experiences in China, addressing the need for standardized care and reducing the disease burden.
Design and population
The guidelines apply the GRADE approach and Oxford Centre for Evidence-Based Medicine levels to assess evidence and develop recommendations for COPD diagnosis and management across 22 clinical questions.
Core methods
The guidelines use the GRADE approach and Oxford Centre for Evidence-Based Medicine levels to assess the certainty of evidence and develop recommendations, primarily focusing on COPD diagnosis and management.
Main results
The guidelines offer 22 practical recommendations for COPD diagnosis and management, including case finding, initial pharmacotherapy, chest CT, preventive measures, and vaccination recommendations.
Interpretation and novelty
The guidelines emphasize early diagnosis, individualized assessment, and comprehensive management of COPD, with strong recommendations for annual influenza vaccination and regular pneumococcal vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The guidelines aim to improve standardized care and reduce the disease burden of COPD in China by promoting early identification and standardized treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The guidelines are based on recent evidence and experiences in China, but the evidence strength is not explicitly assessed, and the recommendations are primarily based on the GRADE approach and Oxford Centre for Evidence-Based Medicine levels.

鼻粘膜免疫与婴儿 RSV 疾病的预防相关

Nasal Mucosal Immunity is Associated with Protection against RSV Illness in Infants
作者： Faith Taliaferro, Ying Tang, Sergio Monares Ortiz, Olyvia J. Jasset, Caroline Bald, Alison Araten, Calvin Fairbourn, Wanda I. Gonzalez, Peter P. Moschovis, Kathryn E. Stephenson
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫预防取得了进展,RSV 仍然是婴儿严重呼吸道疾病的主要原因。感染部位的免疫力可能很重要,但针对 RSV 感染的气道粘膜免疫力仍然不明确。我们收集了三名儿科 队列研究 的鼻腔和血液样本:1) 因呼吸道症状而到急诊室就诊的儿童;2) 在 RSV 季节期间到城市医院系统就诊的婴儿和母亲;3) 接种母体预融合 (F) 疫苗 的母亲所生的婴儿。我们分析了粘膜和血清学抗 RSV 抗体,并评估了影响滴度大小和持久性的因素。对 285 名婴儿/幼儿和 39 名母亲的样本进行了分析。与年龄匹配的对照组相比,在经历第一个病毒季节的婴儿 (p = 0.0011) 和年龄较大的儿童 (p < 0.0001) 中,出现 RSV 感染的婴儿的粘膜抗 RSV 抗体水平较低。在母亲接受母体 pre-F 疫苗接种 的婴儿中观察到较高水平的粘膜抗体,针对 RSV 融合蛋白的 IgG 在婴儿血清和鼻腔室之间紧密相关 (p < 0.0001)。然而,到 6 个月大时,粘膜抗 RSV IgG 显着减弱 (p < 0.0001)。较高的粘膜抗 RSV 抗体水平与预防 RSV 相关疾病有关。当前的免疫预防策略在婴儿粘膜和血清学区室中驱动强大的抗 RSV IgG 反应;然而,被动免疫力在 6 个月内减弱。这些结果表明,优化持久粘膜免疫的 RSV 免疫策略可以保护婴儿免受 RSV 相关呼吸道疾病的侵害。

设计与对象
我们分析了粘膜和血清学抗 RSV 抗体,并评估了影响滴度大小和持久性的因素。
核心方法
对 285 名婴儿/幼儿和 39 名母亲的样本进行了分析。
主要结果
与年龄匹配的对照组相比,在经历第一个病毒季节的婴儿 (p = 0.0011) 和年龄较大的儿童 (p < 0.0001) 中,出现 RSV 感染的婴儿的粘膜抗 RSV 抗体水平较低。

这些结果表明,优化持久粘膜免疫的 RSV 免疫策略可以保护婴儿免受 RSV 相关呼吸道疾病的侵害。

在母亲接受母体 pre-F 疫苗接种 的婴儿中观察到较高水平的粘膜抗体,针对 RSV 融合蛋白的 IgG 在婴儿血清和鼻腔室之间紧密相关 (p < 0.0001)。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Nasal Mucosal Immunity is Associated with Protection against RSV Illness in Infants

Authors: Faith Taliaferro, Ying Tang, Sergio Monares Ortiz, Olyvia J. Jasset, Caroline Bald, Alison Araten, Calvin Fairbourn, Wanda I. Gonzalez, Peter P. Moschovis, Kathryn E. Stephenson
Original abstract (English)
Background: Despite advances in respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunoprophylaxis, RSV remains a leading cause of severe respiratory illness in infants. Immunity at the site of infection is potentially important yet airway mucosal immunity against RSV infection remains poorly defined. We collected nasal and blood samples across three pediatric cohorts: 1) children presenting to the emergency department with respiratory symptoms; 2) infants and mothers presenting to an urban hospital system across the RSV season; 3) infants born to mothers vaccinated with maternal pre-Fusion (F) vaccine. We analyzed mucosal and serologic anti-RSV antibodies and assessed for factors impacting titer magnitude and durability. Specimens from 285 infants/young children and 39 mothers were analyzed. Mucosal anti-RSV antibody levels were lower in infants presenting with RSV infection compared to age-matched controls both in infants experiencing their first viral season (p = 0.0011) and older children (p < 0.0001). Higher levels of mucosal antibodies were seen in infants whose mothers received maternal pre-F vaccination, with IgG against RSV fusion protein tightly correlating between infant serum and nasal compartments (p < 0.0001). However, mucosal anti-RSV IgG waned significantly by 6 months of age (p < 0.0001). Higher mucosal anti-RSV antibody levels are associated with protection against RSV-related illness. Current immunoprophylactic strategies drive robust anti-RSV IgG responses in the infant mucosal and serological compartments; however, passive immunity wanes over 6 months. These results suggest that RSV immunization strategies optimizing durable mucosal immunity may protect infants from RSV-related respiratory illness.

通过 Hankel 动态模式分解对美国和加拿大的流感、RSV 和 COVID-19 相关结果进行数据驱动的预测。

Data-driven forecasting of Flu, RSV, and COVID-19 related outcomes in the United States and Canada via Hankel dynamic mode decomposition.
作者： William T. Redman, Luke C. Mullany
摘要中文翻译(大的)季节变化和有限的动态历史使得传染病的预测成为一个具有挑战性的问题。在这里,我们通过对一种方法 Hankel 动态模式分解 (DMD) 对美国和加拿大 2024-2025 年流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 COVID-19 季节的性能进行基准测试,研究数据驱动的动态建模的进步在多大程度上可以提供准确的预测。使用 Hankel-DMD,我们生成了每周预测,并提交给疾病控制和预防中心 (CDC) 的 FluSight 预测中心和圭尔夫大学的 AI4CastingHub。在这两个中心,我们发现 Hankel-DMD 可以在季节开始和结束时提供高质量的预测,但季节中期的预测严重高估了季节高峰。当对整个季节的提交进行评估时,这会导致 FluSight Forecast Hub 的基准性能更差。Hankel-DMD 被认为是少数模型中最好的,这些模型提交了足够的预测,可以与加拿大的流感、RSV 和 COVID-19 的预测进行比较。由于这是 AI4CastingHub 的第一个活跃年份,这表明当缺乏预测新地区感染动态的专业知识时,Hankel-DMD 可能特别有用。为了研究 Hankel-DMD 如何更好地在季节高峰期间提供准确的预测,我们探讨了带记忆的 DMD(一种最近开发的将 DMD 应用于非平稳动力系统的方法)是否能提供显着的改进。使用具有记忆功能的 DMD 进行回顾性分析显示,有望减少季节高峰期间的高估,但在实时预测环境中其最佳使用程度仍有待观察。总的来说,我们的结果表明 Hankel-DMD 具有作为一种有效且可解释的传染病预测方法的潜力,同时也强调了未来工作应致力于解决的许多剩余方法学挑战。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估数据驱动的动态模型(特别是汉克尔动态模式分解(DMD))在预测传染病结果、解决季节变化和有限动态历史带来的挑战方面的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究对美国和加拿大的 2024-2025 年流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 COVID-19 季节进行了回顾性分析,将使用 Hankel-DMD 生成的预测与提交给 CDC 的 FluSight 预测中心和圭尔夫大学的 AI4CastingHub 的预测进行了比较。
核心方法
Hankel-DMD 用于生成每周预测,并将其提交给 FluSight Forecast Hub 和 AI4CastingHub 进行评估。该方法与提交同一季节预测的其他模型进行了基准测试。
主要结果
Hankel-DMD 在季节开始和结束时提供了高质量的预报,但明显高估了季节中期的峰值。这种高估导致 FluSight 预测中心的性能低于基线。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,虽然 Hankel-DMD 是一种很有前途的传染病预测方法,但其在季节高峰期间的表现需要进一步完善。该研究强调了这种方法的潜力及其当前的局限性。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,Hankel-DMD 可能是预测 呼吸道合胞病毒 和其他传染病的宝贵工具,有助于公共卫生规划和资源分配,特别是在专业知识有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性和用于比较的模型数量有限可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据是温和的,因为对整个赛季的表现进行了评估,并且该方法在高峰期间的有效性仍然不确定。

Data-driven forecasting of Flu, RSV, and COVID-19 related outcomes in the United States and Canada via Hankel dynamic mode decomposition.

Authors: William T. Redman, Luke C. Mullany
Original abstract (English)
The (large) season-to-season variability and limited dynamical history make the forecasting of infectious diseases a challenging problem. Here, we examine the extent to which advances in data-driven dynamical modeling can provide accurate predictions by benchmarking the performance of one such method, Hankel dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), on the 2024-2025 influenza, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and COVID-19 seasons in the United States and Canada. Using Hankel-DMD, we generated weekly forecasts that were submitted to the Center for Disease Control and Prevention's (CDC) FluSight Forecast Hub and the University of Guelph's AI4CastingHub. Across both Hubs, we find that Hankel-DMD can provide high quality forecasts at the beginning and end of the season, but forecasts in the middle of the season suffer from significant overestimation of the season peak. This leads to worse than baseline performance on FluSight Forecast Hub, when submissions are evaluated across the entire season. Hankel-DMD is found to be the best among a small number of models that submitted enough forecasts against which to compare for forecasting influenza, RSV, and COVID-19 in Canada. As this was the first active year of AI4CastingHub, this suggests that Hankel-DMD may be especially useful when expertise is lacking for predicting infectious dynamics in new regions. To study how Hankel-DMD can be better equipped to provide accurate forecasts during the season peak, we explored whether DMD with memory, a recently developed approach for applying DMD to non-stationary dynamical systems, provides significant improvements. Retrospective analysis using DMD with memory shows promise in reducing overestimation during season peak, but the extent to which its optimal use can be achieved in a live forecasting setting remains to be seen. Collectively, our results demonstrate that Hankel-DMD has potential as an efficient and interpretable method for the forecasting of infectious diseases, while also highlighting a number of remaining methodological challenges which future work should aim to address.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the potential of data-driven dynamical modeling, specifically Hankel dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), for forecasting infectious disease outcomes, addressing the challenge posed by season-to-season variability and limited dynamical history.
Design and population
The study conducted a retrospective analysis of the 2024-2025 influenza, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and COVID-19 seasons in the United States and Canada, comparing forecasts generated using Hankel-DMD against those submitted to the CDC's FluSight Forecast Hub and the University of Guelph's AI4CastingHub.
Core methods
Hankel-DMD was used to generate weekly forecasts, which were submitted to the FluSight Forecast Hub and AI4CastingHub for evaluation. The method was benchmarked against other models that submitted forecasts for the same seasons.
Main results
Hankel-DMD provided high-quality forecasts at the beginning and end of the season but significantly overestimated the peak during the middle of the season. This overestimation led to worse than baseline performance on the FluSight Forecast Hub.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that while Hankel-DMD is a promising method for forecasting infectious diseases, its performance during the season peak requires further refinement. The study highlights the potential of this method as well as its current limitations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results indicate that Hankel-DMD could be a valuable tool for forecasting respiratory syncytial virus and other infectious diseases, aiding in public health planning and resource allocation, particularly in regions with limited expertise.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective nature and the limited number of models used for comparison may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate, as the performance was evaluated across the entire season, and the method's effectiveness during the peak remains uncertain.

关于腺病毒 媒介 疫苗 对抗 RSV 的新型口咽喷雾免疫功效的验证性双中心非人灵长类动物研究 - 重要经验教训。

A Confirmatory, Dual Center Nonhuman Primate Study on the Efficacy of Novel Oropharyngeal Spray Immunization With an Adenoviral Vector Vaccine Against RSV-Important Lessons Learned.
作者： Matthias Tenbusch, Gerrit Koopman, Petra Mooij, Berit Roshani, Pascal Irrgang, Dennis Lapuente, Ivanela Kondova, Willy M Bogers, Edmond J Remarque, Ramona Vestweber
摘要中文翻译背景:在欧洲两个灵长类研究机构的一项随机、盲法、双中心研究中,我们评估了 DNA 初免口咽腺病毒增强 疫苗接种 策略针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的免疫原性和保护功效。方法:恒河猴通过电穿孔接收 RSV 融合蛋白 (F) DNA 疫苗,然后两次粘膜注射编码相同抗原的重组腺病毒 媒介。模拟接种动物和模拟原发性 RSV 感染诱导的自然免疫的比较组作为对照。纵向监测全身和粘膜免疫应答。结果:我们检测到了强大的细胞和体液 免疫应答,但两个中心之间存在一些差异。然而,接种 RSV 疫苗的动物在 RSV 攻击感染后,上呼吸道和下呼吸道中的病毒复制明显减少。一个研究中心的对照组中出现了意外的 RSV 特异性免疫,导致无法确认预定义的主要终点。结论:我们的结果支持 DNA 引发后粘膜腺病毒增强诱导针对 RSV 的保护性免疫的潜力,同时强调了与多中心临床前 疫苗 研究相关的挑战。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了评估恒河猴中 DNA 初免口咽腺病毒增强 疫苗接种 策略针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的免疫原性和保护功效,满足改进 RSV 疫苗 的需求。
设计与对象
两个欧洲灵长类研究机构进行的一项随机、盲法、双中心研究评估了恒河猴中的 疫苗。
核心方法
恒河猴通过电穿孔接收 RSV F DNA 疫苗,然后两次粘膜施用编码相同抗原的重组腺病毒 媒介,其中模拟疫苗接种的动物和比较组作为对照。
主要结果
检测到强大的细胞和体液 免疫应答,并且 RSV 疫苗接种的动物在 RSV 攻击感染后证明上呼吸道和下呼吸道中的病毒复制减少。
解释与创新
该研究支持 DNA 引发后粘膜腺病毒增强诱导针对 RSV 的保护性免疫的潜力,凸显了多中心临床前 疫苗 研究中的挑战。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 RSV 疫苗 的开发提供了一种有前途的方法,并强调了跨多个中心一致的研究设计的重要性。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性,包括中心之间的差异和对​​照组的意外免疫力,可能会影响证据的强度。

A Confirmatory, Dual Center Nonhuman Primate Study on the Efficacy of Novel Oropharyngeal Spray Immunization With an Adenoviral Vector Vaccine Against RSV-Important Lessons Learned.

Authors: Matthias Tenbusch, Gerrit Koopman, Petra Mooij, Berit Roshani, Pascal Irrgang, Dennis Lapuente, Ivanela Kondova, Willy M Bogers, Edmond J Remarque, Ramona Vestweber
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In a randomized, blinded, dual center study across two European primate research facilities, we evaluated the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of a DNA prime-oropharyngeal adenoviral boost vaccination strategy against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). METHODS: Rhesus macaques received an RSV fusion protein (F) DNA vaccine via electroporation, followed by two mucosal administrations of a recombinant adenovirus vector encoding the same antigen. Mock-vaccinated animals and a comparator group mimicking natural immunity induced by primary RSV infection were included as controls. Systemic and mucosal immune responses were monitored longitudinally. RESULTS: We detected robust cellular and humoral immune responses, but with some variability between the two centers. Nevertheless, RSV-vaccinated animals demonstrated markedly reduced viral replication in the upper and lower respiratory tract after RSV challenge infection. Unexpected RSV-specific immunity in the control group at one single study site prevented confirmation of the predefined primary endpoint. CONCLUSION: Our results support the potential of mucosal adenoviral boosting following DNA priming to induce protective immunity against RSV, while highlighting challenges associated with multi-center preclinical vaccine studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To evaluate the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of a DNA prime-oropharyngeal adenoviral boost vaccination strategy against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in rhesus macaques, addressing the need for improved RSV vaccines.
Design and population
A randomized, blinded, dual center study across two European primate research facilities evaluated the vaccine in rhesus macaques.
Core methods
Rhesus macaques received an RSV F DNA vaccine via electroporation, followed by two mucosal administrations of a recombinant adenovirus vector encoding the same antigen, with mock-vaccinated animals and a comparator group included as controls.
Main results
Robust cellular and humoral immune responses were detected, and RSV-vaccinated animals demonstrated reduced viral replication in the upper and lower respiratory tract after RSV challenge infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The study supports the potential of mucosal adenoviral boosting following DNA priming to induce protective immunity against RSV, highlighting challenges in multi-center preclinical vaccine studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest a promising approach for RSV vaccine development and highlight the importance of consistent study design across multiple centers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations, including variability between centers and unexpected immunity in the control group, may affect the strength of the evidence.

从实验室定量到快速现场测试:针对 RSV F 蛋白的高亲和力单克隆抗体对的筛选以及跨多个检测平台的分析评估。

From laboratory quantification to rapid on-site testing: Screening of high-affinity monoclonal antibody pairs targeting the RSV F protein and analytical evaluation across multiple assay platforms.
作者： Si-Qing Peng, Qi-Qi Liu, Chen Duan, Jun-Xi Liu, Rui Wang, Ying-Qi Huang, An-Hai Long, Qiao-Ying Deng, Xi-Yan Deng, Ting Wan
摘要中文翻译针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) F 蛋白的单克隆抗体是基于抗原的 诊断 的关键试剂,但与多种检测格式兼容的抗体对仍然有限。在这项研究中,用重组后RSV F蛋白免疫BALB/c小鼠,产生六种分泌抗RSV F单克隆抗体的杂交瘤细胞系。通过间接 ELISA、Western blotting 和表面等离子共振对抗体进行表征,并通过交叉配对 ELISA 选择最佳夹心对。mAb-14/mAb-26对表现出高亲和力和最佳配对性能,用于建立双抗体夹心ELISA(DAS-ELISA)、基于磁性微粒的化学发光免疫分析和基于Au@Pt纳米颗粒的侧流免疫分析(Au@Pt-LFIA)。检测限分别为 0.25、0.04 和 0.78 ng/mL。Au@Pt-LFIA 的视觉检测限比传统胶体金免疫层析低约四倍,无需额外的信号放大步骤。三种方法均未显示出与七种测试的异源呼吸道病原体抗原的可检测的交叉反应性。初步临床样本检测中,DAS-ELISA、化学发光免疫分析和Au@Pt-LFIA的阳性一致性百分比分别为90.0%、93.3%和83.3%,所有方法的阴性一致性百分比均为100.0%。这些结果支持 mAb-14/mAb-26 作为通用抗体对,用于跨互补实验室和现场检测平台检测 RSV 抗原。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在这项研究中,用重组后RSV F蛋白免疫BALB/c小鼠,产生六种分泌抗RSV F单克隆抗体的杂交瘤细胞系。
设计与对象
初步临床样本检测中,DAS-ELISA、化学发光免疫分析和Au@Pt-LFIA的阳性一致性百分比分别为90.0%、93.3%和83.3%,所有方法的阴性一致性百分比均为100.0%。
核心方法
mAb-14/mAb-26对表现出高亲和力和最佳配对性能,用于建立双抗体夹心ELISA(DAS-ELISA)、基于磁性微粒的化学发光免疫分析和基于Au@Pt纳米颗粒的侧流免疫分析(Au@Pt-LFIA)。
主要结果
三种方法均未显示出与七种测试的异源呼吸道病原体抗原的可检测的交叉反应性。
解释与创新
这些结果支持 mAb-14/mAb-26 作为通用抗体对,用于跨互补实验室和现场检测平台检测 RSV 抗原。
科研与公卫意义
Au@Pt-LFIA 的视觉检测限比传统胶体金免疫层析低约四倍,无需额外的信号放大步骤。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

From laboratory quantification to rapid on-site testing: Screening of high-affinity monoclonal antibody pairs targeting the RSV F protein and analytical evaluation across multiple assay platforms.

Authors: Si-Qing Peng, Qi-Qi Liu, Chen Duan, Jun-Xi Liu, Rui Wang, Ying-Qi Huang, An-Hai Long, Qiao-Ying Deng, Xi-Yan Deng, Ting Wan
Original abstract (English)
Monoclonal antibodies against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) F protein are key reagents for antigen-based diagnosis, yet antibody pairs compatible with multiple assay formats remain limited. In this study, BALB/c mice were immunized with recombinant postfusion RSV F protein, yielding six hybridoma cell lines secreting anti-RSV F monoclonal antibodies. The antibodies were characterized by indirect ELISA, Western blotting, and surface plasmon resonance, and the optimal sandwich pair was selected by cross-pairing ELISA. The mAb-14/mAb-26 pair showed high affinity and the best pairing performance and was used to establish double-antibody sandwich ELISA (DAS-ELISA), magnetic microparticle-based chemiluminescent immunoassay, and Au@Pt nanoparticle-based lateral flow immunoassay (Au@Pt-LFIA). The limits of detection were 0.25, 0.04, and 0.78 ng/mL, respectively. Au@Pt-LFIA achieved an approximately fourfold lower visual detection limit than conventional colloidal gold immunochromatography without additional signal-amplification steps. None of the three methods showed detectable cross-reactivity with the seven tested heterologous respiratory pathogen antigens. In preliminary clinical sample testing, the positive percent agreements of DAS-ELISA, chemiluminescent immunoassay, and Au@Pt-LFIA were 90.0%, 93.3%, and 83.3%, respectively, and the negative percent agreements were 100.0% for all methods. These results support mAb-14/mAb-26 as a versatile antibody pair for RSV antigen detection across complementary laboratory and on-site assay platforms.

Question and background
In this study, BALB/c mice were immunized with recombinant postfusion RSV F protein, yielding six hybridoma cell lines secreting anti-RSV F monoclonal antibodies.
Design and population
In preliminary clinical sample testing, the positive percent agreements of DAS-ELISA, chemiluminescent immunoassay, and Au@Pt-LFIA were 90.0%, 93.3%, and 83.3%, respectively, and the negative percent agreements were 100.0% for all methods.

The mAb-14/mAb-26 pair showed high affinity and the best pairing performance and was used to establish double-antibody sandwich ELISA (DAS-ELISA), magnetic microparticle-based chemiluminescent immunoassay, and Au@Pt nanoparticle-based lateral flow immunoassay (Au@Pt-LFIA).
Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Limitations and evidence strength

双适体磁性捕获和电化学检测人工唾液中的 B 型流感病毒和 呼吸道合胞病毒。

Dual-aptamer magnetic capture and electrochemical detection of influenza B and respiratory syncytial viruses in artificial saliva.
作者： Dide Su Demirel, Naci Polat, Hakan Eligul, Elif Esra Altuner, Ali Dogan Dursun, Gunes Kibar, Zeynep Yegin, Barbaros Çetin, Veli Cengiz Ozalp
摘要中文翻译唾液是一种很有前途的呼吸道病毒诊断非侵入性样本,但其生化复杂性挑战了与基于亲和力的生物传感器的直接连接。我们报告了一种双适体磁捕获电化学测定法,用于检测人工唾液中的乙型流感病毒(IBV)和人 呼吸道合胞病毒(hRSV)。对于每种目标病毒,使用两个序列不同的DNA适体:氨基修饰的捕获适体(IBV1或RSV1)通过与聚多巴胺醌/儿茶酚表面的直接席夫碱反应共价偶联至~5μm聚多巴胺包被的磁性微粒,以及硫醇修饰的检测适体(IBV2或RSV2)通过丝网印刷金电极自组装硫醇-金化学。在使用两种适体磁性颗粒类型进行单一多重批量捕获步骤后,将颗粒分开并通过方波伏安法 (SWV) 在两个适体修饰的电极上并行分析。SWV 校准曲线的 Langmuir 分析得出表观解离常数 Kd = 177 ± 62 PFU mL-1 (IBV) 和 226 ± 85 PFU mL-1 (hRSV),检测限分别为 15 和 11 PFU mL-1。对数线性校准在两个通道上的范围为 101-104 PFU mL-1(n = 4 个浓度,R2 = 0.995 和 0.990),灵敏度为每十年 8.58 和 6.61 μA,因此每个病毒特异性检测涵盖三个数量级的病毒载量。序列-正交双适体几何结构-针对不同表位的捕获和检测识别-被提议作为结构缓解。这一概念验证建立了一种可编程的、唾液兼容的架构,可仅通过适体替换来针对新出现的呼吸道病原体进行重新配置。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决检测唾液中呼吸道病毒的挑战,唾液是一种非侵入性样本,但由于其生化复杂性而难以处理。该研究试图开发一种可以克服这些挑战以实现准确病毒检测的方法。
设计与对象
该研究涉及开发和测试双适体磁捕获电化学测定法,用于检测人工唾液中的乙型流感病毒 (IBV) 和人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (hRSV)。该方法旨在通过适体替代适用于新出现的呼吸道病原体。
核心方法
该方法针对每种目标病毒使用两个序列不同的DNA适体:共价连接到磁性微粒上的捕获适体和在金电极上自组装的检测适体。该测定涉及单个多重批量捕获步骤,然后使用方波伏安法对两个适配体修饰的电极进行并行分析。
主要结果
方波伏安法校准曲线的 Langmuir 分析得出 IBV 的表观解离常数为 Kd = 177 ± 62 PFU mL-1,hRSV 的表观解离常数为 226 ± 85 PFU mL-1,检测限分别为 15 和 11 PFU mL-1。两个通道的对数线性校准范围为 101-104 PFU mL-1,灵敏度为每十倍程 8.58 和 6.61 μA。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明了双适体方法在检测人工唾液中的 IBV 和 hRSV 方面的有效性。新颖之处在于所提出的使用序列正交双适体几何结构进行捕获和检测的结构缓解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为唾液中呼吸道病毒的非侵入性检测提供了一种有前景的方法,可以增强监测和诊断对呼吸道合胞病毒和其他新兴病原体的检测能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究是概念验证,不报告临床验证或实际应用。该证据基于人工唾液,可能无法完全代表人类样本的复杂性,因此可信度评估为中等。

Dual-aptamer magnetic capture and electrochemical detection of influenza B and respiratory syncytial viruses in artificial saliva.

Authors: Dide Su Demirel, Naci Polat, Hakan Eligul, Elif Esra Altuner, Ali Dogan Dursun, Gunes Kibar, Zeynep Yegin, Barbaros Çetin, Veli Cengiz Ozalp
Original abstract (English)
Saliva is a promising non-invasive specimen for respiratory virus diagnostics but its biochemical complexity challenges direct interfacing with affinity-based biosensors. We report a dual-aptamer magnetic-capture electrochemical assay for detection of influenza B virus (IBV) and human respiratory syncytial virus (hRSV) in artificial saliva. For each target virus, two sequence-distinct DNA aptamers are used: an amino-modified capture aptamer (IBV1 or RSV1) covalently coupled to ∼5 μm polydopamine-coated magnetic microparticles by direct Schiff-base reaction with the polydopamine quinone/catechol surface, and a thiol-modified detection aptamer (IBV2 or RSV2) self-assembled on screen-printed gold electrodes via thiol-gold chemistry. After a single multiplexed batch capture step with both aptamer-magnetic-particle types, the pellet is split and analysed in parallel on the two aptamer-modified electrodes by square-wave voltammetry (SWV). Langmuir analysis of the SWV calibration curves yielded apparent dissociation constants of Kd = 177 ± 62 PFU mL-1 (IBV) and 226 ± 85 PFU mL-1 (hRSV), with detection limits of 15 and 11 PFU mL-1, respectively. Log-linear calibration spanned 101-104 PFU mL-1 on both channels (n = 4 concentrations, R2 = 0.995 and 0.990) with sensitivities of 8.58 and 6.61 μA per decade, so that each virus-specific assay covers three orders of magnitude of viral load. The sequence-orthogonal dual-aptamer geometry-capture and detection recognitions targeting non-identical epitopes-is proposed as the structural mitigation. This proof-of-concept establishes a programmable, saliva-compatible architecture reconfigurable for emerging respiratory pathogens by aptamer substitution alone.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of detecting respiratory viruses in saliva, which is a non-invasive specimen but difficult to work with due to its biochemical complexity. The research sought to develop a method that could overcome these challenges for accurate virus detection.
Design and population
The study involved the development and testing of a dual-aptamer magnetic-capture electrochemical assay for detecting influenza B virus (IBV) and human respiratory syncytial virus (hRSV) in artificial saliva. The method was designed to be applicable to emerging respiratory pathogens through aptamer substitution.
Core methods
The method utilized two sequence-distinct DNA aptamers for each target virus: a capture aptamer covalently attached to magnetic microparticles and a detection aptamer self-assembled on gold electrodes. The assay involved a single multiplexed batch capture step followed by parallel analysis on the two aptamer-modified electrodes using square-wave voltammetry.
Main results
Langmuir analysis of the square-wave voltammetry calibration curves yielded apparent dissociation constants of Kd = 177 ± 62 PFU mL-1 for IBV and 226 ± 85 PFU mL-1 for hRSV, with detection limits of 15 and 11 PFU mL-1, respectively. The log-linear calibration spanned 101-104 PFU mL-1 on both channels with sensitivities of 8.58 and 6.61 μA per decade.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating the effectiveness of the dual-aptamer approach in detecting both IBV and hRSV in artificial saliva. The novelty lies in the proposed structural mitigation using sequence-orthogonal dual-aptamer geometry for capture and detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a promising method for the non-invasive detection of respiratory viruses in saliva, which could enhance surveillance and diagnosis capabilities for respiratory syncytial virus and other emerging pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a proof-of-concept and does not report on clinical validation or real-world application. The evidence is based on artificial saliva and may not fully represent the complexity of human samples, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

流感、SARS-CoV-2和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)在医疗保健环境中气溶胶的大小、细胞病变效应和患病率

Size, cytopathic effects, and prevalence of Influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and RSV in healthcare settings
作者： Tanvir Ahmed, Kamaljeet Kaur, Elizabeth Myers, Selah Willis, Chase Crimmins, Yue Zhang, Jessica A. Plante, Kenneth Plante, Rachael M. Jones, Darrah K. Sleeth
摘要中文翻译在医疗保健环境中评估呼吸道病毒气溶胶的传播性(传播)具有挑战性,尤其是在病毒气溶胶的大小和活性方面。大多数现场研究依赖于分子检测,这限制了对病毒传染性的推断。本研究在2024-2025年呼吸道病毒季节期间,使用BC(BioCascade)-BioSpot VIVAS(可培养病毒气溶胶采样器)在牙科诊所和医院中收集了具有粒径分辨率的空气样本。我们通过RT-PCR和基于培养的检测方法分析了这些样本,以检测流感A型、流感B型、SARS-CoV-2和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)。在81次采样事件中,有11.1%的样本检测到病毒RNA,其中肺病单元病房的阳性率最高(25%)。当检测到流感和SARS-CoV-2的RNA时,所有检测均发生在4-10 μm粒径组分中,而所有RSV RNA均检测到在<1.5 μm粒径组分中。细胞培养检测证实了通过RT-PCR检测为阳性的样本中存在可培养的流感病毒。仅在流感A型病毒阳性样本中观察到斑块,而RSV和SARS-CoV-2在细胞培养检测中未产生细胞病变效应。这些发现突显了不同病毒在气溶胶粒径分布上的特异性差异,以及在医疗保健环境中从现场收集的生物气溶胶中回收活性病毒的挑战。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估医疗保健环境中呼吸道病毒的气溶胶传播性(传播),重点关注病毒气溶胶的大小和活性,特别是针对呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)的情况。
设计与对象
本研究在2024-2025年呼吸道病毒季节期间,在牙科诊所和医院中收集了具有粒径分辨率的空气样本,共进行了81次采样,覆盖不同地点。
核心方法
使用BC-BioSpot VIVAS(可培养病毒气溶胶采样器)收集空气样本,并通过RT-PCR和基于培养的检测方法分析流感A型、流感B型、SARS-CoV-2和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)。
主要结果
在81次采样事件中,有11.1%的样本检测到病毒RNA,其中肺病单元病房的阳性率最高(25%)。所有RSV RNA均检测到在<1.5 μm粒径组分中。
解释与创新
这些发现突显了不同病毒在气溶胶粒径分布上的特异性差异,以及在医疗保健环境中从现场收集的生物气溶胶中回收活性病毒的挑战。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为制定针对RSV的特定监测(监测)和控制策略提供信息,特别是在气溶胶大小和活性方面。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要证据,且结果基于分子检测而非直接传染性评估,这可能会影响结论的强度。

Size, cytopathic effects, and prevalence of Influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and RSV in healthcare settings

Authors: Tanvir Ahmed, Kamaljeet Kaur, Elizabeth Myers, Selah Willis, Chase Crimmins, Yue Zhang, Jessica A. Plante, Kenneth Plante, Rachael M. Jones, Darrah K. Sleeth
Original abstract (English)
Airborne transmission of respiratory viruses in healthcare settings is difficult to assess, particularly with respect to the size and viability of virus-containing aerosols. Most field studies rely on molecular detection, which limits inference about the infectiousness of virus. In this study we collected size-resolved airborne samples in a dental clinic and hospital during the 2024-2025 respiratory virus season using a BC (BioCascade)-BioSpot VIVAS (Viable Virus Aerosol Sampler). We analyzed these samples by RT-PCR and culture-based assays for influenza A, influenza B, SARS-CoV-2, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Viral RNA was detected in 11.1% of the 81 sampling events, with the highest positivity (25%) observed in pulmonary unit patient rooms. When influenza and SARS-CoV-2 RNA were detected, all of these detections occurred in the 4-10 μm size fraction, whereas all RSV RNA was detected in the <1.5 μm size fraction. Cell culture assays confirmed the presence of viable influenza in the samples that were positive by RT-PCR. Plaques were only observed for influenza A viral positive samples at the ‘neat’ or undiluted rate, whereas RSV and SARS-CoV-2 did not produce cytopathic effects in the cell culture assays. These findings highlight virus-specific differences in aerosol size distributions and the challenges of recovering viable virus from field-collected bioaerosols in healthcare environments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess airborne transmission of respiratory viruses in healthcare settings, focusing on the size and viability of virus-containing aerosols, particularly for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).
Design and population
The study collected size-resolved airborne samples in a dental clinic and hospital during the 2024-2025 respiratory virus season, with 81 sampling events conducted across different locations.
Core methods
Airborne samples were collected using a BC-BioSpot VIVAS (Viable Virus Aerosol Sampler) and analyzed by RT-PCR and culture-based assays for influenza A, influenza B, SARS-CoV-2, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).
Main results
Viral RNA was detected in 11.1% of the 81 sampling events, with the highest positivity (25%) observed in pulmonary unit patient rooms. All RSV RNA was detected in the <1.5 μm size fraction.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight virus-specific differences in aerosol size distributions and the challenges of recovering viable virus from field-collected bioaerosols in healthcare environments.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the development of targeted surveillance and control strategies for RSV in healthcare settings, particularly regarding aerosol size and viability.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on molecular detection rather than direct infectiousness assessment, which may affect the strength of the conclusions.

IL-33 信号传导使肺调节性 T 细胞的特化与 2 型炎症期间的抑制功能脱钩

IL-33 signaling uncouples pulmonary regulatory T-cell specialization from suppressive function during type 2 inflammation
作者： Ciriaco Piccirillo, Fernando Alvarez, Lydia Labrie, Tho-Alfakar Al-Aubodah, Roman Isotomine, Katherine H. Restori, Jörg Hermann Fritz, Elizabeth D. Fixman
摘要中文翻译摘要 调节性 T (TREG) 细胞是粘膜稳态的关键介质,但它们如何无法抑制过度的 2 型 免疫应答 仍知之甚少。新生儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染就说明了这个问题,尽管肺部 TREG 细胞持续存在,但这种感染与以后的哮喘易感性密切相关。在这里,利用互补的小鼠新生儿 RSV 感染和过敏性气道炎症模型,我们确定了重新编程肺 TREG 细胞功能的 IL-33–IL-4Rα 信号轴。从机制上讲,IL-33 增加 IL-4Rα 表达,增强糖酵解依赖性 STAT6 信号传导并促进肺 TREG 细胞的 GATA3 依赖性功能特化。从功能上讲,IL-33 促进长寿命肺 TH2 细胞的扩增和持续存在,同时允许 IL-4 依赖性的 TREG 细胞抑制功能丧失。这些发现将 IL-33 确定为一个发育检查点,可协调编程致病性 TH2 记忆和过度致病性 2 型反应后功能失调的肺 TREG 细胞反应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解调节性 T (TREG) 细胞如何无法抑制过度的 2 型 免疫应答,特别是在新生儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染的情况下,尽管肺部 TREG 细胞持续存在,但 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染与以后的哮喘易感性密切相关。
设计与对象
该研究使用互补的小鼠新生儿 RSV 感染和过敏性气道炎症模型来研究 IL-33–IL-4Rα 信号转导在重编程肺 TREG 细胞功能中的作用。
核心方法
研究人员采用新生儿 RSV 感染和过敏性气道炎症的小鼠模型来鉴定 IL-33–IL-4Rα 信号轴及其对肺 TREG 细胞功能的影响。
主要结果
IL-33 增加 IL-4Rα 表达,增强糖酵解依赖性 STAT6 信号传导并促进肺 TREG 细胞的 GATA3 依赖性功能特化。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,IL-33 作为一个发育检查点,在过度的 2 型 免疫应答 后协调编程致病性 TH2 记忆和功能失调的肺部 TREG 细胞反应。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 TREG 细胞抑制 2 型炎症失败的机制的见解,这可以为未来的哮喘预防策略和 治疗 提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于小鼠模型,可能无法完全转化为人类条件,而且证据仅来自抽象数据,限制了研究结果的可信度。

IL-33 signaling uncouples pulmonary regulatory T-cell specialization from suppressive function during type 2 inflammation

Authors: Ciriaco Piccirillo, Fernando Alvarez, Lydia Labrie, Tho-Alfakar Al-Aubodah, Roman Isotomine, Katherine H. Restori, Jörg Hermann Fritz, Elizabeth D. Fixman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Regulatory T (TREG) cells are critical mediators of mucosal homeostasis, yet how they fail to restrain exaggerated type 2 immune responses remains poorly understood. This question is exemplified by neonatal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, which is strongly associated with asthma susceptibility later in life despite the persistent presence of pulmonary TREG cells. Here, using complementary murine neonatal RSV infection and allergic airway inflammation models, we identify an IL-33–IL-4Rα signaling axis that reprograms pulmonary TREG-cell function. Mechanistically, IL-33 increased IL-4Rα expression, enhancing glycolysis-dependent STAT6 signaling and promoting GATA3-dependent functional specialization of pulmonary TREG cells. Functionally, IL-33 promoted the expansion and persistence of long-lived pulmonary TH2 cells while simultaneously licensing IL-4-dependent loss of TREG-cell suppressive function. These findings identify IL-33 as a developmental checkpoint that coordinately programs pathogenic TH2 memory and dysfunctional pulmonary TREG cell responses following exaggerated pathogenic type 2 responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how regulatory T (TREG) cells fail to restrain exaggerated type 2 immune responses, particularly in the context of neonatal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, which is strongly associated with asthma susceptibility later in life despite the persistent presence of pulmonary TREG cells.
Design and population
The study used complementary murine neonatal RSV infection and allergic airway inflammation models to investigate the role of IL-33–IL-4Rα signaling in reprogramming pulmonary TREG-cell function.
Core methods
The researchers employed murine models of neonatal RSV infection and allergic airway inflammation to identify the IL-33–IL-4Rα signaling axis and its effects on pulmonary TREG-cell function.
Main results
IL-33 increased IL-4Rα expression, enhancing glycolysis-dependent STAT6 signaling and promoting GATA3-dependent functional specialization of pulmonary TREG cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that IL-33 acts as a developmental checkpoint that coordinately programs pathogenic TH2 memory and dysfunctional pulmonary TREG cell responses following exaggerated type 2 immune responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the mechanisms underlying the failure of TREG cells to suppress type 2 inflammation, which could inform future strategies for asthma prevention and treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength

作者： Elisabetta Groaz, Zihua Zheng, Brett L Hurst, Christine Burlein, Carolyn Bahnck-Teets, Kristine Devito, Peng Nie, Saurabh Maity, Jung-Ae Choi, Hoai T T Nguyen
摘要中文翻译大多数核苷 抗病毒药物 类似物模仿典型的 Watson-Crick 碱基配对,以增加其被复制酶识别和掺入不断生长的病毒核酸链的可能性。在此,我们研究了是否也可以利用基于形状互补或非氢键相互作用的非规范碱基识别原理来生成新型小分子 抗病毒药物。因此,7-脱氮腺苷的外源氨基被系统地替换为各种6-烷基和6-炔基取代基,以评估它们对抗病毒药物针对RNA呼吸道病毒(包括甲型和乙型流感、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)、中东呼吸综合征冠状病毒(MERS-CoV)和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV))活性的影响。多种化合物均具有亚微摩尔效力,且具有最小的细胞毒性和对 宿主 DNA 聚合酶的抑制作用。值得注意的是,不同的修饰深刻地影响了 抗病毒药物 谱,这表明尽管缺乏氢键供体,但具有 6-烷基或 6-炔基取代基的类似物可能通过不同的识别机制与聚合酶结合。

6-Substituted 7-Deazapurine Ribonucleosides Capable of Non-canonical Base Recognition as Inhibitors of Respiratory RNA Viruses.

Authors: Elisabetta Groaz, Zihua Zheng, Brett L Hurst, Christine Burlein, Carolyn Bahnck-Teets, Kristine Devito, Peng Nie, Saurabh Maity, Jung-Ae Choi, Hoai T T Nguyen
Original abstract (English)
Most nucleoside antiviral analogues mimic canonical Watson-Crick base pairing to increase their likelihood of recognition and incorporation by replicative enzymes into a growing viral nucleic acid strand. Herein, we investigated whether non-canonical base recognition principles based on either shape complementarity or non-hydrogen bond interactions could also be exploited to generate novel small-molecule antivirals. Thus, the exogenous amino group of 7-deazaadenosine was systematically replaced with various 6-alkyl and 6-alkynyl substituents to assess their impact on antiviral activity against RNA respiratory viruses, including influenza A and B, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), Middle East respiratory syndrome coronavirus (MERS-CoV), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Sub-micromolar potency was observed for multiple compounds, with minimal cytotoxicity and inhibition of host DNA polymerases. Notably, the different modifications profoundly affected the antiviral spectrum, suggesting that despite lacking a hydrogen-bond donor, analogues with either 6-alkyl or 6-alkynyl substituents likely engage polymerases through distinct recognition mechanisms.

The mRNA-based RSV vaccine mRNA-1345 induces robust and durable CD4 + T cell responses against RSV in younger and older adults
作者： Anthony DiPiazza, Emily Marcisak, Wen-Han Yu, Daniel Makrinos, Hsiao-Hsuan Kuo, Jenna Landy, Shannon McGrath, Xiaolin Chang, Maria Cavallaro, Christopher Wu

The mRNA-based RSV vaccine mRNA-1345 induces robust and durable CD4 + T cell responses against RSV in younger and older adults

Authors: Anthony DiPiazza, Emily Marcisak, Wen-Han Yu, Daniel Makrinos, Hsiao-Hsuan Kuo, Jenna Landy, Shannon McGrath, Xiaolin Chang, Maria Cavallaro, Christopher Wu
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a health threat, particularly for infants, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals. Although neutralizing antibodies can help limit infection and viral replication, T cells are essential for reducing disease severity. Assessment of age-related declines in T cell function are important for vaccine evaluation. We used intracellular cytokine staining, activation-induced marker, and Meso Scale Discovery assays and single-cell multiomic profiling to evaluate RSV vaccine mRNA-1345 responses in younger and older adults. mRNA-1345 induced comparable CD4 + and CD8 + T cell responses in both cohorts, with CD4 + responses persisting for 24 months. The CD4 + response was strongly T helper 1 (T H 1)–polarized, with increased interferon-γ, interleukin-2, and tumor necrosis factor–α and minimal T H 2 cytokines. Both CD4 + and CD8 + T cells showed increased polyfunctionality that remained elevated at 2 years. Single-cell and T cell receptor repertoire analyses showed similar Pre-F–specific phenotypes, transcriptional profiles, and clonal overlap across age groups, supporting robust, durable cellular immunity after mRNA-1345 vaccination. These findings indicate shared vaccine-elicited T cell immunity in adults.

Interpretation and novelty

用于离散 SARS-CoV-2 免疫复合物诊断的快速、灵敏的片上微毛细管电泳

Rapid and sensitive on-chip microcapillary electrophoresis for discrete SARS-CoV-2 immunocomplex diagnostics
作者： Gayathri Sureshkumar, To Thanh Truc Truong, Trung Nghia Tang, Tae Seok Seo
摘要中文翻译快速、灵敏和定量的分子诊断仍然具有持续的需求,推动了小型化分析平台的发展。传统的快速抗原测试依赖于侧流技术,需要捕获和检测抗体、金纳米颗粒标记以及精确的结合动力学来产生可见信号。微毛细管电泳 (μCE) 已广泛用于基因组 DNA 分离,但其在蛋白质分析方面的扩展仍然有限。在此,我们报道了一种快速分离和检测 SARS-CoV-2 免疫复合物的 μCE 方法,使用填充有 5.5% 线性聚丙烯酰胺的聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯微流控芯片作为筛分基质进行片上免疫分析。与侧流格式不同,我们的平台仅需要单个荧光标记抗体,可直接根据免疫复合物峰的存在或不存在读取阳性或阴性结果。将荧光标记的抗体与 SARS-CoV-2 核衣壳蛋白 (Np) 一起孵育,形成免疫复合物,根据电荷尺寸比改变引起的电泳迁移率差异,在 30 秒内将结合的复合物与游离抗体分离。通过荧光显微镜采集荧光信号,并使用自定义 Python 管道进行处理以进行电泳图分析和峰分析。该检测可解析 α、β 和 δ 变体中的 Np 抗原,并从临床口咽拭子样本中产生一致、可重复的信号,以最少的试剂消耗实现 5 μg/mL 的检测限。证实了针对一组常见呼吸道病原体、呼吸道合胞病毒、腺病毒或流感病毒的特异性,没有可见的免疫复合物峰,表明交叉反应性可以忽略不计。其亚分钟的分离时间以及与自动化的兼容性使该 μCE 平台成为分散式即时检测和传染病筛查的可扩展途径。

Rapid and sensitive on-chip microcapillary electrophoresis for discrete SARS-CoV-2 immunocomplex diagnostics

Authors: Gayathri Sureshkumar, To Thanh Truc Truong, Trung Nghia Tang, Tae Seok Seo
Original abstract (English)
Rapid, sensitive, and quantitative molecular diagnostics remain in sustained demand, driving the development of miniaturized analytical platforms. Conventional rapid antigen tests rely on lateral-flow techniques, requiring capture and detection antibodies, gold nanoparticle labels, and precise binding kinetics to generate a visible signal. Microcapillary electrophoresis (μCE) is well established for genomic DNA separation, yet its extension to protein analysis remains limited. Here we report a μCE method for rapid separation and detection of SARS-CoV-2 immunocomplexes, using a polymethyl methacrylate microfluidic chip filled with 5.5% linear polyacrylamide as the sieving matrix to perform an on-chip immunoassay. Unlike lateral-flow formats, our platform requires only a single fluorescently labeled antibody, with a positive or negative result read directly from the presence or absence of an immunocomplex peak. Fluorescently labeled antibodies were incubated with SARS-CoV-2 nucleocapsid protein (Np) to form immunocomplexes, the bound complex was resolved from free antibody within 30 s, based on the difference in electrophoretic mobility caused by altered charge-to-size ratio. Fluorescence signals were acquired by fluorescence microscopy and processed with a custom Python pipeline for electropherogram analysis and peak profiling. The assay resolved Np antigen across the alpha, beta, and delta variants and produced consistent, reproducible signals from clinical oropharyngeal swab specimens, achieving a detection limit of 5 μg/mL with minimal reagent consumption. Specificity was confirmed against a panel of common respiratory pathogens, respiratory syncytial virus, adenovirus, or influenza virus, with no visible immunocomplex peak indicating negligible cross-reactivity. Its sub-minute separation time and compatibility with automation position this μCE platform as a scalable route to decentralized point-of-care testing and screening for infectious diseases.
