TRIM69 抑制细胞 AMPK 途径以抑制病毒复制

TRIM69 Suppresses the Cellular AMPK Pathway to Inhibit Viral Replication
作者： Gayatri Subramanian, Manoj Veleeparambil, DaNaylee A. Long, Ritu Chakravarti, Saurabh Chattopadhyay
摘要中文翻译干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 通过针对感染所需的病毒成分和细胞途径来启动 抗病毒药物 防御。在这里,我们发现干扰素和病毒诱导的 抗病毒药物 因子 TRIM69 抑制细胞 AMP 激活蛋白激酶 (AMPK) 信号传导并限制 RNA 和 DNA 病毒的复制。TRIM69 的异位表达可抑制仙台病毒 (SeV) 诱导的自噬,并抑制 AMPK 及其下游靶标 ULK1 的磷酸化,以应对病毒感染和营养缺乏。与抑制这种前病毒 AMPK 信号通路一致,TRIM69 表达强烈限制副粘病毒和肺病毒的复制,包括 SeV、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、人副流感病毒 3 型 (HPIV3) 和疱疹病毒,例如单纯疱疹病毒 1 型 (HSV-1)。与这些发现一致,内源 TRIM69 的敲低增强了病毒复制,证明了 TRIM69 的生理学 抗病毒药物 作用。除了抑制病毒复制外,TRIM69还支持抗病毒药物基因的表达,包括IFNB1、IFIT2和IFIT3,表明先天抗病毒药物信号传导的放大。总之,这些发现将 TRIM69 确定为一种广泛的 抗病毒药物 ISG,其靶向细胞 AMPK 通路,并有助于 宿主 先天 免疫应答 对抗病毒。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过针对病毒感染所需的细胞途径来研究 TRIM69 在 抗病毒药物 防御中的作用,解决了解 ISG 如何促进 宿主 先天 免疫应答 对抗病毒的需要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 TRIM69 的异位表达和细胞系中内源 TRIM69 的敲低来检查对病毒复制和细胞信号传导途径的影响。
核心方法
TRIM69 异位表达,内源 TRIM69 被敲低,以评估其对病毒复制和细胞 AMPK 信号传导的影响。检测包括监测自噬、AMPK 磷酸化以及响应病毒感染和营养缺乏的病毒复制。
主要结果
TRIM69 抑制仙台病毒诱导的自噬并抑制 AMPK 磷酸化及其下游靶标 ULK1。TRIM69 强烈限制副粘病毒、肺病毒和疱疹病毒的复制,包括仙台病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、人类副流感病毒 3 型和单纯疱疹病毒 1 型。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,TRIM69 以细胞 AMPK 通路为目标来限制病毒复制,为 ISG 介导的 抗病毒药物 防御提供了一种新机制。
科研与公卫意义
TRIM69支持抗病毒药物基因的表达并放大先天性抗病毒药物信号传导,这可以为开发增强宿主先天性免疫应答抗病毒的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到细胞系使用和体内模型缺乏的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏随机对照试验和动物模型,证据强度中等。

TRIM69 Suppresses the Cellular AMPK Pathway to Inhibit Viral Replication

Authors: Gayatri Subramanian, Manoj Veleeparambil, DaNaylee A. Long, Ritu Chakravarti, Saurabh Chattopadhyay
Original abstract (English)
Interferon-stimulated genes (ISGs) mount an antiviral defense by targeting both viral components and cellular pathways required for infection. Here, we show that the interferon- and virus-inducible antiviral factor TRIM69 suppresses cellular AMP-activated protein kinase (AMPK) signaling and restricts replication of both RNA and DNA viruses. Ectopic expression of TRIM69 inhibited Sendai virus (SeV)-induced autophagy and suppressed phosphorylation of AMPK and its downstream target, ULK1, in response to viral infection and nutrient deprivation. Consistent with inhibition of this proviral AMPK signaling pathway, TRIM69 expression strongly restricted replication of paramyxoviruses and pneumoviruses, including SeV, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), human parainfluenza virus type 3 (HPIV3), and herpesviruses, e.g., herpes simplex virus type 1 (HSV-1). Consistent with these findings, knockdown of endogenous TRIM69 enhanced viral replication, demonstrating a physiological antiviral role for TRIM69. In addition to suppressing viral replication, TRIM69 supported the expression of antiviral genes, including IFNB1, IFIT2, and IFIT3, suggesting amplification of the innate antiviral signaling. Together, these findings identify TRIM69 as a broad antiviral ISG that targets the cellular AMPK pathway and contributes to host innate immune responses against viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of TRIM69 in antiviral defense by targeting cellular pathways required for viral infection, addressing the need to understand how ISGs contribute to host innate immune responses against viruses.
Design and population
The study involved the use of ectopic expression of TRIM69 and knockdown of endogenous TRIM69 in cell lines to examine the effects on viral replication and cellular signaling pathways.
Core methods
TRIM69 was ectopically expressed and endogenous TRIM69 was knocked down to assess its impact on viral replication and cellular AMPK signaling. Assays included monitoring of autophagy, AMPK phosphorylation, and viral replication in response to viral infection and nutrient deprivation.
Main results
TRIM69 inhibited Sendai virus-induced autophagy and suppressed AMPK phosphorylation and its downstream target ULK1. TRIM69 strongly restricted replication of paramyxoviruses, pneumoviruses, and herpesviruses, including Sendai virus, respiratory syncytial virus, human parainfluenza virus type 3, and herpes simplex virus type 1.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that TRIM69 targets the cellular AMPK pathway to restrict viral replication, providing a novel mechanism for ISG-mediated antiviral defense.
Scientific and public-health significance
TRIM69 supports the expression of antiviral genes and amplifies innate antiviral signaling, which could inform the development of therapeutic strategies to enhance host innate immune responses against viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of cell lines and the absence of in vivo models, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of randomized controlled trials and animal models.

抗病毒药物 治疗结果图集:病毒生物学如何影响 治疗 的影响范围

An Atlas of Antiviral Therapeutic Outcomes: How Viral Biology Shapes the Reach of Treatment
作者： Jithesh Othayoth, Jayant Singh Bist, Pradeep Bist
摘要中文翻译抗病毒药物 抑制不会产生单一的治疗终点:一些感染可以在有限的 治疗 后治愈,另一些感染需要持久抑制,而另一些感染仍然难以影响,因为可操作阶段短暂或在解剖学上难以接近。这些不同的结果反映了保持感染或允许复发的生物学状态、药物敏感过程仍然具有因果重要性的时间、相应病毒或病毒物质驻留的解剖空间以及病毒群体在治疗压力下进化的能力。在这里,我们研究了可治愈、可抑制、潜伏、急性、神经侵袭性和急性后病毒感染之间的关系。HCV、HIV-1、HBV、HSV、流感、RSV、登革热、SARS-CoV-2、埃博拉病毒、CHIKV 和其他系统之间的比较表明,持久性架构、诊断时机、组织药理学、宿主 背景和耐药性如何决定 宿主 活性是否转化为病毒学治愈、持久抑制、复发控制、预防或不完全治疗控制。人类挑战研究、结果不一致的临床项目以及耐药性研究进一步说明了机械活性和治疗充分性之间的分歧。在这些例子中,抗病毒药物 的结果最终取决于 治疗 是否能够拦截决定感染后续命运的生物状态。

An Atlas of Antiviral Therapeutic Outcomes: How Viral Biology Shapes the Reach of Treatment

Authors: Jithesh Othayoth, Jayant Singh Bist, Pradeep Bist
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral inhibition does not produce a single therapeutic endpoint: some infections can be cured after finite treatment, others require durable suppression, and others remain difficult to influence because the actionable phase is brief or anatomically inaccessible. These different outcomes reflect the biological state that preserves infection or permits return, the time during which a drug-sensitive process remains causally important, the anatomical space in which consequential virus or viral material resides, and the capacity of viral populations to evolve under therapeutic pressure. Here, we examine these relationships across curable, suppressible, latent, acute, neuroinvasive, and post-acute viral infections. Comparisons among HCV, HIV-1, HBV, HSV, influenza, RSV, dengue, SARS-CoV-2, Ebola virus, CHIKV, and other systems show how persistence architecture, diagnostic timing, tissue pharmacology, host context, and resistance can determine whether antiviral activity translates into virological cure, durable suppression, recurrence control, prevention, or incomplete therapeutic control. Human challenge studies, clinical programs with discordant outcomes, and resistance studies further illustrate where mechanistic activity and therapeutic sufficiency diverge. Across these examples, antiviral outcome depends ultimately on whether treatment can intercept the biological state that determines the subsequent fate of infection.

Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.

在呼吸道疾病流行期,废水监测Z是否具有预测价值?基于底特律市临床、废水和数字搜索数据的分析

Is Wastewater Surveillance Predictive during the Endemic Phase of Respiratory Disease? An Analysis Based on Clinical, Wastewater, and Digital Search Data in Detroit, Michigan
作者： Liang Zhao, Niloofar Gohari, R. Kelly, Pankaj Bhatt, Michael J. Swain, Russell A. Faust, John W. Norton, Irene Xagoraraki
摘要中文翻译基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 已成为 COVID-19 大流行催化的一项重大公共卫生创新。然而,在 COVID-19 流行期间和之后,其潜力仍未得到充分开发。本研究评估了 WBE 识别 COVID-19 大流行向地方流行转变的潜力,并在大流行后时代跨多个 监测 系统针对甲型流感 (IAV) 和乙型流感 (IBV)、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 提供早期预警。我们从 2020 年 4 月 8 日到 2025 年 7 月 31 日监测了废水中的 SARS-CoV-2 N1 浓度,生成了密歇根州底特律最早且运行时间最长的废水数据集。采用峰值识别方法,通过比较各阶段之间 N1 浓度峰值的频率来识别大流行到地方病的转变。在流行阶段,从2022年10月1日到2025年3月31日,我们监测了底特律废水中的RSV、IAV、IBV和SARS-CoV-2。采用皮尔逊相关系数来量化废水浓度与临床、症状和数字流行病学数据之间的关联。时滞互相关 (TLCC) 用于检查这些数据集之间的时间动态,并确定每种疾病和时滞的最早出现的数据。我们进行了广泛的文献研究,以阐明每种疾病的时滞机制,包括 TLCC 结果。基于 WBE 数据集建立随机森林模型来预测临床病例。这是首批使用基于 WBE 的方法来识别 SARS-CoV-2 从大流行向地方流行转变的研究之一。在这些呼吸道疾病的流行阶段,系统地分析了废水数据与传统临床、症状和数字流行病学 监测 数据的关系。这项研究证明了 WBE 在流行阶段的预测价值,为季节性呼吸道疾病(包括 COVID-19、流感和流感样疾病)提供早期预警和预测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 已成为 COVID-19 大流行催化的一项重大公共卫生创新。然而,在 COVID-19 流行期间和之后,其潜力仍未得到充分开发。
设计与对象
本研究评估了WBE在识别SARS-CoV-2疫情向地方性流行转变以及在后疫情时代对流感A(IAV)和B(IBV)、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)和SARS-CoV-2等多种监测系统中提供早期预警的潜力。我们从2020年4月8日到2025年7月31日监测了底特律市废水中的SARS-CoV-2 N1浓度,生成了该市最早的、持续时间最长的废水数据集。
核心方法
采用皮尔逊相关系数来量化废水浓度与临床、症状和数字流行病学数据之间的关联。时滞互相关 (TLCC) 用于检查这些数据集之间的时间动态,并确定每种疾病和时滞的最早出现的数据。我们进行了广泛的文献研究,以阐明每种疾病的时滞机制,包括 TLCC 结果。
主要结果
在流行阶段,从2022年10月1日到2025年3月31日,我们监测了底特律废水中的RSV、IAV、IBV和SARS-CoV-2。
解释与创新
这项研究证明了 WBE 在流行阶段的预测价值,为季节性呼吸道疾病(包括 COVID-19、流感和流感样疾病)提供早期预警和预测。
科研与公卫意义
本研究系统分析了这些呼吸道疾病流行阶段废水数据与传统临床、症状和数字流行病学 监测 数据的关系。
局限与证据强度

Is Wastewater Surveillance Predictive during the Endemic Phase of Respiratory Disease? An Analysis Based on Clinical, Wastewater, and Digital Search Data in Detroit, Michigan

Authors: Liang Zhao, Niloofar Gohari, R. Kelly, Pankaj Bhatt, Michael J. Swain, Russell A. Faust, John W. Norton, Irene Xagoraraki
Original abstract (English)
Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) has emerged as a major public health innovation catalyzed by the COVID-19 pandemic. However, its potential remains underexplored during and after COVID-19’s transition to endemicity. This study evaluates WBE’s potential to identify the COVID-19 pandemic-to-endemic transition and to provide early warnings across multiple surveillance systems for influenza A (IAV) and B (IBV), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 in the postpandemic era. We monitored SARS-CoV-2 N1 concentrations in wastewater from April 8, 2020, and July 31, 2025, generating the earliest and longest-running wastewater dataset in Detroit, MI. A peak identification method was implemented to identify the pandemic-to-endemic transition by comparing the frequency of N1 concentration peaks between phases. During the endemic phase, from October 1, 2022, and March 31, 2025, we monitored RSV, IAV, IBV, and SARS-CoV-2 in Detroit’s wastewater. Pearson correlations were implemented to quantify the associations between wastewater concentrations and clinical, syndromic, and digital epidemiological data. Time-lagged cross-correlation (TLCC) was used to examine temporal dynamics among these datasets and identify the earliest emerging data for each disease and time lags. Extensive literature studies were conducted to elucidate the time-lag mechanisms for each disease, embracing the TLCC results. Random forest models were established to predict clinical cases based on WBE datasets. This was among the first studies using WBE-based approaches to identify pandemic-to-endemic transition of SARS-CoV-2. The relationships of wastewater data to traditional clinical, syndromic, and digital epidemiological surveillance data were systematically analyzed during the endemic phase of these respiratory diseases. This study demonstrates the predictive value of WBE during the endemic phase, providing early warnings and predictions for seasonal respiratory diseases, including COVID-19, influenza, and flu-like disease.

0-18岁儿童呼吸道病原体概况:单中心经验(2024年10月-2025年6月)

Respiratory pathogen profiles in children aged 0–18 years: a single-center experience (October 2024 – June 2025)
作者： Fatma Kılınç, Ramazan Köklü, Nisa SİPAHİ, Fatma Erdoğan, Zehra TÜRKMEN
摘要中文翻译区分活动性小儿呼吸道感染与上呼吸道定植并根据常规 宿主 实验室标志物解释病因仍然是一项重大的临床挑战。这项研究评估了儿科呼吸道病原体的检测谱,并检查了与常规炎症和血液生物标志物的探索性关联。2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 6 月期间,对 784 例 PCR 阳性儿科病例(0-18 岁)进行了 监测 回顾性研究。评估了病原体谱、时间动态、联合检测模式以及与 C 反应蛋白 (CRP)、白蛋白、绝对中性粒细胞计数 (ANC) 和绝对淋巴细胞计数 (ALC) 的关联。多重 PCR 显示多态性,共检测率为 51.7%。病毒占主导地位(60.5%),其中鼻病毒/肠道病毒(32.1%)、流感病毒(26.1%)、RSV(16.6%)和腺病毒(14.8%)为首。肺炎链球菌 (38.9%) 和流感嗜血杆菌 (31.6%) 是扩展小组测试中最常见的细菌目标。流感检测与中位 ANC 显着降低相关 (p < 0.001),而腺病毒则显示 CRP 和 ANC 升高 (p < 0.001)。肺炎支原体表现出 CRP 升高和相对淋巴细胞减少 (p < 0.001)。肺炎链球菌检测与 CRP 升高无显着相关性 (p = 0.181),而流感嗜血杆菌检测与较高的中位 CRP 水平相关 (p < 0.001)。常规 宿主 生物标志物表现出病毒和细菌 PCR 检测组之间不同的观察关联。然而,鼻咽标本上共生细菌的分子检测必须与全身炎症标志物一起谨慎解释,以避免将活动性疾病过度归因于简单的定植。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决区分活动性小儿呼吸道感染和上呼吸道定植以及从常规 宿主 实验室标志物解释病因的临床挑战。
设计与对象
监测 于 2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 6 月期间对 784 例 PCR 阳性儿科病例(0-18 岁)进行了回顾性研究。
核心方法
使用多重 PCR 评估病原体谱、时间动态、联合检测模式以及与 C 反应蛋白 (CRP)、白蛋白、绝对中性粒细胞计数 (ANC) 和绝对淋巴细胞计数 (ALC) 的关联。
主要结果
病毒占主导地位(60.5%),其中鼻病毒/肠道病毒(32.1%)、流感病毒(26.1%)、RSV(16.6%)和腺病毒(14.8%)最常见。还检测到肺炎衣原体(38.9%)和流感嗜血杆菌(31.6%)。流感检测与中位 ANC 显着降低相关 (p < 0.001),而腺病毒则显示 CRP 和 ANC 升高 (p < 0.001)。肺炎链球菌表现出 CRP 升高和相对淋巴细胞减少 (p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
该研究发现,病毒和细菌 PCR 检测组中常规 宿主 生物标志物存在明显的观察关联,这凸显了对共生细菌与全身炎症标志物进行谨慎解释的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 治疗儿科呼吸道感染的策略提供参考,特别是在区分活动性感染和定植方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和 PCR 检测中潜在的偏倚而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Respiratory pathogen profiles in children aged 0–18 years: a single-center experience (October 2024 – June 2025)

Authors: Fatma Kılınç, Ramazan Köklü, Nisa SİPAHİ, Fatma Erdoğan, Zehra TÜRKMEN
Original abstract (English)
Distinguishing active pediatric respiratory infection from upper respiratory tract colonization and interpreting etiology from routine host laboratory markers remains a significant clinical challenge. This study evaluated the detection spectrum of pediatric respiratory pathogens and examined exploratory associations with routine inflammatory and hematological biomarkers. A retrospective surveillance of 784 PCR-positive pediatric cases (aged 0–18 years) was conducted between October 2024 and June 2025. Pathogen profiles, temporal dynamics, codetection patterns, and associations with C-reactive protein (CRP), albumin, absolute neutrophil count (ANC), and absolute lymphocyte count (ALC) were evaluated. Multiplex PCR revealed a polymorphic landscape with a 51.7% codetection rate. Viruses predominated (60.5%), led by rhinovirus/enterovirus (32.1%), influenza viruses (26.1%), RSV (16.6%), and adenovirus (14.8%). pneumoniae (38.9%) and H. influenzae (31.6%) were the most prevalent bacterial targets among expanded panel tests. Influenza detections were associated with significantly lower median ANC ( p < 0.001), whereas adenovirus showed elevated CRP and ANC ( p < 0.001). Mycoplasma pneumoniae exhibited elevated CRP and relative lymphopenia ( p < 0.001). pneumoniae detection showed no significant correlation with CRP elevation ( p = 0.181), whereas H. influenzae was associated with higher median CRP levels ( p < 0.001). Routine host biomarkers demonstrate distinct observational associations across viral and bacterial PCR detection groups. However, molecular detection of commensal bacteria on nasopharyngeal specimens must be interpreted cautiously alongside systemic inflammatory markers to avoid overattributing active disease to simple colonization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the clinical challenge of distinguishing active pediatric respiratory infections from upper respiratory tract colonization and interpreting etiology from routine host laboratory markers.
Design and population
A retrospective surveillance of 784 PCR-positive pediatric cases (aged 0–18 years) was conducted between October 2024 and June 2025.
Core methods
Multiplex PCR was used to evaluate pathogen profiles, temporal dynamics, codetection patterns, and associations with C-reactive protein (CRP), albumin, absolute neutrophil count (ANC), and absolute lymphocyte count (ALC).
Main results
Viruses predominated (60.5%), with rhinovirus/enterovirus (32.1%), influenza viruses (26.1%), RSV (16.6%), and adenovirus (14.8%) being the most common. pneumoniae (38.9%) and H. influenzae (31.6%) were also detected. Influenza detections were associated with significantly lower median ANC ( p < 0.001), while adenovirus showed elevated CRP and ANC ( p < 0.001). pneumoniae exhibited elevated CRP and relative lymphopenia ( p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found distinct observational associations of routine host biomarkers across viral and bacterial PCR detection groups, highlighting the need for cautious interpretation of commensal bacteria alongside systemic inflammatory markers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for pediatric respiratory infections, particularly in distinguishing active infections from colonization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the potential for bias in PCR detection. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

从概念到临床试验:用新的疫苗开始临床试验

From Concept to Clinical Trial: Starting Clinical Trials with a New Vaccine
作者： Stephen P. Lockhart, Michele Sausser, William C. Gruber
摘要中文翻译摘要 疫苗的开发很复杂,每一步都需要考虑很多事情。疫苗 需要解决未满足的医疗需求,该需求可以在关键的 3 期研究中进行衡量。目标产品概况 (TPP) 和临床开发计划 (CDP) 对于规划至关重要。需要质量管理体系 (QMS),以及基于国际人用药品技术要求协调委员会 (ICH) 指导的标准操作程序 (SOP),并符合国家法律和法规。研究者手册 (IB) 向研究者和现场工作人员通报研究 疫苗 的临床方面。研究药品 (IMP) 必须在质量体系内生产和发布,并且必须生成毒理学数据。临床试验方案 (CTP) 中涵盖了首次人体 (FIH) 1 期 疫苗 临床试验的设计有多种选择。我们比较了最近关于 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 提供融合前 F 抗原的 FIH 研究,以说明一些选择。一旦协议最终确定,就必须准备知情同意文件 (ICD)、现场管理计划和其他与研究相关的文件。必须提交监管和伦理委员会的申请并获得批准,并准备好临床试验地点。FIH 研究为进入更大规模的临床试验提供了医学和财务理由,因此通常是在快速得出结果的压力下进行的,但也必须满足严格的道德、安全和质量标准。

From Concept to Clinical Trial: Starting Clinical Trials with a New Vaccine

Authors: Stephen P. Lockhart, Michele Sausser, William C. Gruber
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Vaccine development is complex, and there are many things that need to be taken into consideration at every step. The vaccine needs to address an unmet medical need that can be measured in a pivotal phase 3 study. A Target Product Profile (TPP) and a Clinical Development Plan (CDP) are critical for planning. A Quality Management System (QMS) is required, with standard operating procedures (SOP) based on International Council for Harmonisation of Technical Requirements for Pharmaceuticals for Human Use (ICH) guidance and compliant with national laws and regulations. The Investigator Brochure (IB) informs investigators and site staff about clinical aspects of the study vaccine. Investigational Medicinal Product (IMP) must be manufactured and released, and toxicology data must be generated, all within quality systems. There are many options for the design of a first-in-human (FIH) phase 1 vaccine clinical trial to be covered in the Clinical Trial Protocol (CTP). We compare recent FIH studies of respiratory syncytial virus vaccines delivering prefusion F antigen to illustrate some options. Once the protocol is final, Informed Consent Documents (ICD), site management plans and other study-related documents must be prepared. Regulatory and ethics committee applications must be submitted and approved, and clinical trial sites prepared. The FIH study provides medical and financial justification for progressing into larger clinical trials, so it is usually performed under pressure to deliver rapid results but also must meet rigorous ethical, safety and quality standards.

Main results

Interpretation and novelty
Once the protocol is final, Informed Consent Documents (ICD), site management plans and other study-related documents must be prepared.
Scientific and public-health significance
Regulatory and ethics committee applications must be submitted and approved, and clinical trial sites prepared.
Limitations and evidence strength
The FIH study provides medical and financial justification for progressing into larger clinical trials, so it is usually performed under pressure to deliver rapid results but also must meet rigorous ethical, safety and quality standards.

使用开源流行病情报数据的地理空间分析对加利福尼亚州里德利的非法实验室进行风险评估

Risk Assessment of an Illegal Laboratory in Reedley, California, Using Geospatial Analysis of Open‐Source Epidemic Intelligence Data
作者： Muluneh A, Dawson R, Edgeworth J, Cain N, Quigly A, Moa A, Lim S, MacIntyre C
摘要中文翻译这项研究旨在利用开源流行病情报数据来估计里德利非法实验室周围的流行病信​​号。2方法2.1数据来源与处理本研究使用EPIWATCH疫情暴发系统的开源情报数据来排查与非法实验室相关的可能疾病暴发。2023)。为了评估是否有任何 暴发 疾病可能与里德利非法实验室有关,我们使用了 2022 年 10 月实验室成立前后期间加利福尼亚州和更广泛的美国的 暴发 数据(特别委员会 2023)。我们纳入了研究期间的所有 暴发 信号。在研究期间报告的 19,233 个 暴发 信号中,33.3% 是没有州级位置数据的国家级报告,25.2% 是州级报告,41.5% 比州级更精细(图 1)。然后以两种方式分析每个数据集(加利福尼亚州或整个美国)。这些是在两年研究期间的时间序列上相互绘制的。我们分析了非法实验室中记录的病原体的趋势,以及美国和加利福尼亚州非法实验室中未记录的病原体的趋势,以比较每个类别内部和之间的趋势。摘要 2022 年 12 月在加利福尼亚州里德利的一个仓库中发现了一个非法实验室,该实验室无证经营,处理了约 1000 只转基因小鼠、化学品以及可能超过 20 种病原体,其中包括标有埃博拉病毒的小瓶,这些小瓶从未经过测试但被销毁。我们利用了 EPIWATCH 2021 年 10 月至 2023 年 10 月(非法实验室成立前一年和后一年)的数据。使用热点分析来分析 暴发 信号的空间模式,以及...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究旨在利用开源流行病情报数据来估计里德利非法实验室周围的流行病信​​号。Gi * 值根据美国或加利福尼亚州的 暴发 信号进行分类:热点(信号数量高于预期)、冷点(信号数量低于预期)和不显着(观察到的和预期的 暴发 信号数量相当),基于 90%、95% 和 99% 置信区间(Ord 和 Getis 2010)。
设计与对象
该研究使用了对 2021 年 10 月至 2023 年 10 月加州和美国开源流行病情报数据的地理空间分析。搜索词包括里德利非法实验室提到的所有可能的病原体(特别委员会 2023;Nava 2023)。
核心方法
使用热点分析对暴发信号的空间模式进行分析,并测量暴发信号与实验室的距离。使用 R tidyverse 软件包和 MS Excel 进行数据管理、处理和清理;图表是使用 ggplot 制作的,地图是使用 ArcGIS 制作的。我们分析了非法实验室中记录的病原体的趋势,以及美国和加利福尼亚州非法实验室中未记录的病原体的趋势,以比较每个类别内部和之间的趋势。
主要结果
在研究期间报告的 19,233 个 暴发 信号中,33.3% 是没有州级位置数据的国家级报告,25.2% 是州级报告,41.5% 比州级更精细(图 1)。在2022年10月里德利非法实验室成立后的一段时间内,全美报告的与该非法实验室相关的病原体暴发在2022年12月、2023年6月至2023年8月和2023年10月略有增加(图2)。在非法实验室开始运营之前和之后,距离 Reedley 非法实验室最近的 暴发 信号分别为 41 公里和 33 公里(图 5)。
解释与创新
2023 年 10 月由实验室相关病原体引起的疾病报告增加,暂时与 2023 年末加州报告的当地登革热病例有关(加州公共卫生部 2025 年,Sutter-Yuba Mosquito 和 媒介 控制区 2025 年)。非法实验室建立后一年内加州或美国报告的疾病模式变化可能与非法实验室有关,也可能无关。
科研与公卫意义
早期预警系统(例如 EPIWATCH)有可能比传统的 暴发 方法更快地发现异常的传染病 暴发,并且在正式数据不可用时。开源数据的可用性并不取决于实验室事件是否已正式报告给国内或国际当局。
局限与证据强度
在没有任何正式测试、报告或数据的情况下,但知道一名工作人员及其孩子生病了,我们已经证明 EPIWATCH 可用于分析该地区是否报告了任何异常的流行病模式。提供的证据没有报告任何正式的测试或数据来确认非法实验室与观察到的疾病模式之间的因果关系。

Risk Assessment of an Illegal Laboratory in Reedley, California, Using Geospatial Analysis of Open‐Source Epidemic Intelligence Data

Authors: Muluneh A, Dawson R, Edgeworth J, Cain N, Quigly A, Moa A, Lim S, MacIntyre C
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to estimate epidemic signals around the Reedley illegal laboratory using open‐source epidemic intelligence data. 2Methods2.1Data Source and ProcessingOpen‐source intelligence data from the EPIWATCH epidemic surveillance system was used in this study to examine possible disease outbreaks associated with the illegal laboratory. 2023).To evaluate if any outbreaks of disease may be associated with the Reedley illegal laboratory, we used outbreak data for California specifically and the US more broadly for the period before and after the establishment of the laboratory in October 2022 (The Select Committee 2023). We included all outbreak signals within the study period. Of the 19,233 outbreak signals reported in the study period, 33.3% were country‐level reports without state‐level location data, 25.2% were state‐level reports, and 41.5% were finer than the state level (Figure 1).Each dataset (California or the whole US) was then analyzed in two ways. These were plotted against each other on a time series over the 2‐year study period.We analyzed the trends of pathogens documented in the illegal laboratory, along with those not documented in the illegal laboratory in the US and California, to compare trends within and across each category. ABSTRACTAn illegal laboratory discovered in Reedley, California, in December 2022 was in a warehouse, operating without a license, handling about a thousand transgenic mice, chemicals, and possibly over 20 pathogens, including vials labeled Ebola, which were never tested but destroyed. We utilized data from EPIWATCH, from October 2021 to October 2023, a year prior to and a year after the establishment of the illegal laboratory. The spatial pattern of outbreak signals was analyzed using hotspot analysis, and the distances o...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to estimate epidemic signals around the Reedley illegal laboratory using open-source epidemic intelligence data. The Gi * value is classified on the basis of the outbreak signals in the US or California: hotspots (a higher number of signals than expected), cold spots (a lower number of signals than expected), and nonsignificant (with comparable numbers of observed and expected outbreak signals), based on 90%, 95%, and 99% confidence intervals (Ord and Getis 2010).
Design and population
The study used geospatial analysis of open-source epidemic intelligence data for California and the US from October 2021 to October 2023. Search terms included all possible pathogens mentioned at the Reedley illegal laboratory (The Select Committee 2023; Nava 2023).
Core methods
The spatial pattern of outbreak signals was analyzed using hotspot analysis, and the distances of outbreak signals from the laboratory were measured. Data curation, processing, and cleaning utilized R tidyverse packages and MS Excel; graphs were made using ggplot, and maps were made using ArcGIS. We analyzed the trends of pathogens documented in the illegal laboratory, along with those not documented in the illegal laboratory in the US and California, to compare trends within and across each category.
Main results
Of the 19,233 outbreak signals reported in the study period, 33.3% were country-level reports without state-level location data, 25.2% were state-level reports, and 41.5% were finer than the state level (Figure 1). In the period after the establishment of the Reedley illegal laboratory in October 2022, reported outbreaks of pathogens associated with the illegal laboratory showed a slight increase in December 2022, June to August 2023, and October 2023 across the US (Figure 2). The nearest outbreak signals from the Reedley illegal laboratory are ∼41 and 33 km away, before and after the illegal laboratory started operating, respectively (Figure 5).
Interpretation and novelty
The October 2023 increase in reports of disease due to pathogens associated with the laboratory is temporally related to locally acquired cases of dengue being reported in California in late 2023 (California Department of Public Health 2025, Sutter-Yuba Mosquito and Vector Control District 2025). Changes in disease patterns reported in California or the US in the year following the establishment of the illegal laboratory may or may not be linked to the illegal laboratory.
Scientific and public-health significance
Early warning systems, such as EPIWATCH, have the potential to detect unusual outbreaks of infectious disease much sooner than traditional surveillance methods, and when formal data are unavailable. The availability of open-source data does not depend on whether a laboratory incident has been formally reported to domestic or international authorities.
Limitations and evidence strength
In the absence of any formal testing, reporting or data, but knowing that a staff member and their child became ill, we have shown that EPIWATCH can be used to analyze whether any unusual epidemic patterns were reported in the area. The supplied evidence does not report any formal testing or data to confirm a causal link between the illegal laboratory and the observed disease patterns.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

马来西亚公立医院通过多重 PCR 检测到的呼吸道病原体:患病率 和 2023 年 1 月至 2024 年 7 月的流行病学模式。

Respiratory pathogens detected by multiplex PCR in Malaysian public hospitals: prevalence and epidemiologic patterns from January 2023 until July 2024.
作者： Mahirah Kamil Puat, Valerie Siau Mei Ting, H. Baharim, A. Othman, Dayangku Seritul Akmar Abd Razak, A. T. Abdulrahman, F. Abdullah, S. H. Hamzah, A. S. Mohd Ali, Yuhin Karina Yusoff
摘要中文翻译呼吸道感染仍然是住院和诊断不确定性的重要原因,多重聚合酶链反应检测可以在常规临床实践中快速检测多种病原体。我们的目的是描述马来西亚公立医院 QIAstat-Dx 呼吸道 SARS-CoV-2 检测组检测到的呼吸道病原体的 患病率、人口统计学关联和时间趋势。这项多中心回顾性横断面研究包括 2023 年 1 月 1 日至 2024 年 7 月 31 日期间检测的疑似急性呼吸道感染病例的鼻咽拭子样本。使用标准化记录表格从参与地点收集数据并进行分析。我们评估了总体阳性率、病原体频率、年龄组和区域分布以及月度和季度趋势。共纳入 8,361 个样本,其中 5,252 个样本(62.8%)至少检测到一种病原体。在 4,279 个样本 (51.2%) 中发现了单一检测,而在 973 个样本 (11.6%) 中发现了联合检测。病毒病原体占主导地位。鼻病毒/肠道病毒是最常检测到的病原体(23.8%),其次是呼吸道合胞病毒 A/B(13.7%)和甲型H1N1/2009流感(4.8%)。1至4岁儿童和1至11个月婴儿的阳性率最高,且与年龄相关的差异比区域差异更明显。主要病原体还表现出不同的时间特征,在整个研究期间具有不同的高峰月份。总之,该医院人群的呼吸道病原体检测具有阳性率高、病毒病原体占主导地位、明显的年龄相关差异和不同的时间模式的特点。这些研究结果支持了马来西亚公立医院呼吸道病原体 监测 多重 PCR 的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们的目的是描述马来西亚公立医院 QIAstat-Dx 呼吸道 SARS-CoV-2 检测组检测到的呼吸道病原体的 患病率、人口统计学关联和时间趋势。
设计与对象
这项多中心回顾性横断面研究包括2023年1月1日至2024年7月31日期间检测的疑似急性呼吸道感染病例的鼻咽拭子样本。
核心方法
使用标准化记录表格从参与地点收集数据并进行分析。
主要结果
主要病原体还表现出不同的时间特征,在整个研究期间具有不同的高峰月份。
解释与创新
总之,该医院人群的呼吸道病原体检测具有阳性率高、病毒病原体占主导地位、明显的年龄相关差异和不同的时间模式的特点。
科研与公卫意义
呼吸道感染仍然是住院和诊断不确定性的重要原因,多重聚合酶链反应检测可以在常规临床实践中快速检测多种病原体。
局限与证据强度
1至4岁儿童和1至11个月婴儿的阳性率最高,且与年龄相关的差异比区域差异更明显。

Respiratory pathogens detected by multiplex PCR in Malaysian public hospitals: prevalence and epidemiologic patterns from January 2023 until July 2024.

Authors: Mahirah Kamil Puat, Valerie Siau Mei Ting, H. Baharim, A. Othman, Dayangku Seritul Akmar Abd Razak, A. T. Abdulrahman, F. Abdullah, S. H. Hamzah, A. S. Mohd Ali, Yuhin Karina Yusoff
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory tract infections remain an important cause of hospitalisation and diagnostic uncertainty, and multiplex polymerase chain reaction testing offers rapid detection of multiple pathogens in routine clinical practice. We aimed to describe the prevalence, demographic associations, and temporal trends of respiratory pathogens detected by the QIAstat-Dx Respiratory SARS-CoV-2 Panel in Malaysian public hospitals. This multicentre retrospective cross-sectional study included nasopharyngeal swab samples from cases with suspected acute respiratory infections tested between 1 January 2023 and 31 July 2024. Data were collected from participating sites using standardised record forms and analysed. We assessed overall positivity, pathogen frequencies, age-group and regional distributions, and monthly and quarterly trends. A total of 8,361 samples were included, of which 5,252 (62.8%) had at least one pathogen detected. Single detections were identified in 4,279 samples (51.2%), while co-detections were observed in 973 samples (11.6%). Viral pathogens predominated. Rhinovirus/enterovirus was the most frequently detected pathogen (23.8%), followed by respiratory syncytial virus A/B (13.7%) and influenza A H1N1/2009 (4.8%). Positivity was highest among children aged 1 to 4 years and infants aged 1 to 11 months, and age-related variation was more pronounced than regional variation. The leading pathogens also demonstrated distinct temporal profiles, with different peak months across the study period. In conclusion, respiratory pathogen detection in this hospital-based population was characterised by a high positivity rate, predominance of viral pathogens, marked age-related differences, and distinct temporal patterns. These findings support the value of multiplex PCR for respiratory pathogen surveillance in Malaysian public hospitals.

Design and population

Core methods

COVID-19大流行期间的数字化医院风险管理框架:来自中国西南地区三级癌症医院的基于实践的见解

A digitalized nosocomial risk management framework during the COVID-19 pandemic: practice-based insights from a tertiary cancer hospital in Southwest China
作者： Tian Q, Huang Q, Tang J, Liu A, Wang H, Yang W, Wu C
摘要中文翻译文献中对肿瘤专科医院的综合院内风险管理框架的记录仍然不足。方法本社区案例研究描述了中国西南最大的三级肿瘤医院四川省肿瘤医院(中国西南最大的三级癌症医院)从 2020 年 1 月至 2022 年 9 月实施的一项全面的、多组成部分的流行病风险管理项目。该项目将七个管理支柱与基于 Questionnaire Star® 和 R 编程语言构建的数字风险报告管道集成在一起。结果在 32 个月的研究期间,该医院实现了 98% 的员工 疫苗接种 率和 95% 的助推器覆盖率。继 2022 年 8 月部署后,在研究期的最后几周,数字报告系统处理了风险调查数据,并在提交后 5 分钟内生成了可操作的汇总表。7 承认概念和方法上的限制本研究受到以下概念和方法上的限制:(1)单中心设计限制了普遍性;研究结果应在不同机构和监管背景下的多中心研究中得到验证。(7)由于整个研究期间没有进行系统的无症状筛查,因此报告的结果仅指发现的病例。背景癌症患者是传染病 暴发 期间特别脆弱的人群,与一般人群相比,感染和 暴发 的风险显着升高。监测期间,患者或工作人员中没有发现院内COVID-19病例。结论经验表明,即使在极端的流行病学压力下,管理创新与数字技术的系统整合也可以支持医院感染预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估在 COVID-19 大流行期间,数字化医院风险管理框架在三级癌症医院内预防 SARS-CoV-2 传播 的有效性,因为癌症患者对感染和 死亡率 的脆弱性更高。
设计与对象
该研究是在中国西南地区一家三级肿瘤医院进行的为期 32 个月的单中心案例研究,重点关注国家“零新冠”政策期间数字化风险管理计划的实施情况。
核心方法
该计划利用基于 Questionnaire Star® 和 R 编程语言构建的数字风险报告管道,将管理策略与数字技术系统集成,并依靠社区层面的非药物干预措施 (NPI),例如封锁、大规模检测和旅行限制。
主要结果
在 32 个月的研究期间,医院实现了 98% 的员工 疫苗接种 率和 95% 的加强覆盖率。监测 期间,在患者或工作人员中未发现院内 COVID-19 病例。该计划在 48 小时内进行了重新配置,以应对流感等其他传染病。
解释与创新
作者将未发现院内病例解释为一种关联,而不是因果关系的证据,并指出该计划的有效性可能受到同时存在的社区层面限制的影响。新颖之处在于使用数字工具展示集成的、多成分的感染预防策略的实用性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,管理创新与数字技术的系统整合可以支持 暴发 期间的医院感染预防,特别是在癌症医院等高风险环境中,并强调了此类框架对于大流行后准备的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单中心设计的限制,限制了普遍性,并且同时存在的社区层面的限制可能会混淆对该项目独立有效性的评估。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

A digitalized nosocomial risk management framework during the COVID-19 pandemic: practice-based insights from a tertiary cancer hospital in Southwest China

Authors: Tian Q, Huang Q, Tang J, Liu A, Wang H, Yang W, Wu C
Original abstract (English)
Comprehensive nosocomial risk management frameworks for specialized oncology hospitals remain under-documented in the literature.MethodsThis community case study describes a comprehensive, multi-component epidemic risk management program implemented at Sichuan Cancer Hospital—the largest tertiary cancer hospital in Southwest China—from January 2020 to September 2022. The program integrated seven management pillars with a digital risk-reporting pipeline built on Questionnaire Star® and R programming language.ResultsOver the 32-month study period, the hospital achieved a 98% staff vaccination rate with 95% booster coverage. Following its deployment in August 2022, during the final weeks of the study period, the digital reporting system processed risk-investigation data and generated actionable summary tables within 5 min of submission. 7Acknowledgment of conceptual and methodological constraintsThis study is subject to the following conceptual and methodological constraints: (1) the single-center design limits generalizability; findings should be validated in multi-center studies across diverse institutional and regulatory contexts. (7) Because systematic asymptomatic screening was not performed throughout the study period, the reported outcome refers to detected cases only. BackgroundCancer patients represent a particularly vulnerable population during infectious disease outbreaks, with substantially elevated risk of infection and mortality compared to the general population. No nosocomial COVID-19 cases were detected among patients or staff during the surveillance period.ConclusionThe experience demonstrates that systematic integration of management innovations with digital technologies can support hospital infection prevention even under extreme epidemiological pressu...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of a digitalized nosocomial risk management framework in preventing SARS-CoV-2 transmission within a tertiary cancer hospital during the COVID-19 pandemic, given the heightened vulnerability of cancer patients to infection and mortality.
Design and population
The study was a single-center case study conducted at a tertiary cancer hospital in Southwest China over a 32-month period, focusing on the implementation of a digitalized risk management program during the national Zero-COVID policy period.
Core methods
The program utilized a digital risk-reporting pipeline built on Questionnaire Star® and R programming language, incorporating systematic integration of management strategies with digital technologies, and relied on community-level non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) such as lockdowns, mass testing, and travel restrictions.
Main results
Over the 32-month study period, the hospital achieved a 98% staff vaccination rate with 95% booster coverage. No nosocomial COVID-19 cases were detected among patients or staff during the surveillance period. The program was reconfigured within 48 hours to address other infectious diseases such as influenza.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the absence of detected nosocomial cases as an association rather than proof of causation, noting that the program's effectiveness may have been influenced by concurrent community-level restrictions. The novelty lies in demonstrating the utility of integrated, multi-component infection prevention strategies using digital tools.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that systematic integration of management innovations with digital technologies can support hospital infection prevention during outbreaks, particularly in high-risk settings like cancer hospitals, and highlight the importance of such frameworks for post-pandemic preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design, which restricts generalizability, and the concurrent community-level restrictions that may have confounded the assessment of the program's standalone effectiveness. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

除了 RSV:为什么婴儿的 毛细支气管炎 负担不会简单消失。

Beyond RSV: why the bronchiolitis burden in infants will not simply disappear.
作者： Francesco Mariani, Rosa Morello, Laura Martino, Andrea A. Carlomagno, Danilo Buonsenso
摘要中文翻译尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗接种 正在显着减少 RSV-毛细支气管炎 住院率,这是儿科预防医学领域的一项里程碑式成就。然而,我们认为,这些成功不应被误认为是严重的 毛细支气管炎 临床和公共卫生问题的解决。首先,RSV 的流行病学优势可能部分反映了其 传播 效率,而不是独特的致病优势:鼻病毒、人偏肺病毒、腺病毒和博卡病毒已经引起严重疾病,CDHR3、GSDMB 和先天免疫基因的遗传变异表明 宿主 易感性,而不是病原体身份,是严重程度的关键驱动因素。其次,尽管目前的证据不支持病毒替代,但其他呼吸道病毒可能扩展到被抑制的 RSV 传播 腾出的生态位的可能性值得密切监测,同时 尼塞韦单抗 抗性 RSV 逃逸变种理论上的适应性有限的风险也值得密切监测。第三,长期病毒学 监测、成本效益评估和全球公平获取方面仍存在重大差距,特别是对于承担 97% 以上 RSV 归因 死亡率 的低收入和中等收入国家而言。结论:我们认为 毛细支气管炎 应该被理解为一种反映婴儿气道对病毒损伤的刻板反应的综合征,而不是由单一病原体定义的疾病。需要持续的广谱 监测、透明的成本效益监测以及对泛呼吸道粘膜 疫苗 管道的持续投资,以确保针对 RSV 的早期成功转化为婴儿 毛细支气管炎 总体负担的持久减少。已知信息: • 尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇RSV 疫苗接种 正在大幅减少实际RSV-毛细支气管炎 住院率,这是儿科预防医学领域的重大成功。最新消息: • 我们认为,RSV 的控制并不等同于重症婴儿 毛细支气管炎 的控制。其他呼吸道病毒——鼻病毒、人类偏肺病毒、腺病毒和博卡病毒——已经引起严重疾病,并可能扩展到被抑制的 RSV 传播 留下的生态位,而 宿主 遗传易感性,而不是病原体身份,仍然是严重程度的关键驱动因素。我们呼吁持续使用广谱病毒 毛细支气管炎,以确保针对 RSV 的成功转化为总体 毛细支气管炎 负担的持久减少,而不是被其他病原体取代。

Beyond RSV: why the bronchiolitis burden in infants will not simply disappear.

Authors: Francesco Mariani, Rosa Morello, Laura Martino, Andrea A. Carlomagno, Danilo Buonsenso
Original abstract (English)
Nirsevimab and maternal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination are delivering real-world important reductions in RSV-bronchiolitis hospitalisations, a landmark achievement in paediatric preventive medicine. We argue, however, that these successes should not be mistaken for the resolution of severe bronchiolitis as a clinical and public health problem. First, RSV's epidemiological dominance may partly reflect its transmission efficiency rather than a unique pathogenic superiority: rhinovirus, human metapneumovirus, adenovirus, and bocavirus already cause substantial disease, and genetic variants in CDHR3, GSDMB, and innate immune genes indicate that host susceptibility, not pathogen identity, is a key driver of severity. Second, although current evidence does not support viral replacement, the possibility that other respiratory viruses could expand into the ecological niche vacated by suppressed RSV transmission warrants close monitoring, alongside the theoretical, fitness-limited risk of nirsevimab-resistant RSV escape variants. Third, important gaps remain in long-term virological surveillance, cost-effectiveness evaluation, and equitable global access, particularly for the low- and middle-income countries that bear over 97% of RSV-attributable mortality. Conclusion: We contend that bronchiolitis should be understood as a syndrome reflecting the infant airway's stereotyped response to viral injury, rather than a disease defined by a single pathogen. Sustained broad-spectrum surveillance, transparent cost-effectiveness monitoring, and continued investment in a pan-respiratory mucosal vaccine pipeline are needed to ensure that early success against RSV translates into a durable reduction of the overall infant bronchiolitis burden. What is Known: • Nirsevimab and maternal RSV vaccination are producing substantial real-world reductions in RSV-bronchiolitis hospitalisations, a major success in paediatric preventive medicine. What is New: • We argue that the control of RSV does not equate to the control of severe infant bronchiolitis. Other respiratory viruses-rhinovirus, human metapneumovirus, adenovirus and bocavirus-already cause substantial disease and could expand into the ecological niche left by suppressed RSV transmission, while host genetic susceptibility, not pathogen identity, remains a key driver of severity. We call for sustained broad spectrum viral surveillance to ensure that success against RSV translates into a durable reduction of the overall bronchiolitis burden, rather than its replacement by other pathogens.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
We argue, however, that these successes should not be mistaken for the resolution of severe bronchiolitis as a clinical and public health problem.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
We contend that bronchiolitis should be understood as a syndrome reflecting the infant airway's stereotyped response to viral injury, rather than a disease defined by a single pathogen.
Controversies and gaps
Second, although current evidence does not support viral replacement, the possibility that other respiratory viruses could expand into the ecological niche vacated by suppressed RSV transmission warrants close monitoring, alongside the theoretical, fitness-limited risk of nirsevimab-resistant RSV escape variants.
Research and practice implications
We call for sustained broad spectrum viral surveillance to ensure that success against RSV translates into a durable reduction of the overall bronchiolitis burden, rather than its replacement by other pathogens.

孕妇 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗接种 和 疫苗接种 覆盖的感知障碍:一项横断面研究。

Perceived barriers to respiratory syncytial virus vaccination and vaccination coverage among pregnant women: A cross-sectional study.
作者： Stella M. Souto, María A. Rodríguez, Fernando A. Torres, Paula A. Domínguez, Gabriel H. Amaya
摘要中文翻译阿根廷于 2024 年引入针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的孕产妇 疫苗接种,以预防婴儿严重呼吸道感染。为了确保令人满意的 疫苗接种 率,识别其实施的障碍非常重要。确定孕妇中 RSV 疫苗接种 的障碍。我们估计了 RSV 疫苗接种 覆盖范围以及覆盖范围和障碍之间的潜在关联。人口和方法。这是一项基于对有资格接受 RSV 疫苗 的母亲的调查的横断面研究。我们开发并验证了一种工具来识别结构、信息、情感和社交障碍。我们使用逻辑回归进行单变量和多变量分析。我们调查了 216 名母亲。RSV 疫苗 覆盖率为 43.7%。在单变量分析中,少于 5 次产前检查和未收到明确信息与较低的 疫苗接种 率相关。在多变量分析中,RSV 疫苗 的知识是与 ZX​​QTERMKQXZ 覆盖率独立相关的唯一因素(调整后 OR:3.4;95% CI:1.9-6.2;p <0.001)。孕妇中 RSV 疫苗接种 的覆盖率低于怀孕期间推荐的其他 疫苗 的覆盖率。我们将有限的 疫苗 可用性和有限的 疫苗 知识确定为与非 疫苗 相关的因素。加强产前护理期间的信息和建议有助于提高 疫苗接种 的覆盖率。这项研究提供了一个经过验证的工具,用于评估孕妇 疫苗接种 的障碍。

Perceived barriers to respiratory syncytial virus vaccination and vaccination coverage among pregnant women: A cross-sectional study.

Authors: Stella M. Souto, María A. Rodríguez, Fernando A. Torres, Paula A. Domínguez, Gabriel H. Amaya
Original abstract (English)
Argentina introduced maternal vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in 2024 to prevent severe respiratory infections in infants. To ensure satisfactory vaccination rates, it is important to identify barriers to its implementation. To identify barriers to RSV vaccination among pregnant women. We estimated RSV vaccination coverage and the potential association between coverage and barriers. Population and methods. A cross-sectional study based on a survey of mothers who were eligible to receive the RSV vaccine. We developed and validated a tool to identify structural, informational, emotional, and social barriers. We used logistic regression for univariate and multivariate analyses. We surveyed 216 mothers. RSV vaccine coverage was 43.7%. In the univariate analysis, fewer than 5 prenatal visits and not receiving clear information were associated with lower vaccination rates. In the multivariate analysis, knowledge of the RSV vaccine was the only factor independently associated with vaccination coverage (adjusted OR: 3.4; 95% CI: 1.9-6.2; p <0.001). RSV vaccination coverage among pregnant women was lower than that for other vaccines recommended during pregnancy. We identified limited vaccine availability and limited knowledge about the vaccine as factors associated with non-vaccination. Strengthening information and recommendations during prenatal care could help improve vaccination coverage. This study provides a validated tool for assessing barriers to vaccination among pregnant women.

We developed and validated a tool to identify structural, informational, emotional, and social barriers.
Evidence base and method
A cross-sectional study based on a survey of mothers who were eligible to receive the RSV vaccine.
Consensus and conclusions
In the univariate analysis, fewer than 5 prenatal visits and not receiving clear information were associated with lower vaccination rates.
Controversies and gaps
We identified limited vaccine availability and limited knowledge about the vaccine as factors associated with non-vaccination.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening information and recommendations during prenatal care could help improve vaccination coverage.

COVID-19 时代前后小儿呼吸道感染模式变化的回顾:全球和印度的视角

A Review of Changing Patterns of Pediatric Respiratory Infections in the Pre- and Post-COVID-19 Era: A Global and Indian Perspective
作者： Tanya Sood, Avilash Guntal
摘要中文翻译呼吸道感染仍然是儿童患病的重要原因,其 流行病学 在 COVID-19 大流行期间和之后发生了显着变化。非药物干预措施,包括佩戴口罩、关闭学校、保持距离、改善手部卫生和减少出行,与几种常见呼吸道病毒,特别是流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的下降有关,而鼻病毒/肠道病毒继续在许多环境中传播。限制放松后,许多国家报告RSV、流感、人类偏肺病毒和其他呼吸道病原体的活动延迟或反季节。BioFire Respiratory Panel 等多重分子平台能够快速、同时检测多种病毒和细菌因子,从而帮助记录这些变化。这篇叙述性综述总结了大流行前、大流行和大流行后的呼吸道病原体模式,重点是儿科感染、全球观察和印度数据。它还讨论了免疫差距、常规免疫接种中断、就医行为改变、共同检测和印度地区差异的作用。加强呼吸道病毒 监测、改善哨点中心多重诊断的可及性、恢复常规免疫接种以及将流感、RSV 和 COVID-19 预防策略纳入儿科公共卫生规划,对于后 COVID 时期至关重要。

A Review of Changing Patterns of Pediatric Respiratory Infections in the Pre- and Post-COVID-19 Era: A Global and Indian Perspective

Authors: Tanya Sood, Avilash Guntal
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory tract infections remain an important cause of illness in children, and their epidemiology has changed noticeably during and after the COVID-19 pandemic. Non-pharmaceutical interventions, including masking, school closure, distancing, improved hand hygiene, and reduced travel, were associated with a fall in several common respiratory viruses, particularly influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), while rhinovirus/enterovirus continued to circulate in many settings. After restrictions were relaxed, many countries reported delayed or out-of-season activity of RSV, influenza, human metapneumovirus, and other respiratory pathogens. Multiplex molecular platforms such as the BioFire Respiratory Panel have helped document these shifts by enabling rapid, simultaneous detection of multiple viral and bacterial agents. This narrative review summarizes pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic respiratory pathogen patterns with emphasis on pediatric infections, global observations, and Indian data. It also discusses the role of immunity gaps, disrupted routine immunization, altered healthcare-seeking behavior, co-detection, and regional variation in India. Strengthening respiratory virus surveillance, improving access to multiplex diagnostics in sentinel centers, restoring routine immunization, and integrating influenza, RSV, and COVID-19 prevention strategies into pediatric public health planning are essential in the post-COVID period.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory tract infections remain an important cause of illness in children, and their epidemiology has changed noticeably during and after the COVID-19 pandemic.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review summarizes pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic respiratory pathogen patterns with emphasis on pediatric infections, global observations, and Indian data.
Consensus and conclusions
Non-pharmaceutical interventions, including masking, school closure, distancing, improved hand hygiene, and reduced travel, were associated with a fall in several common respiratory viruses, particularly influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), while rhinovirus/enterovirus continued to circulate in many settings.
Controversies and gaps
It also discusses the role of immunity gaps, disrupted routine immunization, altered healthcare-seeking behavior, co-detection, and regional variation in India.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening respiratory virus surveillance, improving access to multiplex diagnostics in sentinel centers, restoring routine immunization, and integrating influenza, RSV, and COVID-19 prevention strategies into pediatric public health planning are essential in the post-COVID period.

预防婴儿和儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 疾病的建议:政策声明

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Policy Statement
作者： James D. Campbell, Pia S. Pannaraj, Monica I. Ardura, Kristina A. Bryant, Eric J. Chow, Roberta L. DeBiasi, Claudia Espinosa, Robert W. Frenck, C. Mary Healy, Betsy C. Herold
摘要中文翻译本政策声明更新了美国儿科学会 (AAP) 关于使用 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫接种来预防婴儿和儿童由 RSV 引起的下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的建议。AAP 建议对在第一个 RSV 季节期间出生或进入其第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的所有 8 个月以下婴儿进行 RSV 免疫接种,除非婴儿有其怀孕父母的 疫苗接种 保护记录;以及患有严重 RSV 疾病并进入第二个 RSV 季节的高风险的 8 至 19 个月大的婴儿和儿童。证据审查支持扩大对进入第二个 RSV 季节的 8 至 19 个月婴儿和儿童进行 RSV 免疫接种的高风险标准。随附的技术报告 (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079049) 讨论了支持 AAP 建议的证据以及扩展建议中包含的有关婴儿和儿童的其他详细信息。

提供的证据没有明确说明审查范围或中心问题。

随附的技术报告 (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079049) 讨论了支持 AAP 建议的证据以及扩展建议中包含的有关婴儿和儿童的其他详细信息。
共识与结论
证据审查支持扩大对进入第二个 RSV 季节的 8 至 19 个月婴儿和儿童进行 RSV 免疫接种的高风险标准。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
AAP 建议对在第一个 RSV 季节期间出生或进入其第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的所有 8 个月以下婴儿进行 RSV 免疫接种,除非婴儿有其怀孕父母的 疫苗接种 保护记录;以及患有严重 RSV 疾病并进入第二个 RSV 季节的高风险的 8 至 19 个月大的婴儿和儿童。

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Policy Statement

Authors: James D. Campbell, Pia S. Pannaraj, Monica I. Ardura, Kristina A. Bryant, Eric J. Chow, Roberta L. DeBiasi, Claudia Espinosa, Robert W. Frenck, C. Mary Healy, Betsy C. Herold
Original abstract (English)
This policy statement updates recommendations of the American Academy of Pediatrics (AAP) for the use of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization for the prevention of lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) caused by RSV in infants and children. The AAP recommends RSV immunization for all infants under 8 months of age born during or entering their first RSV season, unless the infant has documented protection from vaccination of their pregnant parent; and for infants and children 8 through 19 months of age who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season. A review of evidence supports expanding the high-risk criteria for administration of RSV immunization in infants and children 8 through 19 months of age entering their second RSV season. A discussion of the evidence supporting the AAP recommendations and additional details on infants and children included in the expanded recommendations are in the accompanying technical report (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079049).

The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review scope or central question.
Evidence base and method
A discussion of the evidence supporting the AAP recommendations and additional details on infants and children included in the expanded recommendations are in the accompanying technical report (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079049).
Consensus and conclusions
A review of evidence supports expanding the high-risk criteria for administration of RSV immunization in infants and children 8 through 19 months of age entering their second RSV season.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The AAP recommends RSV immunization for all infants under 8 months of age born during or entering their first RSV season, unless the infant has documented protection from vaccination of their pregnant parent; and for infants and children 8 through 19 months of age who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season.

呼吸道病毒感染中的中性粒细胞:免疫病理学和治疗策略。

Neutrophils in Respiratory Viral Infection: Immunopathology and Therapeutic Strategies.
作者： Logan S. Dean, Thomas Awamura, Min Ji Byun, Rochelle Leung, Natalie T Subia, Alton Goo, Chun Gwon Park
摘要中文翻译呼吸道不断暴露于周围环境中的微生物。这种粘膜屏障需要精确的控制和响应机制,以保持气体交换,同时保持屏障完整性。中性粒细胞是最丰富的白细胞,是维持这种稳态的关键。肺驻留和招募的中性粒细胞都会迁移到发炎区域,在那里它们执行效应功能,例如吞噬作用、脱粒、活性氧产生和中性粒细胞胞外陷阱(NET)形成。这些过程受到严格调节,肺泡巨噬细胞等免疫细胞有助于平衡病原体清除和炎症。新的证据表明,中性粒细胞除了发挥抗菌作用外,还会导致炎症和组织损伤。中性粒细胞水平和激活升高与呼吸道病毒感染后疾病的严重程度和不良结果相关。此外,呼吸道病毒可以劫持中性粒细胞功能,破坏免疫调节并促进病毒复制。因此,中性粒细胞失调已被提议作为降低急性疾病严重程度和预防肺损伤的目标。然而,中性粒细胞在感染过程中同时表现出保护性和致病性作用,需要在限制病理效应的同时保留其 宿主 防御的治疗策略。本综述总结了中性粒细胞生物学的最新知识及其在呼吸道病毒感染中的免疫病理学作用,重点关注严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2)、甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)。我们还讨论了针对中性粒细胞的治疗方法,包括临床前和临床研究,强调了调节中性粒细胞驱动的炎症的持续努力。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注中性粒细胞在呼吸道病毒感染中的作用,特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),重点关注其免疫病理学功能和治疗靶向潜力。
证据基础与方法
该综述全面总结了当前中性粒细胞生物学知识及其在呼吸道病毒感染中的作用,包括 RSV、甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 和严重急性呼吸系统综合症冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2)。它包括针对中性粒细胞的治疗方法的临床前和临床研究。
共识与结论
中性粒细胞对于维持呼吸道粘膜屏障至关重要,并在感染过程中发挥保护作用和致病作用。它们的失调与疾病的严重程度和肺损伤有关,使它们成为潜在的治疗靶点。
争议与缺口
关于中性粒细胞在呼吸道病毒感染中的双重作用一直存在争议,一些证据表明中性粒细胞除了抗菌功能之外还会导致炎症和组织损伤。该评论强调需要进行更多研究来阐明这些机制并开发靶向疗法。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应侧重于了解中性粒细胞在 RSV 感染中促进 宿主 防御和病理结果的精确机制。治疗策略应旨在调节中性粒细胞驱动的炎症而不损害其保护功能。

Neutrophils in Respiratory Viral Infection: Immunopathology and Therapeutic Strategies.

Authors: Logan S. Dean, Thomas Awamura, Min Ji Byun, Rochelle Leung, Natalie T Subia, Alton Goo, Chun Gwon Park
Original abstract (English)
The respiratory tract is constantly exposed to microbes from the surrounding environment. This mucosal barrier requires precise control and response mechanisms that preserve gas exchange while maintaining barrier integrity. Neutrophils, the most abundant leukocytes, are key to maintaining this homeostasis. Both lung-resident and recruited neutrophils migrate to inflamed areas, where they perform effector functions such as phagocytosis, degranulation, reactive oxygen species production, and neutrophil extracellular trap (NET) formation. These processes are tightly regulated, with immune cells such as alveolar macrophages helping to balance pathogen clearance and inflammation. Emerging evidence indicates that neutrophils contribute to inflammation and tissue damage beyond their antimicrobial roles. Elevated neutrophil levels and activation are associated with disease severity and poor outcomes following respiratory viral infection. In addition, respiratory viruses can hijack neutrophil functions to disrupt immune regulation and promote viral replication. Thus, neutrophil dysregulation has been proposed as a target for reducing acute disease severity and preventing lung injury. However, neutrophils exhibit both protective and pathogenic roles during infection, requiring therapeutic strategies that preserve their host defense while limiting pathological effects. This review summarizes current knowledge of neutrophil biology and their immunopathological roles in respiratory viral infections, focusing on Severe Acute Respiratory Syndrome Coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), Influenza A virus (IAV), and Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV). We also discuss therapeutic approaches targeting neutrophils, including preclinical and clinical studies, highlighting ongoing efforts to modulate neutrophil-driven inflammation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the role of neutrophils in respiratory viral infections, particularly Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV), with an emphasis on their immunopathological functions and therapeutic targeting potential.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive summary of current knowledge on neutrophil biology and their roles in respiratory viral infections, including RSV, Influenza A virus (IAV), and Severe Acute Respiratory Syndrome Coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2). It includes preclinical and clinical studies on therapeutic approaches targeting neutrophils.
Consensus and conclusions
Neutrophils are critical in maintaining the mucosal barrier in the respiratory tract and are involved in both protective and pathogenic roles during infection. Their dysregulation is linked to disease severity and lung injury, making them a potential therapeutic target.
Controversies and gaps
There is ongoing debate about the dual roles of neutrophils in respiratory viral infections, with some evidence suggesting they contribute to inflammation and tissue damage beyond their antimicrobial functions. The review highlights the need for more research to clarify these mechanisms and develop targeted therapies.
Research and practice implications
Future research should focus on understanding the precise mechanisms by which neutrophils contribute to both host defense and pathological outcomes in RSV infections. Therapeutic strategies should aim to modulate neutrophil-driven inflammation without compromising their protective functions.

抗病毒药物 针对 RNA 病毒的药物开发:进展、临床转化和持续的挑战

Antiviral Drug Development against RNA Viruses: Progress, Clinical Translation and Persistent Challenges
作者： Nasir Ahmad Falaki, Zephaniah Isaiah, Abdullahi Usman Ndabata, John Raymond, Hafsat Abba Muhammad, Hussaini Abubakar, Ibrahim Muhammad, Raymond John
摘要中文翻译抗病毒药物 药物开发已经产生了针对某些 RNA 病毒感染的革命性 治疗,同时在急性呼吸道、出血和肠道疾病方面留下了重大空白。进展的不平衡不能仅用目标的可用性来解释。这篇批判性的叙述性综述探讨了病毒生物学、药理学暴露、疾病时机、试验设计、耐药性和卫生系统准入如何共同决定 抗病毒药物 候选药物是否成为临床上有用的干预措施。严格综合了 2000 年至 2026 年 8 月 13 日发表的同行评审机制研究、随机试验、方法论论文和权威机构证据,并在必要时考虑了早期的基础研究结果。针对人类免疫缺陷病毒的持久组合 治疗 和针对丙型肝炎的治疗性直接作用 治疗 显示了持续的靶标验证和临床开发可以实现的目标。相反,流感、2019冠状病毒病、呼吸道合胞病毒、登革热和新出现的感染的试验表明,将病毒复制的减少转化为对症状、住院、呼吸道合胞病毒或生存的可靠影响是困难的。不同疾病阶段的瑞德西韦结果对比、口腔冠状病毒 抗病毒药物 的背景依赖性结果、一项有前景但地理上有限的儿科 呼吸道合胞病毒 试验以及登革热人类挑战研究说明了为什么阳性病毒学或早期临床发现需要精确解释。耐药性是由药物机制、治疗 暴露、病毒群体动态和 监测 质量决定的,而不是由所有 RNA 病毒的统一特性决定的。表观的广谱活性也可能反映测定假象或非生理实验系统。优先事项包括药理学上可信的发现平台、早期的 诊断 和 治疗、预先指定的患者重要终点的适应性试验、耐药性信息组合、暴发 就绪的试验基础设施以及许可前计划的可负担性。中心结论是,当分子特异性、临床相关时机和公平实施被开发为相互关联的证据途径时,抗病毒药物 创新是最有效的。

范围与问题
这篇批判性的叙述性综述探讨了病毒生物学、药理学暴露、疾病时机、试验设计、耐药性和卫生系统准入如何共同决定 抗病毒药物 候选药物是否成为临床上有用的干预措施。
证据基础与方法
严格综合了 2000 年至 2026 年 8 月 13 日发表的同行评审机制研究、随机试验、方法论论文和权威机构证据,并在必要时考虑了早期的基础研究结果。
共识与结论
不同疾病阶段的瑞德西韦结果对比、口腔冠状病毒 抗病毒药物 的背景依赖性结果、一项有前景但地理上有限的儿科 呼吸道合胞病毒 试验以及登革热人类挑战研究说明了为什么阳性病毒学或早期临床发现需要精确解释。
争议与缺口
中心结论是,当分子特异性、临床相关时机和公平实施被开发为相互关联的证据途径时,抗病毒药物 创新是最有效的。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括药理学上可信的发现平台、早期的 诊断 和 治疗、预先指定的患者重要终点的适应性试验、耐药性信息组合、暴发 就绪的试验基础设施以及许可前计划的可负担性。

Antiviral Drug Development against RNA Viruses: Progress, Clinical Translation and Persistent Challenges

Authors: Nasir Ahmad Falaki, Zephaniah Isaiah, Abdullahi Usman Ndabata, John Raymond, Hafsat Abba Muhammad, Hussaini Abubakar, Ibrahim Muhammad, Raymond John
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral drug development has produced transformative treatments for some RNA-virus infections while leaving major gaps in acute respiratory, haemorrhagic and enteric disease. The unevenness of progress cannot be explained by target availability alone. This critical narrative review examines how viral biology, pharmacological exposure, disease timing, trial design, resistance and health-system access jointly determine whether an antiviral candidate becomes a clinically useful intervention. Peer-reviewed mechanistic studies, randomised trials, methodological papers and authoritative institutional evidence published from 2000 to 13 August 2026 were critically synthesised, with earlier foundational findings considered where necessary. Durable combination treatment for human immunodeficiency virus and curative direct-acting treatment for hepatitis C show what sustained target validation and clinical development can achieve. Conversely, trials of influenza, coronavirus disease 2019, respiratory syncytial virus, dengue and emerging infections demonstrate the difficulty of translating reductions in viral replication into reliable effects on symptoms, hospitalisation, transmission or survival. Contrasting remdesivir outcomes across disease stages, context-dependent results for oral coronavirus antivirals, a promising but geographically limited paediatric respiratory syncytial virus trial, and dengue human-challenge studies illustrate why positive virological or early clinical findings require precise interpretation. Resistance is shaped by drug mechanism, treatment exposure, viral population dynamics and surveillance quality, rather than by a uniform property of all RNA viruses. Apparent broad-spectrum activity may also reflect assay artefacts or non-physiological experimental systems. Priorities include pharmacologically credible discovery platforms, earlier diagnosis and treatment, adaptive trials with prespecified patient-important endpoints, resistance-informed combinations, outbreak-ready trial infrastructure and affordability planned before licensing. The central conclusion is that antiviral innovation is most effective when molecular specificity, clinically relevant timing and equitable implementation are developed as a connected evidence pathway.
View review five-element analysis

Controversies and gaps
The central conclusion is that antiviral innovation is most effective when molecular specificity, clinically relevant timing and equitable implementation are developed as a connected evidence pathway.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include pharmacologically credible discovery platforms, earlier diagnosis and treatment, adaptive trials with prespecified patient-important endpoints, resistance-informed combinations, outbreak-ready trial infrastructure and affordability planned before licensing.

人类RNA病毒对肌动蛋白的重塑:机制和靶向干预

Actin remodeling by human RNA virus: mechanisms and targeted intervention
作者： Jie Gao, Wei Yu
摘要中文翻译肌动蛋白细胞骨架是真核细胞中一个核心的动态网络,维持细胞形态、运动和细胞内运输。大量研究已证实肌动蛋白在RNA病毒感染中的重要性。肌动蛋白重塑作为RNA病毒利用的核心宿主机制,强烈影响病毒复制和传播效率。本文综述了参与肌动蛋白动态调控的关键调节分子。在此基础上,我们重点探讨了六种代表性人类RNA病毒(严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒-2、流感A病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、登革热病毒、埃博拉病毒和人类免疫缺陷病毒)劫持宿主肌动蛋白网络的分子机制,揭示了RNA病毒倾向于选择的肌动蛋白劫持途径。最后,我们进一步总结了当前针对抗病毒药物化合物作用于肌动蛋白信号通路的研究现状。鉴于针对宿主因子具有独特的广谱抗病毒药物活性优势,该策略为克服高病毒突变率导致的药物耐受性提供了希望。然而,其临床应用仍面临平衡细胞毒性与疗效的困难。本文旨在为针对肌动蛋白的新型抗病毒药物干预方法提供概念框架。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该文章研究了人类 RNA 病毒(包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV))劫持 宿主 肌动蛋白网络以影响病毒复制和 传播 效率的分子机制。它重点关注肌动蛋白重塑作为 RNA 病毒利用的核心 宿主 机制的作用,重点关注 RSV 及其对疾病进展的影响。
证据基础与方法
该综述基于对肌动蛋白细胞骨架在 RNA 病毒感染中的作用的全面分析,包括 RSV 等六种代表性人类 RNA 病毒操纵 宿主 肌动蛋白动力学的机制。该综述强调了肌动蛋白在病毒感染中的重要性,并总结了理解这些相互作用的最新进展。
共识与结论
肌动蛋白重塑是一种重要的 宿主 机制,包括 RSV 在内的 RNA 病毒利用该机制来增强其复制和 传播 效率。该综述确定了参与肌动蛋白动力学的关键调节分子,并概述了 RSV 和其他病毒操纵 宿主 肌动蛋白网络的途径。
争议与缺口
该综述承认,虽然肌动蛋白重塑已被广泛认为是 RNA 病毒的 宿主 机制,但由于平衡细胞毒性和功效方面存在困难,靶向肌动蛋白信号通路的临床应用仍然具有挑战性。还需要对专门针对 RSV 中肌动蛋白相关途径的 抗病毒药物 化合物进行进一步研究。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调了针对 宿主 因子(如肌动蛋白信号通路)的广谱 抗病毒药物 策略的潜力。这表明未来的研究应集中于开发 抗病毒药物 化合物,该化合物可有效调节肌动蛋白动力学,而不引起显着的细胞毒性,特别是对于 RSV。

Actin remodeling by human RNA virus: mechanisms and targeted intervention

Authors: Jie Gao, Wei Yu
Original abstract (English)
The actin cytoskeleton is a central dynamic network in eukaryotic cells which preserves morphology, motility, and intracellular transport. Extensive research has confirmed the importance of actin in RNA viral infection. Actin remodeling, as a core host mechanism exploited by RNA viruses, strongly affects viral replication and transmission efficiency. This article reviews the key regulatory molecules involved in actin dynamics. Based on this foundation, we focus on recent advances in the molecular mechanisms by which six representative human RNA viruses(severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus-2, influenza A virus, respiratory syncytial virus, dengue virus, ebola virus and human immunodeficiency virus) hijack the host actin network, revealing the actin hijacking pathways that RNA viruses tend to select. Finally, we further summarize the current status of research on antiviral compounds that target the actin signaling pathway. Given that targeting host factors offers the unique advantage of broad-spectrum antiviral activity, this strategy offers promise for overcoming drug resistance caused by high viral mutation rates. However, its clinical application still faces difficulties in balancing cytotoxicity and efficacy. This article intends to provide a conceptual framework for novel antiviral intervention approaches targeting actin.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The article examines the molecular mechanisms by which human RNA viruses, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), hijack the host actin network to influence viral replication and transmission efficiency. It focuses on the role of actin remodeling as a core host mechanism exploited by RNA viruses, with an emphasis on RSV and its impact on disease progression.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a comprehensive analysis of the actin cytoskeleton's role in RNA viral infection, including the mechanisms by which six representative human RNA viruses, including RSV, manipulate host actin dynamics. The review highlights the importance of actin in viral infection and summarizes recent advances in understanding these interactions.
Consensus and conclusions
Actin remodeling is a critical host mechanism that RNA viruses, including RSV, exploit to enhance their replication and transmission efficiency. The review identifies key regulatory molecules involved in actin dynamics and outlines the pathways by which RSV and other viruses manipulate the host actin network.
Controversies and gaps
The review acknowledges that while actin remodeling is well-established as a host mechanism for RNA viruses, the clinical application of targeting actin signaling pathways remains challenging due to difficulties in balancing cytotoxicity and efficacy. There is also a need for further research on antiviral compounds that specifically target actin-related pathways in RSV.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the potential of targeting host factors like actin signaling pathways for broad-spectrum antiviral strategies. It suggests that future research should focus on developing antiviral compounds that effectively modulate actin dynamics without causing significant cytotoxicity, particularly for RSV.

佐剂在成人 RSV 中作用的叙述回顾 疫苗

A Narrative Review of the Role of Adjuvants in Adult RSV Vaccines
作者： Elizabeth Williams, Giovanni Checcucci Lisi, Kate Luisi, Kumar Ilangovan, Negar Aliabadi, Cassandra Hall-Murray, Pier Luigi Lopalco, Edward Walsh, Kyla Hayford, Elizabeth Begier
摘要中文翻译近年来,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗 的开发取得了实质性进展,三个基于预融合 F (preF) 蛋白和 mRNA 的平台获得了监管部门批准用于老年人。在获得许可的蛋白质亚基 疫苗 中,关键的设计差异包括佐剂的使用和抗原效价,这引发了有关佐剂预防 RSV 的必要性和影响的重要问题。这篇叙述性综述探讨了佐剂在 RSV 疫苗 发育中的作用,重点是免疫原性、疫苗 的有效性、安全性以及对老年人和免疫功能低下 (IC) 人群的影响。佐剂有时可以增强 免疫应答,但可能会增加反应原性。临床试验和观察性研究表明,佐剂(RSVPreF3/AS01E)和非佐剂(二价RSVpreF)疫苗 均可针对 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病和住院提供实质性且可比的保护,且安全性相当。迄今为止的全部证据并未表明佐剂 疫苗 在 IC 人群中具有有效性优势。总体而言,这些研究结果表明,无论有或没有辅助治疗,都可以有效预防成人 RSV 疾病。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
临床试验和观察性研究表明,佐剂(RSVPreF3/AS01E)和非佐剂(二价RSVpreF)疫苗 均可针对 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病和住院提供实质性且可比的保护,且安全性相当。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述探讨了佐剂在 RSV 疫苗 发育中的作用,重点是免疫原性、疫苗 的有效性、安全性以及对老年人和免疫功能低下 (IC) 人群的影响。
共识与结论
总体而言,这些研究结果表明,无论有或没有辅助治疗,都可以有效预防成人 RSV 疾病。
争议与缺口
迄今为止的全部证据并未表明佐剂 疫苗 在 IC 人群中具有有效性优势。
科研与实践启示
在获得许可的蛋白质亚基 疫苗 中,关键的设计差异包括佐剂的使用和抗原效价,这引发了有关佐剂预防 RSV 的必要性和影响的重要问题。

A Narrative Review of the Role of Adjuvants in Adult RSV Vaccines

Authors: Elizabeth Williams, Giovanni Checcucci Lisi, Kate Luisi, Kumar Ilangovan, Negar Aliabadi, Cassandra Hall-Murray, Pier Luigi Lopalco, Edward Walsh, Kyla Hayford, Elizabeth Begier
Original abstract (English)
The development of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccines has advanced substantially in recent years, with three prefusion F (preF) protein-based and mRNA platforms achieving regulatory approval for use in older adults. Among licensed protein subunit vaccines, key design differences include the use of adjuvants and antigen valency, raising important questions regarding the necessity and impact of adjuvantation for RSV prevention. This narrative review examines the role of adjuvants in RSV vaccine development, with a focus on immunogenicity, vaccine effectiveness, safety, and implications for older adults and immunocompromised (IC) populations. Adjuvants can sometimes enhance immune responses but may increase reactogenicity. Clinical trials and observational studies demonstrate that both adjuvanted (RSVPreF3/AS01E) and non-adjuvanted (bivalent RSVpreF) vaccines provide substantial and comparable protection against RSV-related lower respiratory tract disease and hospitalization with comparable safety. The totality of evidence to date does not suggest an effectiveness advantage for adjuvanted vaccines among IC populations. Overall, these findings suggest that effective protection against RSV disease in adults can be achieved with or without adjuvantation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Clinical trials and observational studies demonstrate that both adjuvanted (RSVPreF3/AS01E) and non-adjuvanted (bivalent RSVpreF) vaccines provide substantial and comparable protection against RSV-related lower respiratory tract disease and hospitalization with comparable safety.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review examines the role of adjuvants in RSV vaccine development, with a focus on immunogenicity, vaccine effectiveness, safety, and implications for older adults and immunocompromised (IC) populations.
Consensus and conclusions
Overall, these findings suggest that effective protection against RSV disease in adults can be achieved with or without adjuvantation.
Controversies and gaps
The totality of evidence to date does not suggest an effectiveness advantage for adjuvanted vaccines among IC populations.
Research and practice implications
Among licensed protein subunit vaccines, key design differences include the use of adjuvants and antigen valency, raising important questions regarding the necessity and impact of adjuvantation for RSV prevention.

RSV-B Lineage Emerges as Standout in Severe Pediatric Disease - Infectious Disease Special Edition

Send Close Pediatric Infections Share on Facebook Share on LinkedIn Share on X Email Article Copy Article Url Print OCTOBER 23, 2026 IDWeek 2026 RSV-B Lineage Emerges as Standout in Severe Pediatric Disease By Anna Tsioulias Young children infected with certain respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) subgroup B lineages experienced greater disease severity than those infected with RSV-A, according to a study presented at IDWeek 2026, in Washington, D.C. Source: Adobe. The biggest potential culprit? RSV-B lineage B.D.E.1. Related Content RSV Vaccination Shows Effectiveness at Year 3 A new study has found that vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains effective three years ... This Year’s U.S. Measles Cases Soar Beyond 4,000 The United States has identified 4,080 confirmed measles cases in 2026, as of CDC data updated on Oct. 8. Starting HPV Vaccine at Age 9 or 10 Raises Series Completion by 13 Starting the HPV vaccine series younger substantially increased the share of children who began and finished the ... The findings shed new light on a longstanding debate regarding the relative virulence of RSV antigenic subgroups. Although both RSV-A and RSV-B are established players in severe pediatric respiratory disease, prior studies have yielded conflicting results on whether differences in viral glycoproteins, including the fusion (F) and attachment (G) proteins, influence clinical severity. “We actually initially thought RSV-A was more pathogenic than RSV-B,” explained Kim El Haddad, MD, a pediatric infectious diseases physician at MedStar Georgetown University Hospital, in Washington, D.C. “These new findings help clarify with very statistically significant results that the lineage B was associated with worse outcomes, although the fact that this study had more RSV-B than RSV-A cases (216 vs. 129) might affect precision.” In a prospective study spanning seven respiratory seasons (2015-2021), investigators at St. Jude Children’s Research Hospital, in Memphis, Tennessee, collected nasal swabs from 345 previously healthy children younger than 2 years of age: 22% with mild (outpatient) and 78% with moderate to severe (inpatient) RSV infection. After whole-genome sequencing, significant differences emerged across six RSV lineages with regard to disease severity scores (P=0.01), pediatric ICU admissions (P<0.0001), duration of oxygen supplementation (P=0.04), oxygen delivery methods (P<0.0001), and hospital length of stay (P=0.03). B.D.E.1 (n=61) was associated with particularly severe clinical outcomes, with nearly three-fourths of infected children requiring PICU admission. However, Dr. El Haddad, who was not involved with the research, noted that the changing distribution of RSV lineages across respiratory seasons could complicate interpretation, especially considering disruptions in viral circulation during the COVID-19 pandemic. “During peak COVID times, isolation measures created what we call an immunity debt, as many children were not exposed to RSV,” Dr. El Haddad told Infectious Disease Special Edition. “When restrictions eased in 2021, we saw a surge in B.D.E.1 infections. I wonder whether this lack of previous exposure contributed to a stronger inflammatory response with elevated cytokine levels and ultimately more severe disease. “I would also be interested in knowing whether viral loads differed between RSV-A and RSV-B infections, as reflected by cycle threshold values. A higher viral burden could contribute to both the more pronounced inflammatory response and greater disease severity observed with certain RSV-B lineages.” Looking beyond differences in clinical severity, the investigators uncovered distinct genomic and inflammatory profiles among RSV subgroups. More specifically, RSV-B showed greater sequence variability across F, G, and M2-2 proteins, raising questions about potential implications for RSV immunoprophylaxis. Dr. El Haddad noted that nirsevimab (Beyfortus, Sanofi/AstraZeneca) and clesrovimab (Enflonsia, Merck) target the F protein, but whether the observed genetic variation influences monoclonal antibody effectiveness remains unknown; however, she considered a clinically meaningful impact unlikely. RSV-B infections were also linked with a more pronounced mucosal inflammatory response, with age-adjusted analyses identifying 22 cytokines at significantly higher concentrations than in RSV-A infections (P<0.01). These cytokines formed two distinct clusters: one involving innate immune activation and interleukin-1 signaling, the other with chemotaxis and cellular stress pathways. Of importance, previous RSV exposure and the associated immune response could potentially affect cytokine profiles and disease severity during subsequent infections, according to Dr. El Haddad. “As the authors didn’t mention it, I am interested to know whether any children had prior RSV infections. If prior infection was not accounted for, it could represent a potential confounder.” Dr. El Haddad reported no relevant financial disclosures. Britannica Editors Last updated Oct. 26, 2026 •History Britannica AI Ask Anything Table of Contents Table of Contents Ask Anything News • Cranking up a natural defense pathway boosts cells' resistance to RSV • Oct. 20, 2026, 12:40 PM ET (Medical Xpress) ...(Show more) 3 viruses are more active in California in the fall. How to identify symptoms • Oct. 9, 2026, 12:00 PM ET (The Sacramento Bee) Show less respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), infectious agent of the respiratory system that typically produces a mild coldlike illness but in susceptible individuals, particularly infants under six months of age, is a major cause of severe and potentially fatal lower respiratory disease. Severe bronchiolitis and pneumonia caused by respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) occurs most commonly in the elderly and in young children. An estimated 60 to 70 percent of children have been infected with RSV by age one, and nearly all children have been infected by age two. Because RSV effectively evades immunity, repeat infections are common. RSV infection is seasonal, occurring as an annual epidemic during the winter in most countries in temperate regions. What's the Difference Between Bacteria and Viruses?Knowing the difference isn't just a technical distinction—it can save lives.(more) See all videos for this article Direct person-to-person contact is thought to be the most common route of RSV transmission. The virus also can be transmitted through the air, such as by coughing or sneezing, and through direct contact with a contaminated surface followed by touching of the face. Symptoms generally appear within four to six days of infection and include runny nose, cough, sore throat, headache, fever, and wheezing. In instances of severe illness, affected individuals may have a high fever, severe wheezing, rapid or laboured breathing, and bluish lips or fingers. Infants may only show signs of irritability, fatigue, and breathing difficulties, even in severe cases. The only pharmacological therapy available for treatment of RSV is the nucleoside analogue ribavirin, which can be administered orally, parenterally (by injection or infusion), or by inhalation. An injectable humanized monoclonal antibody is available for prevention of severe RSV illness in high-risk infants and children. It provides passive immunity and must by given by intramuscular injection once a month during RSV season. Transmission can be prevented with frequent handwashing, cleaning of surfaces, avoiding close contact with others, and covering the mouth and nose with the upper arm when coughing or sneezing.
